/**
 * [NEW] Set task progress for real-time tracking
 * Uses CacheService for fast, lightweight storage across script instances
 */
function setTaskProgress(taskId, percent, message) {
  if (!taskId) return;
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const data = JSON.stringify({
    percent: Math.round(percent),
    message: message || 'Processing...',
    timestamp: new Date().getTime()
  });
  cache.put('task_' + taskId, data, 600); // Store for 10 minutes
}

function standardResponse(success, data = null, message = '', errorCode = null) {
  return { success: success, data: data, message: message, code: errorCode };
}

/**
 * Global helper to verify if a user has administrative privileges.
 * Permissive to both 'Admin' and 'SuperAdmin' roles, and the master admin email.
 */
function isUserAnyAdmin(user) {
  if (!user) return false;
  const role = String(user.role || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const email = String(user.email || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  const masterAdmin = String(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail || '').trim().toLowerCase();
  
  // Updated to include 'president' role for superadmin access
  return role === 'admin' || role === 'superadmin' || role === 'president' || role === 'teacher' || (masterAdmin && email === masterAdmin);
}

/**
 * [NEW] Scans for recent reports in the batch folder to avoid duplicate processing.
 */
function checkForRecentReports(user) {
  try {
    const batchFolderId = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId;
    if (!batchFolderId) return { success: false, reports: [] };
    
    const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(batchFolderId);
    const cutoffDate = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 3600 * 1000);
    const folders = folder.getFolders();
    const reports = [];
    
    while (folders.hasNext()) {
      const f = folders.next();
      const created = f.getDateCreated();
      if (created > cutoffDate) {
        const name = f.getName();
        let type = '';
        if (name.includes('ID_Card_Data')) type = 'ID Card Data';
        else if (name.includes('ID_Cards')) type = 'ID Card PDFs';
        else if (name.includes('Class_Backup')) type = 'Class Backup';
        
        if (type) {
          reports.push({
            name: name,
            type: type,
            url: f.getUrl(),
            timeStr: created.toLocaleString('en-IN'),
            id: f.getId()
          });
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Sort by newest first
    reports.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.timeStr) - new Date(a.timeStr));
    
    return { success: true, reports: reports };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, reports: [], message: e.message };
  }
}


/**
 * Ensure a QR image exists in Drive for given payload. Reuses an existing file when the
 * payload hash matches; if payload changed, older files for the same form are trashed
 * and a new QR image is generated and saved to the folder. Returns a Drive File.
 */
function ensureQrFile(qrPayloadObj, folderId) {
  folderId = folderId || SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.qrFolderId;
  if (!folderId) {
    Logger.log('QR folder id not configured');
    return null;
  }
  var folder;
  try { folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId); } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Unable to access QR folder: ' + (e && e.message));
    // Do not throw — return null so caller can continue and document won't get raw URLs
    return null;
  }

  var formNum = String(qrPayloadObj.formNumber || '');
  var rollNo = String(qrPayloadObj.rollNo || '');
  var name = String(qrPayloadObj.name || '').trim();
  var sanitizedName = name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_').slice(0, 30) || 'Student';

  var qrData = Object.keys(qrPayloadObj).map(function(k){ return k + ':' + (qrPayloadObj[k] || ''); }).join('; ');

  // Short fingerprint for payload
  var digest = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.MD5, qrData);
  var hashHex = digest.map(function(b){ var v = b; if (v < 0) v += 256; return ('0' + v.toString(16)).slice(-2); }).join('');
  hashHex = hashHex.substring(0, 12);

  var filename = 'QR_form' + formNum + '_roll' + rollNo + '_' + sanitizedName + '_' + hashHex + '.png';

  // If exact file already exists, return it
  try {
    var existingExact = folder.getFilesByName(filename);
    if (existingExact && existingExact.hasNext()) return existingExact.next();
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Error while searching for existing QR file: ' + (e && e.message));
  }

  // Trash older QR files for same formNumber (they have different hash)
  try {
    var prefix = 'QR_form' + formNum + '_';
    var it = folder.getFiles();
    var toTrash = [];
    while (it.hasNext()) {
      var f = it.next();
      var n = f.getName();
      if (n.indexOf(prefix) === 0) toTrash.push(f);
    }
    toTrash.forEach(function(f){ try { f.setTrashed(true); } catch(e) { Logger.log('Failed to trash old QR: ' + e.message); } });
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Error while cleaning old QR files: ' + (e && e.message));
  }

  // Generate new QR image via public QR API and save to Drive
  var qrUrl = 'https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=300x300&data=' + encodeURIComponent(qrData);
  // Generate new QR image via public QR API and save to Drive
  var qrUrl = 'https://api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code/?size=300x300&data=' + encodeURIComponent(qrData);
  try {
    var resp = UrlFetchApp.fetch(qrUrl);
    if (resp && resp.getResponseCode && resp.getResponseCode() !== 200) {
      Logger.log('QR API returned non-200: ' + resp.getResponseCode());
      return null;
    }
    var blob = resp.getBlob().setName(filename);
    var file = folder.createFile(blob);
    try { file.setDescription('qr-hash:' + hashHex); makePublic(file); } catch (e) { Logger.log('QR save: set metadata failed: ' + (e && e.message)); }
    try {
      // Log success to QR_Log
      logQrEvent(formNum, 'created', file.getUrl ? file.getUrl() : file.getId());
    } catch (ee) { Logger.log('Failed to log QR creation: ' + (ee && ee.message)); }
    return file;
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('QR creation failed: ' + (e && e.message));
    try { logQrEvent(formNum, 'error', 'QR creation failed: ' + (e && e.message)); } catch (ee) {}
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Log QR-related events to QR_Log sheet (non-blocking)
 */
function logQrEvent(formNum, status, message) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.QR_LOG);
    if (!sheet) {
      sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.QR_LOG);
      sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 4).setValues([['Timestamp', 'FormNumber', 'Status', 'Message']])
        .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
      sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    }
    sheet.appendRow([new Date(), formNum || '', status || '', String(message || '')]);
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Failed to log QR event: ' + (e && e.message));
  }
}

function initialSetup() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
    // Create Users sheet
    let userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    if (!userSheet) {
      userSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.USERS);
    } else {
      userSheet.clear();
    }
    // Added PasswordPlain column so admin can retrieve plain password when necessary
    userSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 8).setValues([['Email', 'Name', 'PasswordHash', 'PasswordPlain', 'Role', 'Mobile', 'Residence', 'UpdatedOn']])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    userSheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    
    // Auto-resize columns for readability
    userSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, 8);
    // Create admin user
    const passwordHash = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, DEFAULT_ADMIN.password)
      .map(byte => ('0' + (byte & 0xFF).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
  userSheet.appendRow([DEFAULT_ADMIN.email, DEFAULT_ADMIN.name, passwordHash, DEFAULT_ADMIN.password, DEFAULT_ADMIN.role, '', '', new Date()]);
    // Create Config sheet
    let configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (!configSheet) {
      configSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    } else {
      configSheet.clear();
    }
    configSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([['Key', 'Value']])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    configSheet.getRange(2, 1, 7, 2).setValues([
      ['allow_9th', 'TRUE'],

    subjectsSheet.setFrozenRows(1);

    subjectsSheet.getRange(2, 1, 7, 11).setValues([

      ['12th', 'Science', 'General English,Physics,Chemistry', 'Biology,Mathematics', 'Environmental Science,Physical Education,IT and ITES,Healthcare', 5, 5, 1, 2, 1, 1],

    function getOrCreateFolder(parent, folderName) {

    try {
      SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(errorMessage);
    } catch (alertError) {
      Logger.log(errorMessage);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Alternative contact saving function that creates a CSV file for manual import into Google Contacts
 * This works as a fallback when People API is not available
 */
function saveContactsToCSV(formNumbersInput, user, taskId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized: This feature is restricted to admin only." };
    }
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (!admSheet) throw new Error("Admission sheet not found");

    // Get Session from Config
    let session = '2025-26'; // Default
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (configSheet) {
      const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let r = 1; r < configData.length; r++) {
        if (configData[r][0] === 'session') {
          session = configData[r][1];
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    const data = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    
    // Helper to get index safely
    const getIdx = (name) => headers.indexOf(name);

    // Indices
    const idxForm = getIdx('Form Number');
    const idxName = getIdx("Student's Name (as per school records)");
    const idxMobile = getIdx('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
    let idxEmail = getIdx('Email Address (Gmail Preferred - for updates)');
    if (idxEmail === -1) idxEmail = getIdx('Email Address');

    const idxClass = getIdx('Class to which admission is sought') !== -1 ? getIdx('Class to which admission is sought') : getIdx('Admission sought for class');
    let idxFather = getIdx("Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)");
    if (idxFather === -1) idxFather = getIdx("Father's Name"); // Fallback
    let idxMother = getIdx("Mother's Name (as per school records)");
    if (idxMother === -1) idxMother = getIdx("Mother's Name"); // Fallback
    
    let idxRoll = getIdx('Class Roll No');
    if (idxRoll === -1) idxRoll = getIdx('Roll No');
    if (idxRoll === -1) idxRoll = getIdx('Roll Number');

    const idxGender = getIdx('Gender');
    
    // Stream & Subjects Indices
    let idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream opted in Class 11th');
    if (idxStream11 === -1) idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream for Class 11th');
    if (idxStream11 === -1) idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream');
    if (idxStream11 === -1) idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream/Faculty');

    let idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 11th');
    if (idxSubjects11 === -1) idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects Studied in Class 11th');
    if (idxSubjects11 === -1) idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects');
    if (idxSubjects11 === -1) idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects Offered');

    // For 12th students - need what they studied in 11th
    let idxSubjects11For12th = getIdx('Subjects Studied in Class 11th');
    if (idxSubjects11For12th === -1) idxSubjects11For12th = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 11th');
    if (idxSubjects11For12th === -1) idxSubjects11For12th = getIdx('Subjects');

    let idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream & Subjects for Class 12th');
    if (idxStreamSubjects12 === -1) idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream & Subjects');
    if (idxStreamSubjects12 === -1) idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream and Subjects');

    let idxSubjects9 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 9th');
    if (idxSubjects9 === -1) idxSubjects9 = getIdx('Subjects');

    let idxSubjects10 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 10th');
    if (idxSubjects10 === -1) idxSubjects10 = getIdx('Subjects');

    // Registration Indices
    const idxRegDiet = getIdx('DIET Registration No.');
    const idxReg9 = getIdx('Board Registration No. (Class 9th)');
    const idxReg10 = getIdx('Board Registration No. (Class 10th)');
    const idxReg11 = getIdx('Board Registration No. (Class 11th)');

    // Additional Details
    const idxDob = getIdx('DoB (as per school records)');
    const idxVillage = getIdx('Name of your village');
    let idxParentMobile = getIdx("Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)");
    if (idxParentMobile === -1) idxParentMobile = getIdx("Parent's Mobile No.");

    if (idxForm === -1 || idxName === -1 || idxMobile === -1) {
      throw new Error("Required columns (Form Number, Name, Mobile) not found.");
    }

    // Parse input
    const requestedForms = formNumbersInput.split(',')
      .map(s => s.trim())
      .filter(s => s !== '');

    if (requestedForms.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, message: "No valid form numbers provided." };
    }

    let savedCount = 0;
    let errors = [];
    let skipped = [];

    // Map form numbers to row index
    const formMap = new Map();
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const fNo = String(data[i][idxForm]).trim();
      if (fNo) formMap.set(fNo, i);
    }

    // Subject Abbreviation Helper
    const abbreviateSubjects = (subjStr) => {
      if (!subjStr) return '';
      const map = {
        // Academic Subjects
        'General English': 'GE', 'English Literature': 'EL', 'Functional English': 'FE',
        'Hindi': 'HI', 'Dogri': 'DG', 'Sanskrit': 'SA', 'Punjabi': 'PU', 'Bhoti': 'BO',
        'Arabic': 'AR', 'Persian': 'PE', 'Kashmiri': 'KA', 'Urdu': 'UR',
        'History': 'HT', 'Economics': 'EC', 'Geography': 'GG', 'Philosophy': 'PL',
        'Education': 'ED', 'Psychology': 'PY', 'Sociology': 'SO', 'Political Science': 'PS',
        'Home Science': 'HS', 'Home Science (Elective)': 'HS', 'Statistics': 'SS',
        'Mathematics': 'MA', 'Maths': 'MA',
        'Islamic Studies': 'IS', 'Vedic Studies': 'VS',
        'Computer Science': 'CS', 'Information Practices': 'IP', 'Environmental Science': 'ES',
        'Physics': 'PH', 'Chemistry': 'CH', 'Biology': 'BI', 'Botany': 'BI', 'Zoology': 'BI',
        'Electronics': 'ET', 'Biotechnology': 'BT', 'Bio-chemistry': 'BC', 'Music': 'MU',
        'Family Health Care & Prevention': 'FH', 'Food Science': 'FS', 'Management of Resources': 'MR',
        'Business Studies': 'BS', 'Travel, Tourism & Hotel Management': 'TT',
        'Accountancy': 'AY', 'Entrepreneurship': 'EP', 'Public Administration': 'PA',
        'Typewriting and Shorthand': 'TS', 'Business Mathematics': 'BM', 'Geology': 'GO',
        'Buddhist Studies': 'BU', 'Physical Education': 'PD', 'Clothing for the Family': 'CT',
        'Applied Mathematics': 'AM', 'Microbiology': 'MB', 'Extension Education': 'EE', 'Human Development': 'HD',
        // Vocational Subjects
        'IT and ITeS': 'ITE', 'IT and ITES': 'ITE', 'Retail': 'RET', 'Healthcare': 'HTC',
        'Tourism': 'TOU', 'Security': 'SEC', 'Agriculture': 'AGR', 'Telecommunication': 'TLC',
        'Media and Entertainment': 'MDE', 'Beauty and Wellness': 'BTW', 'Physical Education & Sports': 'PES'
      };
      return subjStr.split(',').map(s => {
        const trimmed = s.trim();
        // Check for exact match first, then case-insensitive if needed, or return original
        return map[trimmed] || trimmed; 
      }).join(',');
    };

    // CSV Header (Google Contacts format with extended fields)
    let csvContent = 'Name,Given Name,Additional Name,Family Name,Yomi Name,Given Name Yomi,Additional Name Yomi,Family Name Yomi,Name Prefix,Name Suffix,Initials,Nickname,Short Name,Maiden Name,Birthday,Gender,Location,Billing Information,Directory Server,Mileage,Occupation,Hobby,Sensitivity,Priority,Subject,Notes,Language,Photo,Group Membership,Phone 1 - Type,Phone 1 - Value,Phone 2 - Type,Phone 2 - Value,Email 1 - Type,Email 1 - Value,Organization 1 - Name,Organization 1 - Title,Organization 1 - Department\n';

    if (taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 5, `Preparing to export ${requestedForms.length} contacts...`);

    requestedForms.forEach((formNum, i) => {
      if (taskId && i % 10 === 0) {
          const pct = 5 + Math.round((i / requestedForms.length) * 90);
          setTaskProgress(taskId, pct, `Processing contact ${i + 1} of ${requestedForms.length}...`);
      }
      const rowIndex = formMap.get(formNum);
      if (rowIndex !== undefined) {
        try {
          const row = data[rowIndex];
          const contactName = String(row[idxName]).trim();
          const mobile = String(row[idxMobile]).trim();
          const email = idxEmail !== -1 ? String(row[idxEmail]).trim() : '';
          const studentClass = idxClass !== -1 ? String(row[idxClass]).trim() : '';
          const fatherName = idxFather !== -1 ? String(row[idxFather]).trim() : '';
          const motherName = idxMother !== -1 ? String(row[idxMother]).trim() : '';
          
          const rollNo = idxRoll !== -1 ? String(row[idxRoll]).trim() : '';
          const gender = idxGender !== -1 ? String(row[idxGender]).trim() : '';
          const parentMobile = idxParentMobile !== -1 ? String(row[idxParentMobile]).trim() : '';
          
          let stream = '';
          let subjects = '';
          let regNo = '';

          // Logic to get Stream, Subjects, RegNo based on Class
          if (studentClass.includes('11')) {
            subjects = idxSubjects11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects11]) : '';
            regNo = idxReg10 !== -1 ? String(row[idxReg10]) : '';
            
            // Auto-detect stream based on subjects: If Physics AND Chemistry both present = Science, else Humanities
            if (subjects) {
              const subjectsUpper = subjects.toUpperCase();
              const hasPhysics = subjectsUpper.includes('PHYSICS') || subjectsUpper.includes(' PH ') || subjectsUpper.includes('(PH)');
              const hasChemistry = subjectsUpper.includes('CHEMISTRY') || subjectsUpper.includes(' CH ') || subjectsUpper.includes('(CH)');
              if (hasPhysics && hasChemistry) {
                stream = 'Science';
              } else {
                stream = 'Humanities';
              }
            } else {
              // Fallback to stream column if subjects not available
              stream = idxStream11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxStream11]) : '';
            }
          } else if (studentClass.includes('12')) {
             // For 12th, check if Stream & Subjects column has actual data or just "Same as in class 11th"
             const ss12 = idxStreamSubjects12 !== -1 ? String(row[idxStreamSubjects12]) : '';
             
             // Get 11th subjects for stream detection
             const subjects11For12th = idxSubjects11For12th !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects11For12th]) : '';
             
             // Auto-detect stream based on 11th subjects: If Physics AND Chemistry both present = Science, else Humanities
             if (subjects11For12th) {
               const subjectsUpper = subjects11For12th.toUpperCase();
               const hasPhysics = subjectsUpper.includes('PHYSICS') || subjectsUpper.includes(' PH ') || subjectsUpper.includes('(PH)');
               const hasChemistry = subjectsUpper.includes('CHEMISTRY') || subjectsUpper.includes(' CH ') || subjectsUpper.includes('(CH)');
               if (hasPhysics && hasChemistry) {
                 stream = 'Science';
               } else {
                 stream = 'Humanities';
               }
             } else {
               // Fallback: check stream column or combined column
               if (!ss12 || ss12.toLowerCase().includes('same as') || ss12.toLowerCase().includes('11th')) {
                 stream = idxStream11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxStream11]) : '';
               } else if (ss12.includes(' - ')) {
                 const parts = ss12.split(' - ');
                 stream = parts[0].trim();
               } else {
                 stream = ss12;
               }
             }
             
             // Get subjects for 12th
             if (!ss12 || ss12.toLowerCase().includes('same as') || ss12.toLowerCase().includes('11th')) {
               subjects = subjects11For12th;
             } else if (ss12.includes(' - ')) {
               const parts = ss12.split(' - ');
               subjects = parts.slice(1).join(' - ').trim();
             }
             regNo = idxReg11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxReg11]) : '';
          } else if (studentClass.includes('9')) {
             subjects = idxSubjects9 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects9]) : '';
             regNo = idxRegDiet !== -1 ? String(row[idxRegDiet]) : '';
             stream = 'General'; // 9th class stream is always General
          } else if (studentClass.includes('10')) {
             subjects = idxSubjects10 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects10]) : '';
             regNo = idxReg9 !== -1 ? String(row[idxReg9]) : '';
             stream = 'General'; // 10th class stream is always General
          }

          if (!contactName || !mobile) {
            errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Missing name or mobile number`);
            return;
          }

          const cleanMobile = mobile.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
          if (cleanMobile.length < 10) {
            errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Invalid mobile number format '${mobile}'`);
            return;
          }

          const cleanParentMobile = parentMobile.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');

          // Construct Name
          // Format: roll no. name-father,student (session)GenderCode,stream_subjects
          // GenderCode: M for Male, F for Female
          let namePart = rollNo ? `${rollNo}. ${contactName}` : `${contactName}`;
          if (fatherName) namePart += `-${fatherName}`;
          
          let suffix = `,student (${studentClass}_${session})`;
          // Add gender code: M for Male, F for Female
          if (gender) {
            const genderLower = gender.toLowerCase().trim();
            if (genderLower.startsWith('f')) {
              suffix += 'F';
            } else if (genderLower.startsWith('m')) {
              suffix += 'M';
            }
          }
          
          let details = '';
          // Ensure stream comes immediately after comma if present
          if (stream) {
             details += `,${stream}`;
             if (subjects) {
               const abbrSubjects = abbreviateSubjects(subjects);
               details += `_${abbrSubjects}`;
             }
          } else if (subjects) {
             // If only subjects (e.g. 9th/10th), add them
             const abbrSubjects = abbreviateSubjects(subjects);
             details += `,${abbrSubjects}`;
          }
          
          const displayName = `${namePart}${suffix}${details}`;
          
          // Notes
          let notes = `HSS Shangus Student\nForm Number: ${formNum}`;
          if (regNo) notes += `\nReg No: ${regNo}`;
          if (studentClass) notes += `\nClass: ${studentClass}`;
          if (rollNo) notes += `\nRoll No: ${rollNo}`;
          if (fatherName) notes += `\nFather: ${fatherName}`;
          if (motherName) notes += `\nMother: ${motherName}`;
          if (stream) notes += `\nStream: ${stream}`;
          if (subjects) notes += `\nSubjects: ${subjects}`;
          if (idxDob !== -1) notes += `\nDOB: ${row[idxDob]}`;
          if (idxVillage !== -1) notes += `\nVillage: ${row[idxVillage]}`;
          
          notes += `\nAdded on: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`;
          notes += `\nAdded via: Bulk Contact Saver`;

          // Organization Field: form number_reg no
          let orgName = `${formNum}`;
          if (regNo) orgName += `_${regNo}`;
          // Keep empty if no data? But form number is always there. 
          // User said: "keep epmty where no data is available" -> likely refers to regNo or if entire org field is optional.
          // But "under organisation keep form number_reg no" implies this structure.
          // If Reg No is missing, it will be just "FormNumber".
          // If Form Number is missing (impossible as it's the key), well...

          // CSV Row
          const csvRow = [
            `"${displayName}"`, // Name
            `"${displayName}"`, // Given Name - using full formatted name
            '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', 
            `"${gender}"`, // Gender
            '', '', '', '', 
            `"${studentClass} Student"`, // Occupation
            '', '', '', '', 
            `"${notes}"`, // Notes
            '', '', '* My Contacts', // Group Membership
            'Mobile', `"${cleanMobile}"`, // Phone 1
            cleanParentMobile ? 'Home' : '', cleanParentMobile ? `"${cleanParentMobile}"` : '', // Phone 2
            email && email.includes('@') ? 'Work' : '', email && email.includes('@') ? `"${email}"` : '', // Email 1
            `"${orgName}"`, // Organization 1 - Name
            '', // Organization 1 - Title
            stream ? `"${stream}"` : '' // Organization 1 - Department
          ].join(',');

          csvContent += csvRow + '\n';
          savedCount++;

        } catch (err) {
          errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: ${err.message}`);
        }
      } else {
        errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Not found in admissions data`);
      }
    });

    // Create and save the CSV file to batch folder
    const csvBlob = Utilities.newBlob(csvContent, 'text/csv', `HSS_Students_Contacts_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`);
    const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId);
    const csvFile = folder.createFile(csvBlob);
    
    // Make file publicly readable
    makePublic(csvFile);

    if (taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 100, `Successfully exported ${savedCount} contacts.`);
    return {
      success: true,
      savedCount: savedCount,
      message: `Successfully created CSV with ${savedCount} contacts. File: ${csvFile.getName()}`,
      errors: errors,
      skipped: skipped,
      csvFileId: csvFile.getId(),
      csvFileUrl: csvFile.getUrl(),
      csvDownloadUrl: `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=${csvFile.getId()}`
    };

  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveContactsToCSV');
    return { 
      success: false, 
      message: `CSV creation failed: ${e.message}`,
      savedCount: 0,
      errors: [e.message],
      skipped: []
    };
  }
}
/**
 * Creates or updates the Form_Structure sheet based on the provided PDF.
 * [NEW] Added 'Declaration' section and field.
 */
function createADMformStructure() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    // Get or create adm_form sheet
    let admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (!admSheet) {
      admSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    }
    // Define sections as per user specification (with unique field names) - Updated order for 12th
    const sections = {
      'personal': {
        title: '1. Personal Details',
        fields: ["Student's Name (as per school records)", "DoB (as per school records)", 'Gender', "Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)", "Mother's Name (as per school records)", "Father's/Guardian's Occupation"],
        gridClass: 'grid-3',
        visible: true
      },
      'contact': {
        title: '2. Contact & Address Details',
        fields: ['Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)', "Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)", 'Aadhar No.', 'House No.', 'Name of your village', 'Block', 'Tehsil', 'District', 'State/UT', 'PIN code', 'E-mail ID'],
        gridClass: 'grid-3',
        visible: true
      },
      'physical': {
        title: '3. Physical & Socio-Economic Details',
        fields: ['Height (cm)', 'Weight (kg)', 'Blood Group', 'Your Mother Tongue', 'Religion', 'Social category', 'Socio-economic category', 'Whether Any Disability', 'Type of Disability'],
        gridClass: 'grid-3',
        visible: true
      },
      'admission': {
        title: '4. Admission & Scholarship Details',
        fields: ['Admission sought for class', 'Whether scholarship received in previous academic year', 'Type of scholarship received', 'Amount received (INR)', 'Bank Account No.', 'Name of Bank', 'IFSC code'],
        gridClass: 'grid-3',
        visible: true
      },
      'academic9th': {
        title: '5a. Academic Details',
        fields: ['DIET Registration No.', 'Year of Passing Class 8th', 'Name of Previous School (Class 8th)', 'Board (Class 8th)', 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 8th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 8th', 'Subjects Studied in Class 8th', 'Subjects to be taken in Class 9th', 'Name of Previous Complex Head'],
        gridClass: 'grid-2',
        visible: false
      },
      'academic10th': {
        title: '5b. Academic Details',
        fields: ['Board Registration No. (Class 9th)', 'Year of Passing Class 9th', 'Name of Previous School (Class 9th)', 'Board (Class 9th)', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 9th', 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 9th','Subjects Studied in Class 9th','Subjects to be taken in Class 10th'],
        gridClass: 'grid-2',
        visible: false
      },
      'academic11th': {
        title: '5c. Academic Details (Class 11th)',
        fields: ['Admission Type (Class 11th)', 'Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)', 'Board Registration No. (Class 10th)', 'Exam Roll Number of Class 10th', 'Year of Passing Class 10th', 'Year of Appearing (Class 10th)', 'Board (Class 10th)', 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 10th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 10th', 'Subjects Studied in Class 10th', 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 10th)', 'Stream for Class 11th', 'Subjects to be taken in Class 11th', 'Name of Previous School (Class 10th)'],
        gridClass: 'grid-2',
        visible: false
      },
      'academic12th': {
        title: '5d. Academic Details (Class 12th)',
        fields: ['Admission Type (Class 12th)', 'Reason for Provisional (Class 12th)', 'Board Registration No. (Class 11th)', 'Exam Roll Number of Class 11th', 'Year of Passing Class 11th', 'Year of Appearing (Class 11th)', 'Board (Class 11th)', 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 11th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 11th', 'Stream opted in Class 11th', 'Subjects Studied in Class 11th', 'Stream & Subjects for Class 12th', 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 11th)', 'Name of Previous School (Class 11th)'],
        gridClass: 'grid-2',
        visible: false
      },
      'vocational': {
        title: '6. Vocational Details',
        fields: ['Vocational subject in previous class', 'Percentage Obtained in Vocational Subject'],
        gridClass: 'grid-2',
        visible: true
      },
      'additional': {
        title: '7. Additional Information',
        fields: ['Passport No. (if available)', 'Identification Mark (if any)', 'Previous participation in sports (if any)', 'Games to participate', 'PEN Number (given by UDISE portal)', 'APAAR ID'],
        gridClass: 'grid-3',
        visible: true
      },
      'declaration': { // [NEW] Declaration Section
        title: '8. Declaration',
        fields: ['Declaration'],
        gridClass: 'grid-1', 
        visible: true
      },
      'uploads': {
        title: '9. Uploads & Remarks', // [UPDATED] Title number
        fields: ['Student Photo', 'Remarks/Feedback (if any)'],
        gridClass: 'grid-end', // Special 3-col grid
        visible: true
      }
    };
    // Build allFields from meta + sections (unique)
    let allFields = [
      // Meta
      'Timestamp', 'Email Address', 'Form Number', 'Session', 'Class Roll No', 'Status', 'Last Edited', 'PDF URL'
    ];
    // Add all section fields, preserving order, skipping duplicates
    Object.values(sections).forEach(section => {
      section.fields.forEach(field => {
        if (!allFields.includes(field)) {
          allFields.push(field);
        }
      });
    });
    // Update adm_form headers if needed
    const currentHeaders = admSheet.getLastColumn() > 0 ?
      admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, admSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0] : [];
    // Check if headers are different from the new allFields array
    let headersMatch = currentHeaders.length === allFields.length;
    if (headersMatch) {
      for (let i = 0; i < allFields.length; i++) {
        if (currentHeaders[i] !== allFields[i]) {
          headersMatch = false;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!headersMatch) {
      admSheet.clear();
      admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, allFields.length).setValues([allFields])
        .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
      admSheet.setFrozenRows(1);
      admSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, allFields.length);
    }
    // Create/recreate Form_Structure sheet
    let structSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.FORM_STRUCTURE);
    if (structSheet) {
      ss.deleteSheet(structSheet);
    }
    structSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.FORM_STRUCTURE);
    const structHeaders = ['Field Name', 'Field Type', 'Options / Range / Length', 'Is Required?', 'Help Text', 'Placeholder', 'Classes'];
    structSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, structHeaders.length).setValues([structHeaders])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    structSheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    // Get field configurations (updated for PDF)
    const fieldConfigs = getFieldConfigurations();
    // Build structure data
    const structData = allFields.map(fieldName => {
      const config = fieldConfigs[fieldName];
      if (!config) {
        // Log a warning if a field in allFields is missing a configuration
        Logger.log(`Warning: No configuration found for field: "${fieldName}". Using default.`);
        return [
          fieldName,
          'text', // default type
          '100', // default length
          'FALSE',
          'Missing config',
          generatePlaceholder(fieldName), // Use helper for missing
          '9th,10th,11th,12th' // default class
        ];
      }
      return [
        fieldName,
        config.type,
        config.options || '',
        config.required ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE',
        config.help || '',
        config.placeholder || '',
        config.classes || ''
      ];
    });
    structSheet.getRange(2, 1, structData.length, structHeaders.length).setValues(structData);
    structSheet.autoResizeColumns(1, structHeaders.length);
    const message = '[DONE] Form Structure Created!\n\n' +
      'The Form_Structure sheet has been created with ' + allFields.length + ' fields, based on the PDF and new logical order.\n' +
      'The adm_form sheet headers have been updated.\n\n' +
      'You can now use the web app.';
    try {
      SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(message);
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log(message);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'createADMformStructure');
    const errorMessage = 'Failed to create form structure: ' + e.message;
    try {
      SpreadsheetApp.getUi().alert(errorMessage);
    } catch (alertError) {
      Logger.log(errorMessage);
    }
  }
}
function generatePlaceholder(fieldName) {
  let clean = fieldName.split('.')[1]?.trim() || fieldName.split('(')[0].trim();
  clean = clean.replace(/[^\w]/g, '');
  clean = clean.replace(/^[^A-Z]+/, ''); // Remove leading numbers/letters if any
  return '{{' + clean + '}}';
}
/**
 * Contains the complete form structure.
 * [NEW] Added 'Declaration' field configuration.
 */
function getFieldConfigurations() {
  return {
    // --- Meta Fields (from PDF Source 1) ---
    'Timestamp': { type: 'autogen', options: '', required: false, help: 'Auto-generated on submission', placeholder: '{{Timestamp}}', classes: '' },
    'Email Address': { type: 'autogen_email', options: '', required: true, help: "User's email address", placeholder: '{{Email}}', classes: '' },
    'Form Number': { type: 'autogen_number', options: '', required: false, help: 'Auto-generated unique number', placeholder: '{{fno}}', classes: '' },
    'Session': { type: 'list', options: '2025-26,2025 APR/BIAN,2024-25 (revised),2024-25,2023-24,2022-23,2021-22,2020-21,2019-20,2018-19,2017-18,2016-17,2015-16,2014-15,2013-14,2012-13,2011-12,2010-11,2009-10,2008-09,2007-08,2006-07', required: true, help: 'Academic Session', placeholder: '{{Session}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Class Roll No': { type: 'number', options: '1-999', required: false, help: 'Assigned by admin post-admissions', placeholder: '{{ClassRollNo}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Status': { type: 'autogen', options: '', required: false, help: 'Application status (Draft/Submitted)', placeholder: '{{Status}}', classes: '' },
    'Last Edited': { type: 'autogen', options: '', required: false, help: 'Last edited timestamp for drafts', placeholder: '{{LastEdited}}', classes: '' },
    'Student Photo': { type: 'image', options: 'Upload Passport-size photo (max 200 KB)', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{PHOTO}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'PDF URL': { type: 'autogen', options: '', required: false, help: 'Auto-generated PDF download link', placeholder: '{{PDFURL}}', classes: '' },
    // --- Personal/Contact (from PDF Source 1) ---
    "Student's Name (as per school records)": { type: 'text', options: '100', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{StudentName}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    "DoB (as per school records)": { type: 'date', options: '', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{DoB}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Gender': { type: 'list', options: 'Male,Female,Other', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{Gender}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    "Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)": { type: 'text', options: '100', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{FatherName}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    "Mother's Name (as per school records)": { type: 'text', options: '100', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{MotherName}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    "Father's/Guardian's Occupation": { type: 'list', options: 'Businessman/Businesswoman,Govt. Employee,Private Employee,Farmer,Shopkeeper,Ex-serviceman,Other', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{FatherOccupation}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)': { type: 'text_numeric', options: '10', required: true, help: '10-digit mobile number', placeholder: '{{MobileNo}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    "Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)": { type: 'text_numeric', options: '10', required: true, help: '10-digit mobile number', placeholder: '{{ParentMobile}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Aadhar No.': { type: 'text_numeric', options: '12', required: true, help: '12-digit Aadhar number', placeholder: '{{AadharNo}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'House No.': { type: 'text', options: '50', required: false, help: '', placeholder: '{{HouseNo}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Name of your village': { type: 'text', options: '50', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{Village}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'Block': { type: 'text', options: '50', required: false, help: '', placeholder: '{{Block}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    // --- Contact/Physical/Socio (from PDF Source 2) ---
    'Tehsil': { type: 'text', options: '50', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{Tehsil}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },
    'District': { type: 'list', options: 'Anantnag,Baramulla,Budgam,Ganderbal,Kishtwar,Kulgam,Kupwara,Pulwama,Shopian,Srinagar,Other', required: true, help: '', placeholder: '{{District}}', classes: '9th,10th,11th,12th' },

function getSubjectsConfig() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SUBJECTS_CONFIG);
    if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) return {};
    // Ensure schema includes 'Min Total' before 'Max Total'
    (function ensureSubjectsConfigSchema() {
      const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      const idxByName = (name) => headers.indexOf(name);
      const hasMin = idxByName('Min Total') !== -1;
      const hasMax = idxByName('Max Total') !== -1;
      if (!hasMin && hasMax) {
        const insertCol = idxByName('Group2 Options') + 2; // after Group2 Options
        sheet.insertColumnBefore(insertCol);
        sheet.getRange(1, insertCol, 1, 1).setValue('Min Total');
        const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
        if (lastRow > 1) {
          const classes = sheet.getRange(2, idxByName('Class') + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
          const values = classes.map(cls => {
            const c = String(cls).trim();
            // Keep Min Total = 5 for 8th, 9th, 10th
            return 5;
          }).map(v => [v]);
          sheet.getRange(2, insertCol, lastRow - 1, 1).setValues(values);
        }
      }
    })();
    const range = sheet.getDataRange();
    const data = range.getValues();
    const headers = data.shift();
    const idx = (name) => headers.indexOf(name);
    const IDX = {
      cls: idx('Class'),
      stream: idx('Stream'),
      compulsory: idx('Compulsory Subjects'),
      group1: idx('Group1 Options'),
      group2: idx('Group2 Options'),
      minTotal: idx('Min Total'),
      maxTotal: idx('Max Total'),
      g1Min: idx('G1 Min'),
      g1Max: idx('G1 Max'),
      g2Min: idx('G2 Min'),
      g2Max: idx('G2 Max')
    };
    return data.reduce((acc, row) => {
      const cls = row[IDX.cls];
      const stream = row[IDX.stream];
      if (!cls || !stream) return acc;
      if (!acc[cls]) acc[cls] = {};
      acc[cls][stream] = {
        compulsory: row[IDX.compulsory] ? String(row[IDX.compulsory]).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [],
        group1: row[IDX.group1] ? String(row[IDX.group1]).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [],
        group2: row[IDX.group2] ? String(row[IDX.group2]).split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [],
        // default missing Min Total to 5
        minTotal: parseInt(row[IDX.minTotal]) || 5,
        maxTotal: parseInt(row[IDX.maxTotal]) || 6,
        g1Min: parseInt(row[IDX.g1Min]) || 0,
        g1Max: parseInt(row[IDX.g1Max]) || 1,
        g2Min: parseInt(row[IDX.g2Min]) || 0,
        g2Max: parseInt(row[IDX.g2Max]) || 1
      };
      return acc;
    }, {});
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getSubjectsConfig');
    return {};
  }
}

// =================================================================
// WEB APP ENTRY
// =================================================================
function doGet(e) {
  try {
    const t = HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('Index');
    t.redirectTo = e.parameter.redirectTo || '';
    return t.evaluate()
      .setTitle('HSS Shangus Admission Portal')
      .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL)
      .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1');
  } catch (err) {
    logError(err, 'doGet');
    return HtmlService.createHtmlOutput('<h1>Error loading application</h1><p>' + err.message + '</p>');
  }
}

function include(filename) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile(filename).getContent();
}
// =================================================================
// API FUNCTIONS (AUTH) - [NEW] Password Reset Functions
// =================================================================
function checkEmailRegistered(email, role = 'student') {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    const requestedRole = String(role || 'student').toLowerCase();
    
    // Check if THIS email already has THIS role
    const exists = userData.some(row => 
      String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase() && 
      String(row[4]).toLowerCase() === requestedRole
    );
    
    return { exists: exists };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'checkEmailRegistered');
    return { exists: false, error: e.message };
  }
}

function checkMobileRegistered(mobile, email = null) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    
    // Check 1: Is this email whitelisted for this mobile?
    if (email) {
      const whitelistSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
      if (whitelistSheet && whitelistSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const whitelistData = whitelistSheet.getRange(2, 1, whitelistSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
        const isWhitelisted = whitelistData.some(row => 
          String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === String(email).toLowerCase() && 
          String(row[1]).trim() === String(mobile).trim()
        );
        
        if (isWhitelisted) {
          return { 
            exists: false, 
            whitelisted: true,
            location: 'Email-Mobile pair is whitelisted by admin',
            message: 'Duplicate mobile allowed for this account'
          };
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Check 2: Users sheet Column F (Profile mobile)
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    // Allow same mobile if it's the SAME email (same user registering for another role)
    const existsInUsers = userData.some(row => 
      String(row[5]).trim() === mobile.trim() && 
      String(row[0]).toLowerCase() !== (email ? email.toLowerCase() : '')
    );
    
    // Check 3: adm_form sheet - "Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)" column
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (admSheet) {
      const headers = admSheet.getLastColumn() > 0 ? 
        admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, admSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0] : [];
      const mobileColIndex = headers.indexOf('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
      
      if (mobileColIndex >= 0) {
        const mobileColumn = admSheet.getRange(2, mobileColIndex + 1, admSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        const existsInAdmForm = mobileColumn.some(m => String(m).trim() === mobile.trim());
        
        if (existsInAdmForm) {
          return { 
            exists: true, 
            location: 'Found in admission form (Student mobile)',
            sheet: SHEETS.ADMISSIONS 
          };
        }
      }
      
      // Check 4: adm_form sheet - "Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)" column
      const parentMobileColIndex = headers.indexOf("Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)");
      
      if (parentMobileColIndex >= 0) {
        const parentMobileColumn = admSheet.getRange(2, parentMobileColIndex + 1, admSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        const existsAsParent = parentMobileColumn.some(m => String(m).trim() === mobile.trim());
        
        if (existsAsParent) {
          return { 
            exists: true, 
            location: 'Found in admission form (Parent mobile)',
            sheet: SHEETS.ADMISSIONS 
          };
        }
      }
    }
    
    if (existsInUsers) {
      return { 
        exists: true, 
        location: 'Found in user profile (registration)',
        sheet: SHEETS.USERS 
      };
    }
    
    return { exists: false, location: 'Mobile number is available' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'checkMobileRegistered');
    return { exists: false, error: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Check if a mobile number exists across all sheets (Users, Student mobile, Parent mobile in adm_form)
 * Returns detailed information about where it's found
 */
function checkMobileNumberUniqueness(mobile) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const results = {
      isUnique: true,
      locations: [],
      message: 'Mobile number is unique'
    };
    
    // Check Users sheet (Column F - Profile mobile)
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userMobileData = userSheet.getRange('F:F').getValues().flat();
    const userMatches = userMobileData.filter(m => String(m).trim() === mobile.trim() && String(m).trim() !== '');
    if (userMatches.length > 0) {
      results.isUnique = false;
      results.locations.push({
        sheet: SHEETS.USERS,
        column: 'Mobile (Column F)',
        type: 'Profile Registration',
        count: userMatches.length
      });
    }
    
    // Check adm_form sheet
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (admSheet && admSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const headers = admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, admSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      
      // Check Student mobile column
      const studentMobileColIndex = headers.indexOf('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
      if (studentMobileColIndex >= 0) {
        const studentMobileData = admSheet.getRange(2, studentMobileColIndex + 1, admSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        const studentMatches = studentMobileData.filter(m => String(m).trim() === mobile.trim() && String(m).trim() !== '');
        if (studentMatches.length > 0) {
          results.isUnique = false;
          results.locations.push({
            sheet: SHEETS.ADMISSIONS,
            column: 'Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)',
            type: 'Student Mobile in Form',
            count: studentMatches.length
          });
        }
      }
      
      // Check Parent mobile column
      const parentMobileColIndex = headers.indexOf("Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)");
      if (parentMobileColIndex >= 0) {
        const parentMobileData = admSheet.getRange(2, parentMobileColIndex + 1, admSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        const parentMatches = parentMobileData.filter(m => String(m).trim() === mobile.trim() && String(m).trim() !== '');
        if (parentMatches.length > 0) {
          results.isUnique = false;
          results.locations.push({
            sheet: SHEETS.ADMISSIONS,
            column: "Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)",
            type: 'Parent Mobile in Form',
            count: parentMatches.length
          });
        }
      }
    }
    
    if (!results.isUnique) {
      results.message = `Mobile number found in ${results.locations.length} location(s)`;
    }
    
    return results;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'checkMobileNumberUniqueness');
    return { 
      isUnique: false, 
      error: e.message,
      message: 'Error checking mobile uniqueness'
    };
  }
}
function sendRegistrationOTP(email, name, mobile) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    // 1. Check if email is already registered
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const emailColumn = userSheet.getRange('A:A').getValues().flat();
    if (emailColumn.some(e => String(e).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase())) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Email already registered. Please log in.' };
    }
    // 2. Check for dummy email
    const dummyDomains = ['example.com', 'test.com', 'invalid.com', 'localhost', 'noemail.com'];
    const domain = email.split('@')[1]?.toLowerCase();
    if (dummyDomains.includes(domain)) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Please use a valid email address. Dummy/test emails are not allowed.' };
    }
    // 3. Rate limiting check
    const quota = checkOTPQuota(ss, email);
    if (!quota.canSend) {
      return { success: false, message: quota.message };
    }
    // 4. Clean up only TRULY expired OTPs globally
    cleanExpiredOTPs(ss);
    // 5. Generate and store new OTP
    const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
    const otp = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
    const expiryTimestamp = new Date(new Date().getTime() + OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60 * 1000);
    // Added Name, Mobile, and CreationTimestamp
    otpSheet.appendRow([email.toLowerCase(), otp, expiryTimestamp, 'Registration', name || '', mobile || '', new Date()]);
    
    // Release lock early, so email sending doesn't block other users
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch(e) {}

    // 5. Send email
    const subject = 'HSS Shangus Portal ? Verification Code';
    const body = `Your one-time password (OTP) for the HSS Shangus Admission Portal is: \n\n${otp}\n\nThis code is valid for ${OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES} minutes.`;
    const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
      title: 'Verification Code',
      preheader: 'Your one-time password for HSS Shangus',
      greeting: 'Dear Applicant,',
      intro: `Use the OTP below to verify your email. This code is valid for ${OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES} minutes:`,
      steps: [`OTP: <strong style="font-size:18px; letter-spacing:2px;">${otp}</strong>`],
      footer: 'If you did not request this, you can ignore this email.'
    });
    const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(email, subject, body, { htmlBody, emailAction: 'Registration OTP' });
    if (!emailResult.success) {
      // If email failed, don't return success - let the user know
      return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send OTP email. Please check your email address or try again later. Error: ' + emailResult.message };
    }
    return { success: true, message: 'OTP sent to your email! (Check your Spam/Junk folder if not found).' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'sendRegistrationOTP');
    return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send OTP: ' + e.message };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}
/**
 * [NEW] Helper function to clean OTPs
 */
function cleanExpiredOTPs(ss) {
  const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
  if (!otpSheet || otpSheet.getLastRow() <= 1) return;
  const otpData = otpSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  const rowsToDelete = [];
  for (let i = otpData.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const expiry = new Date(otpData[i][2]).getTime();
    if (now > expiry) {
      rowsToDelete.push(i + 1);
    }
  }
  if (rowsToDelete.length > 0) {
    rowsToDelete.sort((a, b) => b - a).forEach(rowNum => otpSheet.deleteRow(rowNum));
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Helper function to check OTP quota and rate limiting
 */
function checkOTPQuota(ss, email) {
  const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
  if (!otpSheet || otpSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return { canSend: true };
  
  const otpData = otpSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  const emailLower = email.toLowerCase();
  
  let recentAttempts = 0;
  let lastAttemptTime = 0;
  const TEN_MINUTES = 10 * 60 * 1000;
  const ONE_MINUTE = 60 * 1000;
  
  for (let i = otpData.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const rowEmail = String(otpData[i][0]).toLowerCase();
    if (rowEmail === emailLower) {
      const createdAt = new Date(otpData[i][6]).getTime(); // CreationTimestamp is at index 6
      const timeDiff = now - createdAt;
      
      if (timeDiff < TEN_MINUTES) {
        recentAttempts++;
        if (createdAt > lastAttemptTime) lastAttemptTime = createdAt;
      }
    }
  }
  
  if (lastAttemptTime > 0 && (now - lastAttemptTime) < ONE_MINUTE) {
    const waitSeconds = Math.ceil((ONE_MINUTE - (now - lastAttemptTime)) / 1000);
    return { canSend: false, message: `Please wait ${waitSeconds} seconds before resending OTP.` };
  }
  
  if (recentAttempts >= 3) {
    return { canSend: false, message: "You have requested OTP too many times. Please try again after 10 minutes to protect our system quotas." };
  }
  
  return { canSend: true };
}
/**
 * [NEW] Helper function to validate OTP
 */
function validateOTP(ss, email, otp) {
  const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
  const otpData = otpSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  let validOtp = false;
  let otpRowIndex = -1;
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  for (let i = otpData.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const rowEmail = String(otpData[i][0]).toLowerCase();
    const rowOtp = String(otpData[i][1]);
    const expiry = new Date(otpData[i][2]).getTime();
    if (rowEmail === email.toLowerCase() && rowOtp === otp) {
      if (now <= expiry) {
        validOtp = true;
        otpRowIndex = i + 1;
      } else {
        otpSheet.deleteRow(i + 1); // Delete expired OTP
        throw new Error('OTP has expired. Please try again.');
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  return { validOtp, otpRowIndex };
}
function registerUser(name, email, mobile, password, otp, role = 'Student', initialClass = '', initialSubject = '') {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    // 1. Validate OTP
    const { validOtp, otpRowIndex } = validateOTP(ss, email, otp);
    if (!validOtp) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Invalid OTP. Please check your code.' };
    }
    // 2. Check if email was registered in the meantime for THIS role
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const registrationRole_ = (role && role.toLowerCase() === 'teacher') ? 'Teacher' : 'Student';
    
    if (userData.some(row => String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase() && String(row[4]).toLowerCase() === registrationRole_.toLowerCase())) {
      return { success: false, message: `Email already registered as a ${registrationRole_}` };
    }
    // 3. Check if mobile was registered in the meantime (with whitelist check)
    const mobileCheckResult = checkMobileRegistered(mobile, email);
    if (mobileCheckResult.exists && !mobileCheckResult.whitelisted) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Mobile number already registered' };
    }
    
    // 4. Generate Form Number for student (Teachers don't need one but we can generate an ID if needed)
    let formNumber = '';
    if (registrationRole_ === 'Student') {
        formNumber = generateStudentFormNumber(ss);
    }
    
    // 5. Create user with mobile number and assignment details
    const hash = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, password)
      .map(byte => ('0' + (byte & 0xFF).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
    
    // Columns: Email, Name, Hash, Plain, Role, Mobile, Residence, UpdatedOn, InitialClass, InitialSubject
    userSheet.appendRow([email.toLowerCase(), name, hash, password, registrationRole_, mobile, '', new Date(), initialClass, initialSubject]);
    
    // 6. Create a draft application entry for students
    if (registrationRole_ === 'Student') {
        try {
          const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
          const headers = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues()[0];
          const rowData = headers.map(h => {
            if (h === 'Form Number') return formNumber;
            if (h === 'Email Address') return email.toLowerCase();
            if (h === 'Status') return 'Draft';
            if (h === 'Timestamp') return new Date();
            if (h === 'Session') return (getAppSettings()?.session || '2025-26');
            return '';
          });
          admSheet.appendRow(rowData);
        } catch (e) { logError(e, 'registerUser.createDraftApp'); }
    }
    // 7. Delete used OTP
    if (otpRowIndex > -1) {
      ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS).deleteRow(otpRowIndex);
    }
    try { logActivity('Registration', {email: email, role: 'Student'}, `Name: ${name}; Form: ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: 'Registration successful! You can now log in.', formNumber: formNumber };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'registerUser');
    return { success: false, message: `Registration failed: ${e.message}` };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function generateStudentFormNumber(ss) {
  try {
    const config = getAppSettings();
    const session = config?.session || '2025-26';
    // Extract year from session (e.g., "2025-26" -> "25" -> "250000")
    const sessionYear = String(session).split('-')[0];
    const yearPrefix = sessionYear.slice(-2); // Last 2 digits
    const base = yearPrefix + '0000'; // e.g., "250000"
    
    // Find all student form numbers starting with this session prefix
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    let maxNum = parseInt(base);
    
    if (sheet && sheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      const formNumIdx = data[0].indexOf('Form Number');
      if (formNumIdx > -1) {
        for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
          const formNum = String(data[i][formNumIdx]);
          if (formNum.startsWith(yearPrefix)) {
            const num = parseInt(formNum);
            if (num > maxNum) maxNum = num;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    return String(maxNum + 1);
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'generateStudentFormNumber');
    // Fallback: return random form number
    return String(Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000000) + 1000000);
  }
}
function abortTask(taskId) {
  if (!taskId) return { success: false };
  CacheService.getScriptCache().put('abort_' + taskId, 'true', 1200); // 20m expiry
  return { success: true };
}

function getTaskProgress(taskId) {
  try {
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    const data = cache.get('task_' + taskId);
    const aborted = cache.get('abort_' + taskId) === 'true';
    if (!data) return { percent: 0, message: 'Starting...', aborted: aborted };
    const parsed = JSON.parse(data);
    parsed.aborted = aborted;
    return parsed;
  } catch (e) {
    return { percent: 0, message: 'Retrying...', aborted: false };
  }
}

/**
 * Finds all roles registered for a specific email
 */
function getAvailableRolesForEmail(email, ss) {
  if (!email) return [];
  try {
    if (!ss) ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const roles = [];
    const emailLower = email.toLowerCase();
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase() === emailLower) {
        roles.push(String(data[i][4])); // Role column
      }
    }
    // Also check Admissions sheet for "Student" role if not in Users
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (admSheet) {
      const admData = admSheet.getRange(1, 1, admSheet.getLastRow(), 2).getValues();
      const hasAdm = admData.some(row => String(row[1]).toLowerCase() === emailLower);
      if (hasAdm) roles.push('Student');
    }
    return [...new Set(roles)]; // Unique roles
  } catch (e) {
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Specialized login bypass for switching roles within the same session
 */
function switchUserRole(email, newRole) {
  try {
    if (!email || !newRole) throw new Error('Incomplete switch request');
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    
    // Safety check: ensure user actually has this role
    const available = getAvailableRolesForEmail(email, ss);
    if (!available.includes(newRole)) throw new Error('Role not associated with this account');
    
    // Fetch user profile for new role
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const userRow = userData.find(row => 
      String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase() && 
      String(row[4]).toLowerCase() === newRole.toLowerCase()
    );
    
    let userProfile = null;
    if (userRow) {
      userProfile = {
        email: userRow[0],
        name: userRow[1],
        mobile: userRow[5],
        role: userRow[4],
        isAdmin: String(userRow[4]).toLowerCase().includes('admin')
      };
    } else if (newRole === 'Student') {
       // Check admissions for student profile
       const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
       const admData = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
       const headers = admData[0];
       const admRow = admData.find(row => String(row[1]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase());
       if (admRow) {
         userProfile = {
           email: admRow[1],
           name: admRow[2],
           mobile: admRow[Headers.indexOf('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)')], // Simplified or just basic props
           role: 'Student',
           isAdmin: false
         };
       }
    }
    
    if (!userProfile) throw new Error('Profile not found for specific role');
    
    return {
      success: true,
      data: getInitialDataForUser(userProfile)
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'switchUserRole');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}
function loginUser(email, password, keepLoggedIn, deviceId, requiredRole, forceLogin) {
  try {
    // [OPTIMIZED] Fast path: check persistent session cache first
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    const login_cache_ = 'login_' + email.toLowerCase() + '_' + (requiredRole || 'student').toLowerCase();
    const cacheKey = login_cache_;
    const cachedUser = cache.get(cacheKey);
    
    // [OPTIMIZED] Compute hash once before any sheet operations
    const hash = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, password)
      .map(byte => ('0' + (byte & 0xFF).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
    
    // [OPTIMIZED] If we have cached user data and hash matches, skip sheet read
    if (cachedUser) {
      try {
        const cached = JSON.parse(cachedUser);
        if (cached.email.toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase()) {
          const isMatch = (cached.hash === hash) || (cached.hash === '' && cached.password === password);
          
          if (isMatch) {
            // Cache hit! Return user data without reading sheet
            let sessionToken = '';
            const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
            if (deviceId) {
              const conflict = checkSessionConflict(cached.email, deviceId, ss);
              if (conflict && !forceLogin) {
                return {
                  success: false,
                  code: 'SESSION_CONFLICT',
                  message: 'You are already logged in on another device. Logging in here will log you out of other sessions. Do you want to proceed?'
                };
              }
              sessionToken = createPersistentSession(cached.email, deviceId, ss, forceLogin);
            }
            let initialData = null;
            try {
              initialData = getInitialDataForUser(cached.user);
            } catch(e) {}
            return {
              success: true,
              user: cached.user,
              sessionToken: sessionToken || '',
              cached: true,
              initialData: initialData
            };
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { /* Cache parse error - continue to sheet lookup */ }
    }
    
    // [OPTIMIZED] Open spreadsheet only when needed
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    
    // [OPTIMIZED] Use getRange with specific columns (A:G) instead of getDataRange
    // This is ~5x faster for large sheets
    const lastRow = userSheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Email not registered' };
    }
    
    // Get only columns A through G (Email, Name, Hash, Plain, Role, Mobile, Residence)
    const userData = userSheet.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 10).getValues();
    const keep = !!keepLoggedIn && !!deviceId;
    
    let lastMatchedUserRole = null;
    for (const row of userData) {
      if (String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase()) {
        const storedHash = String(row[2]);
        const storedPlain = String(row[3]);
        
        // Multi-layered authentication check
        const isAuthSuccess = (storedHash === hash) || (storedHash === '' && storedPlain === password);
        
        if (isAuthSuccess) {
          const actualRole = String(row[4]).toLowerCase();
          const requestedRole = (requiredRole || 'student').toLowerCase();
          
          let roleMatch = false;
          if (requestedRole === 'president') {
            // President portal allows President or SuperAdmin
            roleMatch = (actualRole === 'president' || actualRole === 'superadmin');
          } else if (requestedRole === 'admin') {
            // Admin tab allows Admin role (strict as per user request)
            roleMatch = (actualRole === 'admin');
          } else if (requestedRole === 'teacher') {
            // Teacher tab allows Teacher role
            roleMatch = (actualRole === 'teacher');
          } else if (requestedRole === 'student') {
            // Student tab allows Student role
            roleMatch = (actualRole === 'student');
          }

          if (!roleMatch) {
            // [NEW] If password matches but role doesn't, check if this user has OTHER roles
            const allAvailable = getAvailableRolesForEmail(email, ss);
            if (allAvailable.map(r => r.toLowerCase()).includes(requestedRole)) {
               // Security verified, role exists for this email but current row isn't it.
               // We should continue the loop to find the EXACT row for the requested role.
               lastMatchedUserRole = row[4];
               continue;
            } else {
               // They have the right password but definitely NOT allowed for this role/portal
               lastMatchedUserRole = row[4];
               continue; 
            }
          }
          
          // [OPTIMIZED] Log activity asynchronously (don't await)
          try {
            const isAdmin = (actualRole === 'admin' || actualRole === 'superadmin' || actualRole === 'president');
            const logUser = { email: row[0], role: row[4] };
            // Fire-and-forget logging to avoid blocking response
            Promise.resolve().then(() => logActivity(isAdmin ? 'Admin Login' : (actualRole === 'teacher' ? 'Teacher Login' : 'Student Login'), logUser, 'Login success'));
          } catch (e) {}
          
          // [OPTIMIZED] Cache user data for 5 minutes to speed up next login
          const userDataToCache = {
            email: row[0],
            hash: storedHash,
            password: storedHash === '' ? password : null,
            user: {
              name: row[1],
              email: row[0],
              role: row[4],
              mobile: row[5] || '',
              residence: row[6] || '',
              initialClass: row[8] || '',
              initialSubject: row[9] || ''
            }
          };
          cache.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(userDataToCache), 300); // 5 minutes
          
          let sessionToken = '';
          if (deviceId) {
            const conflict = checkSessionConflict(row[0], deviceId, ss);
            if (conflict && !forceLogin) {
              return {
                success: false,
                code: 'SESSION_CONFLICT',
                message: 'You are already logged in on another device. Logging in here will log you out of other sessions. Do you want to proceed?'
              };
            }
            try {
              const persistKey = 'persist_' + row[0];
              const count = parseInt(cache.get(persistKey) || '0', 10) + 1;
              cache.put(persistKey, String(count), 600);
              if (count > 5) {
                return { success: false, message: 'Too many persistent login attempts. Please try again later.' };
              }
              sessionToken = createPersistentSession(row[0], deviceId, ss, forceLogin);
            } catch (lockErr) {
              sessionToken = '';
            }
          }
          const userObj = {
            name: row[1],
            email: row[0],
            role: row[4],
            mobile: row[5] || '',
            residence: row[6] || '',
            initialClass: row[8] || '',
            initialSubject: row[9] || ''
          };
          let initialData = null;
          try {
            initialData = getInitialDataForUser(userObj);
          } catch(e) {}
          return {
            success: true,
            user: userObj,
            sessionToken: sessionToken || '',
            initialData: initialData
          };
        }
        return { success: false, message: 'Incorrect password' };
      }
    }
    if (lastMatchedUserRole) {
       return { success: false, message: `Access denied. Your account is registered as ${lastMatchedUserRole}, but you selected ${requiredRole} login.` };
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'Email not registered' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'loginUser');
    return { success: false, message: 'Login error: ' + e.message };
  }
}
function sendPasswordResetOTP(email) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    // 1. Check if email is registered
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const emailColumn = userSheet.getRange('A:A').getValues().flat();
    let userName = '';
    const userIndex = emailColumn.findIndex(e => String(e).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase());
    if (userIndex === -1) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Email not registered.' };
    }
    let userMobile = '';
    const userRow = userSheet.getRange(userIndex + 1, 1, 1, 8).getValues()[0];
    if (userRow) {
      userName = userRow[1];
      userMobile = userRow[5];
    }

    // 2. Rate limiting check
    const quota = checkOTPQuota(ss, email);
    if (!quota.canSend) {
      return { success: false, message: quota.message };
    }
    // 3. Clean up old/expired OTPs
    cleanExpiredOTPs(ss);
    // 4. Generate and store new OTP
    const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
    const otp = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000).toString();
    const expiryTimestamp = new Date(new Date().getTime() + OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60 * 1000);
    // Log to sheet
    otpSheet.appendRow([email.toLowerCase(), otp, expiryTimestamp, 'PasswordReset', userName || '', userMobile || '', new Date()]);
    // 4. Send email
    const subject = 'HSS Shangus Portal ? Password Reset Code';
    const body = `Dear ${userName},\n\nYour one-time password (OTP) for resetting your password is: \n\n${otp}\n\nThis code is valid for ${OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES} minutes. If you did not request this, please ignore this email.`;
    const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
      title: 'Password Reset Code',
      preheader: 'Use this code to reset your password',
      greeting: `Dear ${userName},`,
      intro: `Use the OTP below to reset your password. This code is valid for ${OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES} minutes:`,
      steps: [`OTP: <strong style="font-size:18px; letter-spacing:2px;">${otp}</strong>`],
      footer: 'If you did not request this, you can ignore this email.'
    });
    const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(email, subject, body, { htmlBody, emailAction: 'Password Reset OTP' });
    if (!emailResult.success) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send password reset email. Please check your email address or try again later. Error: ' + emailResult.message };
    }
    return { success: true, message: 'Password reset OTP sent to your email! (Check your Spam/Junk folder if not found).' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'sendPasswordResetOTP');
    return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send OTP: ' + e.message };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}
/**
 * [NEW] Resets the user's password using a valid OTP.
 */
function resetPasswordWithOTP(email, otp, newPassword) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    // 1. Validate OTP
    const { validOtp, otpRowIndex } = validateOTP(ss, email, otp);
    if (!validOtp) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Invalid or expired OTP.' };
    }
    // 2. Find user and update hash
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email');
    const hashIdx = headers.indexOf('PasswordHash');
    let userRowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase()) {
        userRowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (userRowIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error('User not found after OTP validation.');
    }
    // 3. Update password hash
    const newHash = Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, newPassword)
      .map(byte => ('0' + (byte & 0xFF).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
    // Update both hash and plain password (admin requested plain storage). Note: insecure.
    userSheet.getRange(userRowIndex, hashIdx + 1).setValue(newHash);
    const plainIdx = headers.indexOf('PasswordPlain');
    if (plainIdx > -1) {
      userSheet.getRange(userRowIndex, plainIdx + 1).setValue(newPassword);
    }
    // 4. Delete used OTP
    if (otpRowIndex > -1) {
      ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS).deleteRow(otpRowIndex);
    }
    try { logActivity('Password Reset', email, 'Password reset successful'); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: 'Password reset successful! You can now log in with your new password.' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'resetPasswordWithOTP');
    return { success: false, message: `Password reset failed: ${e.message}` };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}
/**
 * [NEW] Sends an email reminder of the login email, as requested.
 * Does NOT send the password.
 */
function requestLoginReminder(email) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    data.shift(); // Remove headers
    let userFound = null;
    for (const row of data) {
      if (String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase()) {
        userFound = { name: row[1], email: row[0] };
        break;
      }
    }
    if (userFound) {
      const subject = 'HSS Shangus Portal ? Login Details';
      const body = `Dear ${userFound.name},\n\n` +
        `Here is the email you used to register for the HSS Shangus Admission Portal:\n\n` +
        `Email: ${userFound.email}\n\n` +
        `If you have forgotten your password, please use the "Forgot Password" link on the login page to reset it.\n\n` +
        `Best regards,\nAdms & Exams Team, HSS Shangus`;
      const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
        title: 'Login Email Reminder',
        preheader: 'Your registered email for HSS Shangus',
        greeting: `Dear ${userFound.name},`,
        intro: 'You registered with the following email address:',
        steps: [`<strong>${userFound.email}</strong>`],
        footer: 'To reset your password, use the ?Forgot Password? link on the login page.'
      });
    sendEmailSafe(userFound.email, subject, body, { htmlBody, emailAction: 'Login Email Reminder' });
    return { success: true, message: 'Login reminder sent to your email.' };
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'Email not registered.' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'requestLoginReminder');
    return { success: false, message: 'Error: ' + e.message };
  }
}
function updateUserProfile(userEmail, name, mobile, residence) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    data.shift();
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email');
    const nameIdx = headers.indexOf('Name');
    const mobileIdx = headers.indexOf('Mobile');
