    const residenceIdx = headers.indexOf('Residence');
    const updatedOnIdx = headers.indexOf('UpdatedOn');
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === userEmail.toLowerCase()) {
        userSheet.getRange(i + 2, nameIdx + 1).setValue(name);
        userSheet.getRange(i + 2, mobileIdx + 1).setValue(mobile);
        userSheet.getRange(i + 2, residenceIdx + 1).setValue(residence);
        userSheet.getRange(i + 2, updatedOnIdx + 1).setValue(new Date());
        try { logActivity('Update Profile', userEmail, `Name: ${name}, Mobile: ${mobile}`); } catch (e) {}
        return { success: true, message: 'Profile updated successfully' };
      }
    }
    throw new Error('User not found');
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'updateUserProfile');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}
// =================================================================
// API FUNCTIONS (DATA) - Updated for subjects
// =================================================================
function getFormStructure() {
  const cacheKey = 'form_structure_v1';
  const cached = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached);
  
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const structSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.FORM_STRUCTURE);
    if (!structSheet || structSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return [];
    const data = structSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    data.shift(); // Remove headers
    const result = data.map(row => ({
      fieldName: row[0],
      fieldType: row[1],
      options: row[2],
      required: row[3] === 'TRUE',
      helpText: row[4],
      placeholder: row[5],
      classes: row[6] || ''
    }));
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(result), 1800); } catch(e) {}
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getFormStructure');
    return [];
  }
}
function getInitialDataForUser(user) {
  try {
    if (!user) throw new Error('User not provided');
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    const role = (user.role || 'Student');
    const roleLower = role.toLowerCase();
    
    const availableRoles = getAvailableRolesForEmail(user.email, ss);
    user.availableRoles = availableRoles;

    if (roleLower === 'teacher') {
      return {
        settings: getAppSettings(),
        profile: user,
        appVersion: PORTAL_VERSION,
        formStructure: getFormStructure(),
        subjectsConfig: getSubjectsConfig(),
        availableRoles: availableRoles
      };
    } else if (isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      const data = getAdminDashboardData(user);
      data.availableRoles = availableRoles;
      data.profile = user; // Ensure profile is included
      return data;
    } else {
      const data = getStudentApplicationData(user);
      data.availableRoles = availableRoles;
      data.profile = user; // Ensure profile is included
      return data;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getInitialDataForUser');
    throw new Error('Failed to fetch data: ' + e.message);
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Returns settings that are safe for non-logged in users (e.g. logo)
 */
function getPublicSettings() {
  try {
    const settings = getAppSettings();
    return {
      logo_url: settings.logo_url,
      logo_url_resolved: settings.logo_url_resolved,
      logoUrl: settings.logoUrl
    };
  } catch (e) {
    return {};
  }
}
/**
 * [MODIFIED] Returns ALL applications for a student, not just the last one.
 * This allows the client to offer pre-filling data from a previous year's application.
 */
function getStudentApplicationData(user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const commonData = {
      settings: getAppSettings(),
      formStructure: getFormStructure(),
      subjectsConfig: getSubjectsConfig(),
      profile: user,
      appVersion: PORTAL_VERSION
    };
    if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return {
        applications: [],
        ...commonData
      };
    }
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    let userRows = []; // Changed from userRow = null
    for (let j = 1; j < data.length; j++) { // Loop from top to bottom
      if (String(data[j][emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === user.email.toLowerCase()) {
        userRows.push({ rowData: data[j], rowIndex: j + 1 }); // Store row data and sheet index
      }
    }
    if (userRows.length === 0) {
      return {
        applications: [],
        ...commonData
      };
    }
    const applicationsData = [];
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    let unlocksData = null;
    if (unlocksSheet && unlocksSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    }
    const currentSession = commonData.settings?.session || '2025-26';
    for (const userRow of userRows) {
      const appData = headers.reduce((obj, h, i) => {
        obj[h] = userRow.rowData[i] instanceof Date ?
          userRow.rowData[i].toISOString() :
          userRow.rowData[i];
        return obj;
      }, {});
      // Preserve original session on records; do not override here
      appData.lastModified = new Date().toISOString(); // Server timestamp
      // Check for unlocks
      let isEditable = appData['Status'] === 'Draft';
      let unlockExpiry = null;
      let draftCount = 0;
      let isUnlockedEditMode = false;
      if (appData['Status'] === 'Submitted' && unlocksData) {
        const now = new Date().getTime();
        const formNumber = appData['Form Number'];
        for (let j = 1; j < unlocksData.length; j++) {
          if (unlocksData[j][0] == formNumber) {
            const expTime = new Date(unlocksData[j][2]).getTime();
            draftCount = unlocksData[j][5] || 0;
            const notes = String(unlocksData[j][4] || '');
            if (now < expTime) {
              isEditable = true;
              isUnlockedEditMode = true;
              unlockExpiry = unlocksData[j][2].toISOString();
            } else {
              // Expired: lock
              isEditable = false;
              // Check if needs new PDF
              if ((appData['Last Edited'] && new Date(appData['Last Edited']) > new Date(unlocksData[j][1])) && (!appData['PDF URL'] || true)) { // Always generate if edited after unlock
                try {
                  const { pdfBlob, pdfUrl: newPdfUrl, pdfDownloadUrl } = generatePdf(appData, true); // isEdit true
                  // Update sheet
                  sheet.getRange(userRow.rowIndex, pdfIdx + 1).setValue(newPdfUrl);
                  appData['PDF URL'] = newPdfUrl;
                  // Send email
                  const subject = `Updated Admission Form - Form #${formNumber} (Edited)`;
                  const body = `Dear ${appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"]},\n\nYour admission form has been updated and locked after the edit period.\n\nDownload your updated form: ${newPdfUrl}\n(If download does not start, open in browser and save as PDF.)\n\nRegards,\nHSS Shangus Admission Portal`;
                  const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
                    title: 'Form Updated and Locked',
                    preheader: `Form #${formNumber} updated and locked`,
                    greeting: `Dear ${appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"]},`,
                    intro: 'Your admission form was updated during the unlock window and is now locked. Use the button below to download your updated PDF.',
                    steps: ['Download the updated form', 'Review and follow earlier submission instructions'],
                    footer: 'For any queries, contact the admissions office.',
                    ctaLabel: 'Download Updated PDF',
                    ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || newPdfUrl
                  });
                  const safeEmail = String(user?.email || '').trim();
                  if (safeEmail) {
                    sendEmailSafe(safeEmail, subject, body, { htmlBody: htmlBody, attachments: pdfBlob ? [pdfBlob] : undefined, emailAction: 'PDF Ready Reminder', formNumber: String(formNumber) });
                  }
                } catch (e) {
                  logError(e, 'getStudentApplicationData-pdf-on-expiry');
                }
              }
            }
            if (notes && notes.indexOf('Rejected:') === 0) {
              appData.rejectionReason = notes.replace(/^Rejected:\s*/, '');
            }
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      appData.isEditable = isEditable;
      appData.unlockExpiry = unlockExpiry;
      appData.draftCount = draftCount;
      appData.isUnlockedEditMode = isUnlockedEditMode;
      applicationsData.push(appData);
    }
    return {
      applications: applicationsData, // Return array
      ...commonData
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getStudentApplicationData');
    throw e;
  }
}

// =================================================================
// MOBILE WHITELIST MANAGEMENT FUNCTIONS (ADMIN ONLY)
// =================================================================

/**
 * Add email-mobile pair to whitelist (allows twins with same parent mobile)
 * Admin function - should be called by authenticated admin users only
 */
function addMobileWhitelist(email, mobile, reason, adminUser) {
  try {
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(adminUser)) return { success: false, message: 'Permission denied' };
    // Validate inputs
    if (!email || !mobile) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Email and mobile are required' };
    }
    
    if (!/^[0-9]{10}$/.test(mobile.trim())) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Invalid mobile format. Must be 10 digits.' };
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const whitelistSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
    
    if (!whitelistSheet) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Mobile whitelist sheet not found' };
    }
    
    // Check if entry already exists
    if (whitelistSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const existingData = whitelistSheet.getRange(2, 1, whitelistSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
      const exists = existingData.some(row => 
        String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === String(email).toLowerCase() && 
        String(row[1]).trim() === String(mobile).trim()
      );
      
      if (exists) {
        return { success: false, message: 'This email-mobile pair is already whitelisted' };
      }
    }
    
    // Add new whitelist entry
    whitelistSheet.appendRow([
      email.toLowerCase(),
      mobile.trim(),
      reason || 'Twins/siblings with same parent mobile',
      String(adminUser.email).toLowerCase(),
      new Date()
    ]);
    try { logActivity('Add Mobile Whitelist', adminUser, `${email} ? ${mobile} (${reason || ''})`); } catch (e) {}
    
    return { 
      success: true, 
      message: `Email-mobile pair (${email}, ${mobile}) has been whitelisted for duplicate mobile registration`
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'addMobileWhitelist');
    return { success: false, message: `Error adding whitelist: ${e.message}` };
  }
}

/**
 * Remove email-mobile pair from whitelist (revoke duplicate mobile permission)
 * Admin function
 */
function removeMobileWhitelist(email, mobile, adminUser) {
  try {
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(adminUser)) return { success: false, message: 'Permission denied' };
    if (!email || !mobile) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Email and mobile are required' };
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const whitelistSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
    
    if (!whitelistSheet || whitelistSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { success: false, message: 'No whitelist entries found' };
    }
    
    const data = whitelistSheet.getRange(2, 1, whitelistSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5).getValues();
    let foundRow = -1;
    
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase() === String(email).toLowerCase() && 
          String(data[i][1]).trim() === String(mobile).trim()) {
        foundRow = i + 2; // +2 because data array starts at 0 and sheet rows start at 1 (header at 1)
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (foundRow === -1) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Whitelist entry not found' };
    }
    
    whitelistSheet.deleteRow(foundRow);
    try { logActivity('Remove Mobile Whitelist', adminUser, `${email} ? ${mobile}`); } catch (e) {}
    
    return { 
      success: true, 
      message: `Whitelist entry for ${email} removed. This email can no longer register with duplicate mobile numbers.`
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'removeMobileWhitelist');
    return { success: false, message: `Error removing whitelist: ${e.message}` };
  }
}

/**
 * Get all whitelisted email-mobile pairs
 * Admin function
 */
function getWhitelistedMobiles(user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      return { success: false, message: 'Permission denied' };
    }
    const whitelistSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
    
    if (!whitelistSheet || whitelistSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { success: true, entries: [] };
    }
    
    const headers = whitelistSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).getValues()[0];
    const data = whitelistSheet.getRange(2, 1, whitelistSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5).getValues();
    
    const entries = data.map(row => ({
      email: row[0],
      mobile: row[1],
      reason: row[2],
      adminEmail: row[3],
      dateAdded: row[4]
    }));
    
    return { success: true, entries: entries, totalCount: entries.length };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getWhitelistedMobiles');
    return { success: false, message: `Error fetching whitelist: ${e.message}` };
  }
}

/**
 * Check if specific email-mobile pair is whitelisted
 */
function isMobileWhitelisted(email, mobile) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const whitelistSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
    
    if (!whitelistSheet || whitelistSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { whitelisted: false };
    }
    
    const data = whitelistSheet.getRange(2, 1, whitelistSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 2).getValues();
    const isWhitelisted = data.some(row => 
      String(row[0]).toLowerCase() === String(email).toLowerCase() && 
      String(row[1]).trim() === String(mobile).trim()
    );
    
    return { whitelisted: isWhitelisted };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'isMobileWhitelisted');
    return { whitelisted: false, error: e.message };
  }
}

function getAdminDashboardData(user) {
  // Use chunked cache for full dashboard payload
  const cacheKey = 'admin_dashboard_full_cache_v3';
  const cached = getCacheChunked(cacheKey);
  if (cached) {
    // Failsafe: Ensure cached data has necessary fields
    const data = (typeof cached === 'string') ? JSON.parse(cached) : cached;
    if (data.applications) return data;
  }

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);

    const SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL = 'adm.exam.hss.shangus@gmail.com';
    const currentUserEmail = (user?.email || '').toLowerCase();
    const currentUserRole = (user?.role || '').toLowerCase();
    const isSuperAdmin = currentUserEmail === SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL || currentUserRole === 'superadmin' || currentUserRole === 'president';
    
    const userProfileMap = (() => {
      try {
        if (!userSheet || userSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return { profiles: {}, adminTabs: {} };
        const udata = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const headers = udata[0];
        const permColIdx = headers.indexOf('Permissions');
        
        // Determine if the current caller is an admin
        const currentUserRow = udata.find(r => String(r[0]).toLowerCase() === currentUserEmail);
        const isAdminType = (r) => r === 'Admin' || r === 'SuperAdmin' || r === 'President';
        const currentUserIsAdmin = isSuperAdmin || (currentUserRow && isAdminType(String(currentUserRow[4])));
        
        const map = {};
        const adminTabs = {};

        const rolePriority = { 'president': 4, 'superadmin': 3, 'admin': 2, 'teacher': 1, 'student': 0 };
        
        for (let i = 1; i < udata.length; i++) {
          const row = udata[i];
          const email = String(row[0] || '').toLowerCase().trim();
          if (!email) continue;
          
          const role = String(row[4] || '');
          const currentRoleRank = rolePriority[role.toLowerCase()] || 0;
          const existingRoleRank = map[email] ? (rolePriority[map[email].role.toLowerCase()] || 0) : -1;
          
          if (currentRoleRank > existingRoleRank) {
            map[email] = {
              name: String(row[1] || ''),
              role: role,
              accountMobile: String(row[5] || ''),
              residence: String(row[6] || ''),
              registeredAt: row[7] instanceof Date ? row[7].toISOString() : String(row[7] || ''),
              passwordPlain: currentUserIsAdmin ? String(row[3] || '') : '********'
            };
          }
          
          // Load tab permissions (keep any that exist)
          if (permColIdx !== -1 && row[permColIdx]) {
            try {
              const perms = JSON.parse(row[permColIdx]);
              if (Array.isArray(perms) && (!adminTabs[email] || perms.length > adminTabs[email].length)) {
                adminTabs[email] = perms;
              }
            } catch (e) { /* invalid JSON */ }
          }
        }
        return { profiles: map, adminTabs: adminTabs };
      } catch (e) { return { profiles: {}, adminTabs: {} }; }
    })();

    const appSettings = getAppSettings();
    if (!appSettings.admin_tabs) appSettings.admin_tabs = userProfileMap.adminTabs;

    // Prepare mobile whitelist
    let mobileWhitelist = [];
    try {
      const wlSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.MOBILE_WHITELIST);
      if (wlSheet && wlSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const data = wlSheet.getRange(2, 1, wlSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 5).getValues();
        mobileWhitelist = data.map(r => ({
          email: String(r[0] || ''),
          mobile: String(r[1] || ''),
          reason: String(r[2] || ''),
          adminEmail: String(r[3] || ''),
          dateAdded: r[4] instanceof Date ? r[4].toISOString() : String(r[4] || '')
        }));
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    if (!sheet || sheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return {
        applications: [],
        settings: appSettings,
        formStructure: getFormStructure(),
        subjectsConfig: getSubjectsConfig(), // New
        userProfiles: userProfileMap.profiles,
        mobileWhitelist,
        lastModified: new Date().toISOString(),
        appVersion: PORTAL_VERSION
      };
    }

    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data.shift();
    const applications = data.map(row =>
      headers.reduce((obj, h, i) => {
        obj[h] = row[i] instanceof Date ?
          row[i].toISOString() :
          row[i];
        return obj;
      }, {})
    ).reverse();
    // Augment with account mobile and residence
    applications.forEach(app => {
      try {
        const email = String(app['Email Address'] || '').toLowerCase();
        const profile = userProfileMap.profiles[email];
        if (profile) {
          app['Account Mobile'] = profile.accountMobile || '';
          if (!app['Residence'] && profile.residence) {
            app['Residence'] = profile.residence;
          }
          app['Account Name'] = profile.name || '';
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    });
    // Add lastModified for change detection
    applications.forEach(app => app.lastModified = new Date().toISOString());
    // Cache Unlocks data for faster lookup
    const unlocksMap = {};
    try {
      const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
      if (unlocksSheet && unlocksSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const uValues = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        const uHeaders = uValues[0];
        const uIdxForm = uHeaders.indexOf('Form Number');
        const uIdxStart = uHeaders.indexOf('Unlock Start');
        const uIdxExp = uHeaders.indexOf('Unlock Expiry');
        const uIdxAdmin = uHeaders.indexOf('Admin Email');
        const uIdxNotes = uHeaders.indexOf('Notes');
        
        for (let j = 1; j < uValues.length; j++) {
          const fNum = String(uValues[j][uIdxForm]);
          if (!fNum) continue;
          unlocksMap[fNum] = {
            start: uValues[j][uIdxStart],
            expiry: uValues[j][uIdxExp],
            admin: String(uValues[j][uIdxAdmin] || ''),
            notes: String(uValues[j][uIdxNotes] || '')
          };
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { logError(e, 'processUnlocks'); }

    const now = new Date().getTime();
    applications.forEach(app => {
      try {
        const formNumber = String(app['Form Number']);
        // 1. Initial metadata from app sheet
        app.rejectedByAdmin = app['Rejected By'] || '';
        
        // 2. Override/Augment with active or recent unlock data
        const unlock = unlocksMap[formNumber];
        if (unlock) {
          const expTime = unlock.expiry instanceof Date ? unlock.expiry.getTime() : new Date(unlock.expiry).getTime();
          const isActive = now < expTime;
          app.isUnlockedEditMode = isActive && (app['Status'] === 'Submitted');
          app.unlockStart = unlock.start instanceof Date ? unlock.start.toISOString() : (unlock.start ? new Date(unlock.start).toISOString() : null);
          app.unlockExpiry = unlock.expiry instanceof Date ? unlock.expiry.toISOString() : (unlock.expiry ? new Date(unlock.expiry).toISOString() : null);
          
          if (unlock.notes && unlock.notes.indexOf('Rejected:') === 0) {
            app.rejectionReason = unlock.notes.replace(/^Rejected:\s*/, '');
            // Prefer the specific admin who did the current rejection recorded in unlocks
            if (unlock.admin) app.rejectedByAdmin = unlock.admin;
          }

          // Resubmission detection
          if (app.rejectionReason && app['Status'] === 'Submitted' && app['Last Edited'] && unlock.start) {
            const lastEdited = new Date(app['Last Edited']);
            const start = unlock.start instanceof Date ? unlock.start : new Date(unlock.start);
            if (lastEdited > start) {
              app.wasResubmitted = true;
              app.resubmittedReason = app.rejectionReason;
            }
          }
        }
      } catch (e) { console.error('Error processing app meta:', e); }
    });
    
    // Clear unused iteration code below
    /* Previous nested loop removed for optimization and accuracy */

    const result = {
      applications,
      allHeaders: headers,
      settings: appSettings,
      formStructure: getFormStructure(),
      subjectsConfig: getSubjectsConfig(), // New
      userProfiles: userProfileMap.profiles,
      mobileWhitelist,
      photoMap: (() => {
          const cacheKey = 'photo_map_v1';
          const cached = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(cacheKey);
          if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached);

          const photoMap = {};
          try {
            const photoFolderId = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId;
            if (photoFolderId) {
              const photoFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(photoFolderId);
              const files = photoFolder.getFiles();
              while (files.hasNext()) {
                const file = files.next();
                const name = file.getName();
                photoMap[name] = file.getId();
              }
            }
          } catch (e) { logError(e, 'getAdminDashboardData.buildPhotoMap'); }
          try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(photoMap), 3600); } catch(e) {}
          return photoMap;
      })(),
      lastModified: new Date().toISOString(),
      appVersion: PORTAL_VERSION
    };
    
    // Save to chunked cache for fast immediate reloads
    putCacheChunked(cacheKey, result, 120);
    
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getAdminDashboardData');
    throw e;
  }
}
/**
 * [NEW] Helper to cache large data in chunks in CacheService
 */
function putCacheChunked(baseKey, data, ttl = 600) {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(data);
  const chunkSize = 90000;
  const totalChunks = Math.ceil(jsonStr.length / chunkSize);
  for (let i = 0; i < totalChunks; i++) {
    cache.put(`${baseKey}_${i}`, jsonStr.substring(i * chunkSize, (i + 1) * chunkSize), ttl);
  }
  cache.put(`${baseKey}_count`, totalChunks.toString(), ttl);
}

function getCacheChunked(baseKey) {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const countStr = cache.get(`${baseKey}_count`);
  if (!countStr) return null;
  const count = parseInt(countStr);
  let combinedStr = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const part = cache.get(`${baseKey}_${i}`);
    if (!part) return null;
    combinedStr += part;
  }
  try { return JSON.parse(combinedStr); } catch (e) { return null; }
}

function getAppSettings() {
  const cacheKey = 'app_settings_v3';
  const cached = CacheService.getScriptCache().get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return JSON.parse(cached);

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (!configSheet || configSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return {
        allow_9th: true,
        allow_10th: true,
        allow_11th: true,
        allow_12th: true
      };
    }
    const data = configSheet.getRange('A2:B' + configSheet.getLastRow()).getValues();
    const settingsObj = data.reduce((obj, row) => {
      const key = row[0];
      const val = row[1];
      if (key === 'columnWidths') {
        try {
          obj[key] = JSON.parse(val);
        } catch (e) {
          obj[key] = {};
        }
      } else if (typeof val === 'string') {
        const up = val.toUpperCase();
        if (up === 'TRUE' || up === 'FALSE') {
          obj[key] = up === 'TRUE';
        } else {
          obj[key] = val;
        }
      } else {
        obj[key] = String(val).toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
      }
      return obj;
    }, {});
    
    // [NEW] Append Centralized Portal Hub configuration
    settingsObj.portals = SCHOOL_PORTALS;
    
    try {
      const raw = settingsObj['logo_url'] || settingsObj['logoUrl'] || '';
      let resolved = '';
      if (raw) {
        const folderMatch = String(raw).match(/\/folders\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const fileMatch = String(raw).match(/\/file\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/) || String(raw).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        if (folderMatch) {
          const folderId = folderMatch[1];
          const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId);
          const files = folder.getFiles();
          let best = null;
          let bestTime = 0;
          while (files.hasNext()) {
            const f = files.next();
            const mime = f.getMimeType();
            if (mime && mime.indexOf('image/') === 0) {
              const t = f.getLastUpdated().getTime();
              if (t > bestTime) { best = f; bestTime = t; }
            }
          }
          if (best) {
            best.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
            resolved = 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id=' + best.getId();
          }
        } else if (fileMatch) {
          const fileId = fileMatch[1];
          const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
          file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
          resolved = 'https://drive.google.com/uc?export=view&id=' + fileId;
        } else {
          resolved = raw;
        }
      }
      if (resolved) settingsObj['logo_url_resolved'] = resolved;
    } catch (e) {}
    try {
      if (settingsObj.columnWidths && typeof settingsObj.columnWidths === 'string') {
        settingsObj.columnWidths = JSON.parse(settingsObj.columnWidths);
      }
      if (settingsObj.admin_tabs && typeof settingsObj.admin_tabs === 'string') {
        settingsObj.admin_tabs = JSON.parse(settingsObj.admin_tabs);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    
    // Cache for 1 hour
    try { CacheService.getScriptCache().put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(settingsObj), 3600); } catch(e) {}
    
    return settingsObj;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getAppSettings');
    return {
      allow_9th: true,
      allow_10th: true,
      allow_11th: true,
      allow_12th: true
    };
  }
}
function getUserRoleFromSheet(email, ss) {
  if (!email) return 'Student';
  try {
    if (!ss) ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    if (!userSheet) return 'Student';
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const emailStr = String(email).toLowerCase().trim();
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim() === emailStr) {
        return data[i][4] || 'Student';
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getUserRoleFromSheet');
  }
  return 'Student';
}
function ensureUserRole(user, ss) {
  if (!user || !user.email) return user;
  const role = getUserRoleFromSheet(user.email, ss);
  if (role) user.role = role;
  return user;
}
function normalizeImageFileData(fileData) {
  if (!fileData || !fileData.base64Data) return null;
  const mime = String(fileData.mimeType || '').toLowerCase();
  if (!mime || mime.indexOf('image/') !== 0) {
    throw new Error('Invalid photo format. Please upload a valid image.');
  }
  const name = String(fileData.fileName || 'photo.jpg');
  const map = {
    'image/jpeg': 'jpg',
    'image/jpg': 'jpg',
    'image/png': 'png',
    'image/webp': 'webp',
    'image/gif': 'gif',
    'image/bmp': 'bmp',
    'image/heic': 'heic',
    'image/heif': 'heif'
  };
  let ext = map[mime];
  if (!ext) {
    const parts = name.split('.');
    ext = parts.length > 1 ? parts.pop().toLowerCase() : 'jpg';
  }
  const baseName = name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');
  return {
    base64Data: fileData.base64Data,
    mimeType: mime,
    fileName: `${baseName}.${ext}`
  };
}
function ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers) {
  let currentHeaders = headers || sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  const missing = FORM_STATE_HEADERS.filter(h => currentHeaders.indexOf(h) === -1);
  if (missing.length > 0) {
    missing.forEach(h => {
      sheet.insertColumnAfter(sheet.getLastColumn());
      sheet.getRange(1, sheet.getLastColumn()).setValue(h);
    });
    currentHeaders = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
  }
  return currentHeaders;
}
function ensureSessionsSheet(ss) {
  let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SESSIONS);
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.SESSIONS);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 7).setValues([['Email', 'DeviceId', 'TokenHash', 'CreatedAt', 'ExpiresAt', 'LastUsedAt', 'Revoked']])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
  }
  return sheet;
}
function hashToken(token) {
  return Utilities.computeDigest(Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256, token)
    .map(byte => ('0' + (byte & 0xFF).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
}
function checkSessionConflict(email, deviceId, ss) {
  if (!deviceId) return null;
  const sessionsSheet = ensureSessionsSheet(ss);
  const data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const emailLower = String(email).toLowerCase().trim();
  const now = new Date().getTime();
  
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    const sEmail = String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim();
    const sDeviceId = String(data[i][1]).trim();
    const expires = new Date(data[i][4]).getTime();
    const revoked = String(data[i][6] || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
    
    if (sEmail === emailLower && sDeviceId !== deviceId && !revoked && !isNaN(expires) && expires > now) {
      return {
        conflict: true,
        deviceId: sDeviceId,
        expires: expires
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function getUserByEmail(email, ss) {
  if (!email) return null;
  if (!ss) ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
  if (!userSheet) return null;
  const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const emailLower = String(email).toLowerCase().trim();
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim() === emailLower) {
      return {
        name: data[i][1],
        email: data[i][0],
        role: data[i][4],
        mobile: data[i][5] || '',
        residence: data[i][6] || '',
        initialClass: data[i][8] || '',
        initialSubject: data[i][9] || ''
      };
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function createPersistentSession(email, deviceId, ss, terminateConflicting) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const sessionsSheet = ensureSessionsSheet(ss);
    const token = Utilities.getUuid() + Utilities.getUuid();
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
    const now = new Date();
    const expires = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const nowTime = now.getTime();
    
    // Read all sessions
    let data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const emailLower = String(email).toLowerCase().trim();
    const header = data[0];
    
    const activeRows = [header];
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    
    // 1. Filter out expired, revoked, and conflicting sessions in memory
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const sEmail = String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim();
      const sDeviceId = String(data[i][1]).trim();
      const sExpires = new Date(data[i][4]).getTime();
      const sRevoked = String(data[i][6] || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
      
      const isExpired = isNaN(sExpires) || nowTime > sExpires;
      const isConflict = sEmail === emailLower && sDeviceId !== deviceId && terminateConflicting;
      
      if (sRevoked || isExpired || isConflict) {
        if (isConflict) {
          try {
            cache.put('sess_active_' + String(data[i][2]), 'REVOKED', 21600);
          } catch(e) {}
        }
        continue;
      }
      activeRows.push(data[i]);
    }
    
    // 2. Count active sessions for this email to enforce 5-session limit
    let userSessionIndices = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < activeRows.length; i++) {
      if (String(activeRows[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim() === emailLower) {
        userSessionIndices.push({ index: i, expires: new Date(activeRows[i][4]).getTime() });
      }
    }
    
    // Sort oldest first
    userSessionIndices.sort((a, b) => a.expires - b.expires);
    
    // If we have 5 or more (including the new one we will append), delete oldest from activeRows
    if (userSessionIndices.length >= 5) {
      const toDelete = userSessionIndices.slice(0, userSessionIndices.length - 4);
      // Remove those indices from activeRows and mark them revoked in cache
      const deleteIndices = new Set(toDelete.map(item => item.index));
      const filteredRows = [header];
      for (let i = 1; i < activeRows.length; i++) {
        if (deleteIndices.has(i)) {
          try {
            cache.put('sess_active_' + String(activeRows[i][2]), 'REVOKED', 21600);
          } catch(e) {}
        } else {
          filteredRows.push(activeRows[i]);
        }
      }
      activeRows.length = 0;
      activeRows.push(...filteredRows);
    }
    
    // 3. Append the new session row to activeRows
    activeRows.push([email, deviceId, tokenHash, now, expires, now, 'FALSE']);
    
    // Cache the new session status
    try {
      cache.put('sess_active_' + tokenHash, 'VALID', 21600);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    // 4. Overwrite sheet contents in a single call (extremely fast!)
    sessionsSheet.clearContents();
    sessionsSheet.getRange(1, 1, activeRows.length, header.length).setValues(activeRows);
    
    return token;
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
function validatePersistentSession(token, deviceId) {
  try {
    if (!token || !deviceId) return { success: false, message: 'Invalid session' };
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sessionsSheet = ensureSessionsSheet(ss);
    const data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
    const now = new Date();
    let rowIndex = -1;
    let email = '';
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][2]) === String(tokenHash) && String(data[i][1]) === String(deviceId)) {
        rowIndex = i + 1;
        email = data[i][0];
        const expires = new Date(data[i][4]);
        const revoked = String(data[i][6] || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
        if (revoked || isNaN(expires.getTime()) || now > expires) {
          sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
          return { success: false, message: 'Session expired' };
        }
        break;
      }
    }
    if (rowIndex === -1) return { success: false, message: 'Session not found' };
    sessionsSheet.getRange(rowIndex, 6).setValue(now);
    const user = getUserByEmail(email, ss);
    if (!user) return { success: false, message: 'User not found' };
    let initialData = null;
    try {
      initialData = getInitialDataForUser(user);
    } catch(e) {}
    return { success: true, user: user, initialData: initialData };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'validatePersistentSession');
    return { success: false, message: 'Session validation failed' };
  }
}
function revokePersistentSession(token, deviceId) {
  try {
    if (!token || !deviceId) return { success: false, message: 'Invalid session' };
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sessionsSheet = ensureSessionsSheet(ss);
    const data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
    for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (String(data[i][2]) === String(tokenHash) && String(data[i][1]) === String(deviceId)) {
        sessionsSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
        try {
          const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
          cache.put('sess_active_' + tokenHash, 'REVOKED', 21600);
        } catch(e) {}
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'Session not found' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'revokePersistentSession');
    return { success: false, message: 'Session revoke failed' };
  }
}
function validateSessionHeartbeat(token, deviceId) {
  try {
    if (!token || !deviceId) return { success: false, message: 'Invalid session params' };
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token);
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    let cachedStatus = null;
    try {
      cachedStatus = cache.get('sess_active_' + tokenHash);
    } catch(e) {}
    
    if (cachedStatus === 'VALID') {
      return { success: true };
    }
    if (cachedStatus === 'REVOKED') {
      return { success: false, message: 'Your session has been terminated because you logged in on another device or tab.' };
    }
    
    // Cache miss: verify in spreadsheet
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sessionsSheet = ensureSessionsSheet(ss);
    const data = sessionsSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const now = new Date();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][2]) === String(tokenHash) && String(data[i][1]) === String(deviceId)) {
        const expires = new Date(data[i][4]);
        const revoked = String(data[i][6] || '').toUpperCase() === 'TRUE';
        if (revoked || isNaN(expires.getTime()) || now > expires) {
          try {
            cache.put('sess_active_' + tokenHash, 'REVOKED', 21600);
          } catch(e) {}
          return { success: false, message: 'Session expired or revoked.' };
        }
        // Valid! Cache it
        try {
          cache.put('sess_active_' + tokenHash, 'VALID', 21600);
        } catch(e) {}
        return { success: true };
      }
    }
    
    // Not found in spreadsheet
    try {
      cache.put('sess_active_' + tokenHash, 'REVOKED', 21600);
    } catch(e) {}
    return { success: false, message: 'Session not found.' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'validateSessionHeartbeat');
    return { success: false, message: 'Session check failed: ' + e.message };
  }
}
function setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(sheet, headers, formNumber, updates) {
  if (!formNumber) return false;
  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const formIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
  if (formIdx === -1) return false;
  let rowIndex = -1;
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][formIdx]) === String(formNumber)) {
      rowIndex = i + 1;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (rowIndex === -1) return false;
  const row = data[rowIndex - 1];
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(updates, h)) {
      row[i] = updates[h];
    }
  });
  sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([row]);
  return true;
}
function getUnlockInfo(unlocksSheet, formNumber) {
  if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return { active: false, rows: [], expiry: null };
  const data = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const rows = [];
  let active = false;
  let expiry = null;
  const now = new Date();
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    if (String(data[i][0]) === String(formNumber)) {
      rows.push(i + 1);
      const exp = new Date(data[i][2]);
      if (!isNaN(exp.getTime()) && now <= exp) {
        active = true;
        if (!expiry || exp > expiry) expiry = exp;
      }
    }
  }
  return { active, rows, expiry };
}
function removeUnlockRows(unlocksSheet, formNumber) {
  if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return 0;
  const data = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  let deleted = 0;
  for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
    if (String(data[i][0]) === String(formNumber)) {
      unlocksSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      deleted++;
    }
  }
  return deleted;
}
function saveAppSettings(settings, user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      throw new Error('Permission denied');
    }
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    const current = getAppSettings();
    const diffEntries = Object.entries(settings).filter(([k, v]) => {
      const oldVal = current.hasOwnProperty(k) ? current[k] : undefined;
      const normOld = (typeof oldVal === 'string') ? oldVal : String(oldVal);
      const normNew = (typeof v === 'string') ? v : String(v);
      return normOld !== normNew;
    }).map(([k, v]) => {
      if (k === 'logo_url' || k === 'logoUrl') return 'logo_url updated';
      return `${k}=${typeof v === 'string' ? v : (v ? 'true' : 'false')}`;
    });
    const settingsData = Object.entries(settings).map(([k, v]) => {
      // Allow string values (e.g., session) or boolean values
      if (typeof v === 'string') return [k, v];
      return [k, v ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE'];
    });
    configSheet.getRange(2, 1, settingsData.length, 2).setValues(settingsData);
    try { logActivity('Save Settings', user, (diffEntries.length ? diffEntries.join(', ') : 'No changes')); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: 'Settings saved successfully' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveAppSettings');
    throw new Error('Failed to save settings: ' + e.message);
  }
}


function saveColumnWidths(widths, user) {
  try {
    if (typeof user === 'string') {
      try { user = JSON.parse(user); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      throw new Error('Permission denied');
    }
    
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    const data = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    // Find or add columnWidths row
    let found = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase().trim() === 'columnwidths') {
        configSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(JSON.stringify(widths));
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (!found) {
      configSheet.insertRowAfter(configSheet.getLastRow()); // Better than appendRow if there's trailing data
      configSheet.getRange(configSheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([['columnWidths', JSON.stringify(widths)]]);
    }
    
    try { logActivity('Save Column Widths', user, 'Default widths updated'); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: 'Column widths saved as default' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveColumnWidths');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

function saveSubjectsConfig(configData, user) {
  try {
    // Deserialize if stringified (fix for transmission issues)
    if (typeof user === 'string') {
      try { user = JSON.parse(user); } catch (e) {}
    }

    if (!user) {
      throw new Error('User authentication lost. Please reload and log in.');
    }
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      throw new Error('Permission denied');
    }
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SUBJECTS_CONFIG);
    sheet.clear();
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 11).setValues([['Class', 'Stream', 'Compulsory Subjects', 'Group1 Options', 'Group2 Options', 'Min Total', 'Max Total', 'G1 Min', 'G1 Max', 'G2 Min', 'G2 Max']])
      .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    
    const rows = Object.entries(configData).flatMap(([cls, streams]) =>
      Object.entries(streams).map(([stream, data]) => [
        cls,
        stream,
        data.compulsory.join(','),
        data.group1.join(','),
        data.group2.join(','),
        // --- CHANGED LINES START ---
        // Default to 5 for 11th/12th, and 6 for 8th/9th/10th, matching the screenshot
        (data.minTotal != null ? data.minTotal : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') ? 5 : 6)),
        // Default to 5 for 11th/12th, and 6 for 8th/9th/10th, matching the screenshot
        (data.maxTotal != null ? data.maxTotal : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') ? 5 : 6)),
        // Defaults based on class and stream (1 for General, 2 for Science, 3 for Humanities)
        (data.g1Min != null ? data.g1Min : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') ? (stream === 'Science' ? 1 : (stream === 'Humanities' ? 3 : 1)) : 1)),
        // Defaults based on stream (3 for Humanities, 1 for all else)
        (data.g1Max != null ? data.g1Max : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') && stream === 'Humanities' ? 3 : 1)),
        // Default to 1 instead of 0
        (data.g2Min != null ? data.g2Min : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') ? 1 : 0)),
        // Default to 1
        (data.g2Max != null ? data.g2Max : ((cls === '11th' || cls === '12th') ? 1 : 1))
        // --- CHANGED LINES END ---
      ])
    );

  if (rows.length > 0) {
    sheet.getRange(2, 1, rows.length, 11).setValues(rows);
  }
  try { logActivity('Save Subjects Config', user, JSON.stringify(configData)); } catch (e) {}
  return { success: true, message: 'Subjects configuration saved successfully' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveSubjectsConfig');
    throw new Error('Failed to save subjects config: ' + e.message);
  }
}

// =================================================================
// API FUNCTIONS (APPLICATION SAVING & PDF)
// =================================================================
/**
 * [MODIFIED] Added new email templates for Provisional and Full admission.
 */
function convertProvisionalToFull(payload) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const { formData, fileData, oldPhotoUrl, deletePhoto, user, status } = payload || {};
    if (!formData) throw new Error('Missing form data');
    if (status !== 'Submitted') throw new Error('Full admission upgrade requires final submission');
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (!sheet) throw new Error('Admissions sheet not found');
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let headers = data[0] || sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    let currentSession = '';
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
        if (String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase() === 'session') {
          currentSession = configData[i][1] || '';
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!currentSession) currentSession = '2025-26';
    const normalized = headers.map(h => String(h || '').trim());
    const sessionIdxs = normalized.map((h, i) => ({ h: h.toLowerCase(), i })).filter(x => x.h === 'session').map(x => x.i);
    if (sessionIdxs.length === 0) {
      sheet.insertColumnAfter(sheet.getLastColumn());
      const newLastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
      sheet.getRange(1, newLastCol).setValue('Session');
    } else {
      const firstIdx = sessionIdxs[0] + 1;
      sheet.getRange(1, firstIdx).setValue('Session');
      for (let k = 1; k < sessionIdxs.length; k++) {
        sheet.getRange(1, sessionIdxs[k] + 1).setValue('Session_DUP');
      }
    }
    headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const statusIdx = headers.indexOf('Status');
    const classIdx = headers.indexOf('Admission sought for class');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    const formNumberIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const sessionIdx = headers.indexOf('Session');
    const adm11Idx = headers.indexOf('Admission Type (Class 11th)');
    const adm12Idx = headers.indexOf('Admission Type (Class 12th)');
    const reason11Idx = headers.indexOf('Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)');
    const reason12Idx = headers.indexOf('Reason for Provisional (Class 12th)');
    const lockStatusIdx = headers.indexOf('Lock Status');
    const rejectedAtIdx = headers.indexOf('Rejected At');
    const submissionAttemptsIdx = headers.indexOf('Submission Attempts');
    const lastSubmissionAtIdx = headers.indexOf('Last Submission At');
    const originalFormNumber = String(formData['Form Number'] || '').trim();
    if (!originalFormNumber) throw new Error('Original form number missing');
    const formNumbers = data.slice(1).map(r => String(r[formNumberIdx] || '').trim());
    let originalRowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][formNumberIdx] || '').trim() === originalFormNumber) {
        originalRowIndex = i;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (originalRowIndex === -1) throw new Error('Original provisional application not found');
    const originalRow = data[originalRowIndex];
    const originalStatus = String(originalRow[statusIdx] || '');
    if (originalStatus !== 'Submitted') throw new Error('Only submitted provisional forms can be upgraded');
    const originalAdmType = (originalRow[adm11Idx] || originalRow[adm12Idx] || '').toString().trim();
    const originalReason = (originalRow[reason11Idx] || originalRow[reason12Idx] || '').toString().trim();
    if (originalAdmType !== 'Provisional' || (originalReason !== 'Awaiting Result' && originalReason !== 'Reappear Candidate')) {
      throw new Error('This application is not eligible for upgrade');
    }
    const originalEmail = String(originalRow[emailIdx] || formData['Email Address'] || user?.email || '').trim();
    if (user && user.role === 'Student' && originalEmail && String(user.email).toLowerCase() !== originalEmail.toLowerCase()) {
      throw new Error('Permission denied');
    }
    const originalSession = String(originalRow[sessionIdx] || formData['Session'] || currentSession || '').trim();
    const normalizedSession = String(originalSession || currentSession || '').trim();
    const emailLower = String(originalEmail || '').toLowerCase();
    const hasFullInSession = data.slice(1).some(row => {
      const rowEmail = String(row[emailIdx] || '').toLowerCase();
      const rowSession = String(row[sessionIdx] || '').trim();
      const rowStatus = String(row[statusIdx] || '');
      const rowAdm = (row[adm11Idx] || row[adm12Idx] || '').toString().trim();
      return rowEmail === emailLower && rowSession === normalizedSession && rowStatus === 'Submitted' && rowAdm === 'Full';
    });
    if (hasFullInSession) throw new Error(`Only one full admission is allowed in session ${normalizedSession}.`);

    // Use the same form number for upgrade instead of adding "p" suffix
    let newFormNumber = originalFormNumber;

    const classSought = formData['Admission sought for class'] || originalRow[classIdx] || '';
    formData['Form Number'] = newFormNumber;
    formData['Status'] = 'Submitted';
    const timestampIdx = headers.indexOf('Timestamp');
    formData['Timestamp'] = (timestampIdx !== -1 && originalRow[timestampIdx]) ? originalRow[timestampIdx] : new Date();
    formData['Session'] = normalizedSession || currentSession;
    if (lockStatusIdx !== -1) formData['Lock Status'] = 'Locked';
    if (rejectedAtIdx !== -1 && !formData['Rejected At']) formData['Rejected At'] = originalRow[rejectedAtIdx] || '';
    if (submissionAttemptsIdx !== -1) {
      let attempts = parseInt(originalRow[submissionAttemptsIdx] || 0, 10);
      if (isNaN(attempts)) attempts = 0;
      formData['Submission Attempts'] = attempts + 1;
    }
    if (lastSubmissionAtIdx !== -1) formData['Last Submission At'] = new Date();
    if (classSought === '11th') formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] = 'Full';
    if (classSought === '12th') formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] = 'Full';
    if (!formData['Admission sought for class'] && originalRow[classIdx]) {
      formData['Admission sought for class'] = originalRow[classIdx];
    }
    if (user && user.role === 'Admin') {
      formData['Email Address'] = originalEmail;
    } else {
      formData['Email Address'] = originalEmail || user?.email || '';
    }
    let createdPhotoId = null;
    const normalizedFileData = normalizeImageFileData(fileData);
    if (normalizedFileData && normalizedFileData.base64Data) {
      const blob = Utilities.newBlob(
        Utilities.base64Decode(normalizedFileData.base64Data),
        normalizedFileData.mimeType,
        normalizedFileData.fileName
      );
      let rootFolder;
      try {
        rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
      } catch (e) {
        throw new Error('Storage folder misconfigured. Please set a valid rootFolderId in script properties.');
      }
      const photoFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('Student Photos');
      const photoFolder = photoFolders.hasNext() ? photoFolders.next() : rootFolder.createFolder('Student Photos');
      const studentName = formData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || 'unknown';
      const cleanName = studentName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]/g, '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
      const cleanClass = String(classSought || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '');
      const ext = normalizedFileData.fileName.split('.').pop();
      const fileName = `${newFormNumber}_${cleanName}_${cleanClass}`;
      const photoFile = photoFolder.createFile(blob).setName(`${fileName}.${ext}`);
      photoFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
      formData['Student Photo'] = photoFile.getUrl();
      createdPhotoId = photoFile.getId();
    } else if (oldPhotoUrl && !deletePhoto) {
      formData['Student Photo'] = oldPhotoUrl;
    } else if (deletePhoto) {
      formData['Student Photo'] = '';
    }
    const validationErrors = validateFormForSubmission(formData, classSought, user);
    if (validationErrors.length > 0) {
      const maint = validationErrors.find(e => e === MAINTENANCE_MESSAGE);
      if (maint) throw new Error(maint);
      throw new Error(`Missing required fields: ${validationErrors.join(', ')}. Please fill all necessary fields before submission.`);
    }
    const normalizedHeaders = headers.map(h => String(h || '').trim());
    const rowData = normalizedHeaders.map((h, i) => {
      const incoming = formData[h];
      const existing = (originalRow && i < originalRow.length) ? originalRow[i] : '';
      
      // [FIX] Preserve Class Roll No if incoming is empty/missing but existing exists
      if (h === 'Class Roll No' && (incoming === '' || incoming === undefined || incoming === null) && existing) {
        return existing;
      }
      
      // Priority 1: Payload data
      if (formData.hasOwnProperty(h)) return incoming;
      // Priority 2: Existing record data
      return existing;
    });
    
    // Archive original provisional row before updating
    try {
      let archiveSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.PROVISIONAL_ARCHIVE);
      if (!archiveSheet) {
        archiveSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.PROVISIONAL_ARCHIVE);
        archiveSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, headers.length + 1).setValues([[...headers, 'ArchivedAt']])
          .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
      }
      const archivedRow = [...originalRow];
      // Pad archivedRow to match headers length if necessary
      while (archivedRow.length < headers.length) archivedRow.push('');
      archivedRow.push(new Date()); // Add ArchivedAt timestamp
      archiveSheet.appendRow(archivedRow);
    } catch (archiveErr) {
      logError(archiveErr, 'archiveProvisionalRow');
    }

    // Update existing row instead of appending new row
    const rowIndex = originalRowIndex + 1;
    sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, rowData.length).setValues([rowData]);

    // Release global lock early to prevent timeouts during PDF generation and emailing
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch(e) {}

    let generatedPdfUrl = '';
    let pdfDownloadUrl = '';
    let pdfId = '';
    let pdfBlob = null;
    try {
      const pdfResult = generatePdf(formData, false);
      generatedPdfUrl = pdfResult.pdfUrl;
      pdfDownloadUrl = pdfResult.pdfDownloadUrl;
      pdfId = pdfResult.pdfId;
      pdfBlob = pdfResult.pdfBlob;
    } catch (pdfErr) {
      // For upgrade, don't delete the row if PDF fails, as it's an update of an existing record
      if (createdPhotoId) {
        try { DriveApp.getFileById(createdPhotoId).setTrashed(true); } catch (photoErr) {}
      }
      throw pdfErr;
    }
    if (pdfIdx !== -1) {
      const _miniLock = LockService.getScriptLock();
      _miniLock.waitLock(30000);
      try {
        sheet.getRange(rowIndex, pdfIdx + 1).setValue(generatedPdfUrl);
      } finally {
        try { _miniLock.releaseLock(); } catch(e) {}
      }
    }
    try {
      cleanupOldPdfs(newFormNumber, pdfId, 'upgrade');
    } catch (cleanupErr) {
      logError(cleanupErr, 'cleanupOldPdfs-upgrade');
    }
    if (user && user.role === 'Student') {
      const studentName = formData["Student's Name (as per school records)"];
      const formNum = formData['Form Number'];
      const subject = `Full Admission Submitted - Form #${formNum}`;
      const emailBody = `Dear ${studentName} (Form No. ${formNum}),\n\n` +
        `Your provisional admission has been upgraded to a Full Admission successfully.\n\n` +
        `Next Steps:\n\n` +
        `1. Download Your Form: Use this link to download your admission form: ${generatedPdfUrl}\n` +
        `2. Complete the Form: Print the downloaded form. Review it carefully, obtain the necessary signatures (yours and your parent/guardian's).\n` +
        `3. Gather Documents: Prepare the required supporting documents: Discharge and Character Certificates (original); one photocopy each of marks sheet(s), bank passbook, ration card (if applicable), Aadhaar card, and category certificate (if applicable).\n` +
        `4. Submit & Pay Fee: Submit the signed form, required documents, and the admission fee to the admissions section at HSS Shangus.\n\n` +
        `Best regards,\nAdms & Exams Team, HSS Shangus`;
      const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
        title: 'Full Admission Submitted',
        preheader: `Form #${formNum} submitted successfully`,
        greeting: `Dear ${studentName},`,
        intro: 'Your provisional admission has been upgraded to a <strong>Full Admission</strong>. Please follow these next steps:',
        steps: [
          '<strong>Download</strong> your admission form using the button below.',
          '<strong>Print</strong>, review, and sign (student and parent/guardian).',
          '<strong>Gather</strong> required supporting documents and photocopies.',
          '<strong>Submit</strong> signed form, documents, and fee at HSS Shangus.'
        ],
        footer: 'For any queries, contact the admissions office.',
        ctaLabel: 'Download Admission Form',
        ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || generatedPdfUrl
      });
      const safeEmail = String(originalEmail || user.email || '').trim();
      const mailOpts = { htmlBody: htmlBody, emailAction: 'Upgrade Provisional to Full', formNumber: String(formNum) };
      if (pdfBlob) mailOpts.attachments = [pdfBlob];
      if (safeEmail) sendEmailSafe(safeEmail, subject, emailBody, mailOpts);
    }
    try { logActivity('Upgrade Provisional to Full', user, `From ${originalFormNumber} to ${newFormNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
    const updatedRow = sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
    const savedAppData = headers.reduce((obj, h, i) => {
      obj[h] = updatedRow[i] instanceof Date ? updatedRow[i].toISOString() : updatedRow[i];
      return obj;
    }, {});
    savedAppData.lastModified = new Date().toISOString();
    return {
      success: true,
      message: 'Full admission submitted successfully! A new PDF has been generated and is available in your dashboard.',
      applicationData: savedAppData
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'convertProvisionalToFull');
    throw new Error('Upgrade failed: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
function saveApplicationData(payload) {
  if (payload && payload.isUpgradeFlow) {
    return convertProvisionalToFull(payload);
  }
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const { formData, fileData, oldPhotoUrl, deletePhoto, user, status } = payload;
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let headers = data[0] || sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    // Ensure Session column exists and set current session from Config
    try {
      const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
      let currentSession = '';
      if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
          if (String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase() === 'session') {
            currentSession = configData[i][1] || '';
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!currentSession) currentSession = '2025-26';
      formData['Session'] = currentSession;
      // Normalize and de-duplicate 'Session' header
      let normalized = headers.map(h => String(h || '').trim());
      const sessionIdxs = normalized.map((h, i) => ({ h: h.toLowerCase(), i })).filter(x => x.h === 'session').map(x => x.i);
      if (sessionIdxs.length === 0) {
        sheet.insertColumnAfter(sheet.getLastColumn());
        const newLastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
        sheet.getRange(1, newLastCol).setValue('Session');
      } else {
        const firstIdx = sessionIdxs[0] + 1;
        sheet.getRange(1, firstIdx).setValue('Session');
        for (let k = 1; k < sessionIdxs.length; k++) {
          sheet.getRange(1, sessionIdxs[k] + 1).setValue('Session_DUP');
        }
      }
      headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    } catch (sessErr) { logError(sessErr, 'ensureSessionColumn'); }
    headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const statusIdx = headers.indexOf('Status');
    const classIdx = headers.indexOf('Admission sought for class');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    const lastEditedIdx = headers.indexOf('Last Edited');
    const formNumberIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const lockStatusIdx = headers.indexOf('Lock Status');
    const rejectedAtIdx = headers.indexOf('Rejected At');
    const submissionAttemptsIdx = headers.indexOf('Submission Attempts');
    const lastSubmissionAtIdx = headers.indexOf('Last Submission At');
    const timestampIdx = headers.indexOf('Timestamp');
    let isNew = !formData['Form Number'];
    const classSought = formData['Admission sought for class'] || '';
    let formNumber = formData['Form Number'];
    if (classSought && !isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      const appSettings = getAppSettings();
      if (appSettings['allow_' + classSought] === false) {
        throw new Error(MAINTENANCE_MESSAGE);
      }
    }

    // [NEW] Data Cleanup: Clear fields not relevant to the selected class
    // This ensures that if a user switches from 9th to 10th, 9th-specific data is wiped.
    if (classSought) {
      const formStructure = getFormStructure();
      formStructure.forEach(field => {
        // Skip cleaning if no specific classes are defined (applies to all)
        if (!field.classes || field.classes.trim() === '') return;
        
        const allowedClasses = field.classes.split(',').map(c => c.trim());
        // If the current class is NOT in the allowed list for this field
        if (!allowedClasses.includes(classSought)) {
          // Clear the data from formData
          // We check if the property exists to avoid creating keys for fields that weren't even there
          if (formData.hasOwnProperty(field.fieldName)) {
             formData[field.fieldName] = '';
          }
        }
      });
    }
    const isDraft = status === 'Draft';
    const isEditMode = formData['Status'] === 'Submitted' && isDraft; // Draft save in edit mode
    // Disallow editing drafts from a different session when admin has changed session
    try {
      const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
      let currentSession = '';
      if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
          if (String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase() === 'session') {
            currentSession = configData[i][1] || '';
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (!currentSession) currentSession = '2025-26';
      const isNewSubmissionWithoutForm = !formNumber;
      if (!isNew && isDraft && !isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
        const originalSession = formData['Session'] || '';
        if (originalSession && originalSession !== currentSession) {
          throw new Error(`Editing is disabled for drafts from session ${originalSession}. Please start a new application for session ${currentSession}.`);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      if (String(e.message || '').indexOf('disabled for drafts') >= 0) throw e;
      // otherwise ignore
    }
    // Try to reuse existing Draft row in this session for this account BEFORE duplicate check
    try {
      const sessionHeaderIdx = headers.indexOf('Session');
      const timestampIdx = headers.indexOf('Timestamp');
      if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user) && sessionHeaderIdx !== -1) {
        const currentSessionVal = formData['Session'];
        const candidates = data.slice(1).filter(row =>
          String(row[emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === String(user.email).toLowerCase() &&
          (row[statusIdx] === 'Draft' || row[statusIdx] === 'Submitted') &&
          (row[sessionHeaderIdx] || currentSessionVal) === currentSessionVal
        );
        // Prefer a Draft row if available; otherwise latest by timestamp
        let target = candidates.find(r => r[statusIdx] === 'Draft');
        if (!target && candidates.length > 0) {
          target = candidates.sort((a,b) => new Date(b[timestampIdx]||0) - new Date(a[timestampIdx]||0))[0];
        }
        if (target && target[statusIdx] === 'Draft') {
          const existingFormNo = target[formNumberIdx];
          if (existingFormNo) {
            formData['Form Number'] = existingFormNo;
            isNew = false;
            formNumber = existingFormNo;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* ignore */ }
    let existingRow = null;
    let rowIndex = -1;
    if (!isNew && formNumber) {
      for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (String(data[i][formNumberIdx]) === String(formNumber)) {
          existingRow = data[i];
          rowIndex = i - 1; // 0-based index of row in dataRows
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    let existingStatus = existingRow ? String(existingRow[statusIdx] || '') : '';
    if (status === 'Submitted' && !isUserAnyAdmin(user) && !isNew && existingStatus === 'Submitted') {
      const unlockInfo = getUnlockInfo(unlocksSheet, formNumber);
      if (!unlockInfo.active) {
        throw new Error('Edit period has expired');
      }
    }
    if (lockStatusIdx !== -1) {
      formData['Lock Status'] = status === 'Submitted' ? 'Locked' : 'Unlocked';
    }
    if (existingRow && rejectedAtIdx !== -1 && !formData['Rejected At']) {
      formData['Rejected At'] = existingRow[rejectedAtIdx];
    }
    if (existingRow && submissionAttemptsIdx !== -1 && status !== 'Submitted') {
      formData['Submission Attempts'] = existingRow[submissionAttemptsIdx] || 0;
    }
    if (!existingRow && submissionAttemptsIdx !== -1 && status !== 'Submitted') {
      formData['Submission Attempts'] = 0;
    }
    if (existingRow && lastSubmissionAtIdx !== -1 && status !== 'Submitted') {
      formData['Last Submission At'] = existingRow[lastSubmissionAtIdx] || '';
    }
    if (status === 'Submitted' && submissionAttemptsIdx !== -1) {
      let attempts = existingRow ? parseInt(existingRow[submissionAttemptsIdx] || 0, 10) : 0;
      if (isNaN(attempts)) attempts = 0;
      formData['Submission Attempts'] = attempts + 1;
      if (lastSubmissionAtIdx !== -1) formData['Last Submission At'] = new Date();
    }
    // Check edit mode limits
    // Check edit mode limits
    // [Modified] Admins bypass all edit mode restrictions (expiry, draft limits)
    if (isEditMode && !isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) throw new Error('No unlock record found');
      const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      let unlockRow = -1;
      for (let j = 1; j < unlocksData.length; j++) {
        if (unlocksData[j][0] == formNumber) {
          unlockRow = j + 1;
          const expTime = new Date(unlocksData[j][2]).getTime();
          const now = new Date().getTime();
          if (now > expTime) throw new Error('Edit period has expired');
          const currentDraftCount = unlocksData[j][5] || 0;
          if (currentDraftCount >= MAX_DRAFTS_IN_EDIT_MODE) throw new Error(`Maximum ${MAX_DRAFTS_IN_EDIT_MODE} draft saves reached in edit mode`);
          // Increment draft count
          unlocksSheet.getRange(unlockRow, 6).setValue(currentDraftCount + 1);
          break;
        }
      }
      if (unlockRow === -1) throw new Error('Unlock record not found');
    }
    // Enforce one application per SESSION per student (Draft or Submitted)
    const normalizedHeaders = headers.map(h => String(h || '').trim());
    const sessionIdx = normalizedHeaders.findIndex(h => h.toLowerCase() === 'session');
    const currentSession = formData['Session'];
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      const existingInSession = data.slice(1).filter(row =>
        String(row[emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === String(user.email).toLowerCase() &&
        (row[statusIdx] === 'Draft' || row[statusIdx] === 'Submitted') &&
        row[sessionIdx] === currentSession &&
        row[formNumberIdx] != formNumber
      );
      if (existingInSession.length > 0) {
        throw new Error(`Only one application is allowed per account in session ${currentSession}.`);
      }
    }
    
    // Handle photo upload
    const normalizedFileData = normalizeImageFileData(fileData);
    if (normalizedFileData && normalizedFileData.base64Data) {
      const blob = Utilities.newBlob(
        Utilities.base64Decode(normalizedFileData.base64Data),
        normalizedFileData.mimeType,
        normalizedFileData.fileName
      );
      let rootFolder;
      try {
        rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
      } catch (e) {
        throw new Error('Storage folder misconfigured. Please set a valid rootFolderId in script properties.');
      }
      const photoFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('Student Photos');
      const photoFolder = photoFolders.hasNext() ? photoFolders.next() : rootFolder.createFolder('Student Photos');
      const formNum = formData['Form Number'] || user.email.split('@')[0];
      const cleanName = String(formData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
      const cleanClass = String(formData['Admission sought for class'] || '').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_');
      const ext = normalizedFileData.fileName.split('.').pop();
      const fileName = `${formNum}_${cleanName}_${cleanClass}`;
      const photoFile = photoFolder.createFile(blob).setName(`${fileName}.${ext}`);
      photoFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
      formData['Student Photo'] = photoFile.getUrl();
      if (oldPhotoUrl && !isNew) {
        try {
          const m1 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
          const m2 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
          const oldId = (m1 && m1[1]) ? m1[1] : (m2 && m2[1]) ? m2[1] : '';
          if (oldId) { try { DriveApp.getFileById(oldId).setTrashed(true); } catch (err) {} }
        } catch (e) {
          Logger.log('Could not delete old photo: ' + e.message);
        }
      }
      try { if (!isNew) cleanupOldPhotos(formNumber || formData['Form Number'], photoFile.getId()); } catch (e) {}
    } else if (oldPhotoUrl && !isNew && !deletePhoto) {
      formData['Student Photo'] = oldPhotoUrl;
      try {
        const m1 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const m2 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const keepId = (m1 && m1[1]) ? m1[1] : (m2 && m2[1]) ? m2[1] : '';
        cleanupOldPhotos(formNumber || formData['Form Number'], keepId);
      } catch (e) {}
    } else if (deletePhoto && oldPhotoUrl && !isNew) {
      try {
        const m1 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const m2 = String(oldPhotoUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const oldId = (m1 && m1[1]) ? m1[1] : (m2 && m2[1]) ? m2[1] : '';
        if (oldId) { try { DriveApp.getFileById(oldId).setTrashed(true); } catch (err) {} }
      } catch (e) {
        Logger.log('Could not delete photo on request: ' + e.message);
      }
      formData['Student Photo'] = '';
      try { cleanupOldPhotos(formNumber || formData['Form Number'], null); } catch (e) {}
    }
    // Server-side validation for submission (drafts skip validation)
    if (status === 'Submitted') {
      const validationErrors = validateFormForSubmission(formData, classSought, user);
      if (validationErrors.length > 0) {
        const maint = validationErrors.find(e => e === MAINTENANCE_MESSAGE);
        if (maint) throw new Error(maint);
        throw new Error(`Missing required fields: ${validationErrors.join(', ')}. Please fill all necessary fields before submission.`);
      }
    }
    if (existingRow && timestampIdx !== -1 && existingRow[timestampIdx]) {
      formData['Timestamp'] = existingRow[timestampIdx];
    } else {
      formData['Timestamp'] = new Date();
    }
    try {
      if (user && user.role === 'Admin') {
        let originalEmail = String(formData['Email Address'] || '').trim();
        if (!originalEmail && !isNew) {
          const formNumbersCol = sheet.getRange(2, formNumberIdx + 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
          const emailCol = sheet.getRange(2, emailIdx + 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
          const idx = formNumbersCol.indexOf(formData['Form Number']);
          if (idx > -1) originalEmail = String(emailCol[idx] || '').trim();
        }
        if (!originalEmail) {
          // Fallback to the explicit form field if present
          originalEmail = String(formData['E-mail ID'] || '').trim();
        }
        if (originalEmail) {
          formData['Email Address'] = originalEmail;
        } else {
          // As a last resort, do NOT overwrite with admin email
          // Keep whatever is already in the sheet or payload
          formData['Email Address'] = formData['Email Address'] || '';
        }
      } else {
        formData['Email Address'] = user.email;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      // If anything goes wrong, avoid changing the email
      formData['Email Address'] = formData['Email Address'] || user.email || '';
    }
    if (!isNew) {
      if (rowIndex === -1) {
        const emailNumbers = sheet.getRange(2, emailIdx + 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        rowIndex = emailNumbers.lastIndexOf(user.email);
      }
      // Re-read existingStatus if rowIndex changed
      if (rowIndex !== -1 && statusIdx !== -1) {
        // We but already have existingStatus from the beginning of the function if it was found by Form Number
        // Let's ensure it's up to date if we just found it by email
        existingStatus = String(sheet.getRange(rowIndex + 2, statusIdx + 1).getValue()).trim();
      }
    }

    formData['Status'] = status; // 'Draft' or 'Submitted'
    if (isDraft && !isNew) {
      formData['Last Edited'] = new Date();
    }
    if (isNew) {
      formData['Form Number'] = generateFormNumber();
    }
    const rowData = normalizedHeaders.map((h, i) => {
      const incoming = formData[h];
      const existing = (existingRow && i < existingRow.length) ? existingRow[i] : '';
      
      // [FIX] Preserve Class Roll No if resubmitted without one
      if (h === 'Class Roll No' && (incoming === '' || incoming === undefined || incoming === null) && existing) {
        return existing;
      }
      
      if (formData.hasOwnProperty(h)) return incoming;
      return existing;
    });
    if (isNew) {
      sheet.appendRow(rowData);
      rowIndex = sheet.getLastRow();
    } else {
      if (rowIndex === -1) throw new Error('Application not found');
      rowIndex += 2; // Adjust for header and 0-index
      sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, headers.length).setValues([rowData]);
    }
    // Release the main script lock early to prevent timeouts during PDF/email generation
