    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) { }

    // --- PDF LOGIC ---
    let pdfUrl = null;
    let isEdit = false;
    if (status === 'Submitted') { // Only on FINAL submit
      isEdit = isEditMode;
      let logAction = isEdit ? 'Application Update' : 'Application Submission';
      if (existingStatus === 'Rejected') {
        logAction = 'Application Resubmitted';
      }
      try { logActivity(logAction, user, `Form: ${formData['Form Number']}; Class: ${classSought}`); } catch (e) { }
      const { pdfBlob, pdfUrl: generatedPdfUrl, pdfDownloadUrl, pdfId } = generatePdf(formData, isEdit);
      pdfUrl = generatedPdfUrl;
      // Update the sheet with PDF URL using a short, scoped lock
      if (pdfIdx !== -1) {
        const _miniLock = LockService.getScriptLock();
        _miniLock.waitLock(30000);
        try {
          sheet.getRange(rowIndex, pdfIdx + 1).setValue(pdfUrl);
        } finally {
          try { _miniLock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) { }
        }
      }
      // Email PDF to user if student
      if (user.role === 'Student') {
        // [MODIFIED] New email templates (HTML + plaintext fallback)
        const admTypeRaw = (classSought === '11th') ? formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] : (classSought === '12th') ? formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] : '';
        const admType = String(admTypeRaw || '').trim() || 'Full';
        const formNum = formData['Form Number'];
        const studentName = formData["Student's Name (as per school records)"];
        let subject = '';
        let emailBody = '';
        let htmlBody = '';
        if (admType === 'Provisional') {
          subject = `Provisional Admission Submitted - Form #${formNum}`;
          emailBody = `Dear ${studentName} (Form No. ${formNum}),\n\n` +
            `Congratulations! Your online Provisional Admission Form has been submitted successfully. We are excited to welcome you provisionally to our institution.\n\n` +
            `Next Steps for Provisional Admission:\n\n` +
            `1. Download Your Form: Please find your provisional admission form attached below.\n` +
            `2. Complete the Form: Print the downloaded form. Review it carefully, sign the student's declaration, and attach a photocopy of your recent exam admit card (or marks card if available).\n` +
            `3. Submit the Form: Submit the completed form and attached photocopy to the admissions section at HSS Shangus at your earliest convenience.\n` +
            `4. Confirmation: Final admission is subject to meeting eligibility criteria upon declaration of results.\n\n` +
            `If you have any questions, please feel free to contact our admissions office.\n\n` +
            `Best regards,\nAdms & Exams Team, HSS Shangus`;
          htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
            title: 'Provisional Admission Submitted',
            preheader: `Form #${formNum} submitted successfully`,
            greeting: `Dear ${studentName},`,
            intro: 'Your <strong>Provisional Admission Form</strong> has been submitted successfully. Please follow these next steps:',
            steps: [
              '<strong>Download</strong> your provisional form using the button below.',
              '<strong>Print</strong> and sign the student\'s declaration.',
              '<strong>Attach</strong> a photocopy of your recent exam admit card or marks card.',
              '<strong>Submit</strong> the completed form and attachment at HSS Shangus admissions section.'
            ],
            footer: 'Final admission is subject to eligibility after results are declared.',
            ctaLabel: 'Download Provisional Form',
            ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || pdfUrl
          });
        } else { // Full Admission
          subject = `Admission Form Submitted - Form #${formNum}`;
          emailBody = `Dear ${studentName} (Form No. ${formNum}),\n\n` +
            `Congratulations! Your online Admission Form has been submitted successfully.\n\n` +
            `Next Steps:\n\n` +
            `1. Download Your Form: Use this link to download your admission form: ${pdfUrl}\n` +
            `2. Complete the Form: Print the downloaded form. Review it carefully, obtain the necessary signatures (yours and your parent/guardian's).\n` +
            `3. Gather Documents: Prepare the required supporting documents: Discharge and Character Certificates (original); one photocopy each of marks sheet(s), bank passbook, ration card (if applicable), Aadhaar card, and category certificate (if applicable).\n` +
            `4. Submit & Pay Fee: Submit the signed form, required documents, and the admission fee to the admissions section at HSS Shangus.\n\n` +
            `Best regards,\nAdms & Exams Team, HSS Shangus`;
          htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
            title: 'Admission Form Submitted',
            preheader: `Form #${formNum} submitted successfully`,
            greeting: `Dear ${studentName},`,
            intro: 'Your <strong>Admission Form</strong> has been submitted successfully. Please follow these next steps:',
            steps: [
              '<strong>Download</strong> your admission form using the button below.',
              '<strong>Print</strong>, review, and sign (student and parent/guardian).',
              '<strong>Gather</strong> required supporting documents: Discharge and Character Certificates (original); one photocopy each of marks sheet(s), bank passbook, ration card, Aadhaar card, and category certificate (if applicable).',
              '<strong>Submit</strong> signed form, documents, and fee at HSS Shangus.'
            ],
            footer: 'For any queries, contact the admissions office.',
            ctaLabel: 'Download Admission Form',
            ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || pdfUrl
          });
        }
        if (isEdit) {
          subject = `[UPDATED] ${subject}`;
          const editNoticePlain = `Your admission form (Form No. ${formNum}) has been updated. The original instructions still apply. Download the updated form here: ${pdfUrl}\n\n`;
          emailBody = `Dear ${studentName},\n\n${editNoticePlain}` + emailBody;
          htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
            title: 'Admission Form Updated',
            preheader: `Form #${formNum} updated`,
            greeting: `Dear ${studentName},`,
            intro: 'Your <strong>admission form</strong> has been updated. The original instructions still apply. Please review the updated form and follow the steps below:',
            steps: (admType === 'Provisional') ? [
              '<strong>Download</strong> the updated provisional form using the button below.',
              '<strong>Print</strong> and sign the student\'s declaration.',
              '<strong>Attach</strong> a photocopy of your recent exam admit card or marks card.',
              '<strong>Submit</strong> the completed form and attachment at HSS Shangus admissions section.'
            ] : [
              '<strong>Download</strong> the updated admission form using the button below.',
              '<strong>Print</strong>, review, and sign (student and parent/guardian).',
              '<strong>Gather</strong> required documents and photocopies.',
              '<strong>Submit</strong> signed form, documents, and fee at HSS Shangus.'
            ],
            footer: 'For any queries, contact the admissions office.',
            ctaLabel: 'Download Updated Form',
            ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || pdfUrl
          });
        }
        const safeEmail = String(user.email || '').trim();
        const mailOpts = { htmlBody: htmlBody, emailAction: (isEdit ? 'Edit Submit PDF' : 'Submit PDF'), formNumber: String(formNumber) };
        if (pdfBlob) mailOpts.attachments = [pdfBlob];
        if (safeEmail) {
          const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(safeEmail, subject, emailBody, mailOpts);
          if (!emailResult.success) {
            console.warn('Form submission email failed:', emailResult.message);
          }
        }
      }
      
      try {
        if (unlocksSheet && unlocksSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
          removeUnlockRows(unlocksSheet, formNumber);
        }
      } catch (lockErr) { logError(lockErr, 'autoLockAfterSubmit'); }
      try {
        cleanupOldPdfs(formNumber, pdfId, isEdit);
      } catch (cleanupErr) { logError(cleanupErr, 'cleanupOldPdfs-after-submit'); }
      // Refetch the saved data to include PDF URL
      const updatedRow = sheet.getRange(rowIndex, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
      const savedAppData = headers.reduce((obj, h, i) => {
        obj[h] = updatedRow[i] instanceof Date ? updatedRow[i].toISOString() : updatedRow[i];
        return obj;
      }, {});
      savedAppData.lastModified = new Date().toISOString(); // Add timestamp
      return {
        success: true,
        message: isEdit ? 'Application updated and re-locked successfully. Updated PDF is available in your dashboard.' : 'Application submitted successfully! PDF is now available for download in your dashboard.',
        applicationData: savedAppData
      };
    }
    // Refetch the saved data to return the complete object
    const savedAppData = headers.reduce((obj, h) => {
        const val = formData[h];
        if (val instanceof Date) {
          obj[h] = val.toISOString();
        } else {
          obj[h] = val !== undefined && val !== null ? val : '';
        }
        return obj;
    }, {});
    savedAppData.lastModified = new Date().toISOString(); // Add timestamp
    try { logActivity('Draft Saved', user, `Form: ${formData['Form Number']}; Class: ${classSought}`); } catch (e) {}
    return {
      success: true,
      message: isEditMode ? `Application draft saved (Draft ${formData.draftCount + 1}/${MAX_DRAFTS_IN_EDIT_MODE}).` : `Application draft saved successfully!`,
      applicationData: savedAppData
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveApplicationData');
    throw new Error('Save failed: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
/**
 * [MODIFIED] Validates form, including new 'Declaration' field.
 */
function validateFormForSubmission(formData, classSought, user) {
  const errors = [];
  const formStructure = getFormStructure();
  const appSettings = getAppSettings();
  if (classSought && (!user || !isUserAnyAdmin(user))) {
    if (appSettings['allow_' + classSought] === false) {
      errors.push(MAINTENANCE_MESSAGE);
      return errors;
    }
  }
  const subjectsConfig = getSubjectsConfig();
  formStructure.forEach(field => {
    // 1. Check if field is relevant for this class
    const allowedClasses = field.classes.split(',').map(c => c.trim());
    if (allowedClasses.length > 0 && !allowedClasses.includes(classSought)) {
        return;
    }
    let isRequired = field.required;
    // 2. Check for dynamic required status
    if (field.fieldName === 'Type of Disability') {
        isRequired = formData['Whether Any Disability'] === 'Yes';
    } else if (field.fieldName === 'Type of scholarship received' || field.fieldName === 'Amount received (INR)') {
        isRequired = formData['Whether scholarship received in previous academic year'] === 'Yes';
    } else if (field.fieldName === 'Percentage Obtained in Vocational Subject') {
        isRequired = formData['Vocational subject in previous class'] === 'Yes';
    }
    // [MODIFIED] Check provisional/reappear logic
    if (field.fieldName === 'Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)') {
      isRequired = formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] === 'Provisional';
    } else if (field.fieldName === 'Reason for Provisional (Class 12th)') {
      isRequired = formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] === 'Provisional';
    } else if (field.fieldName === 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 10th)') {
      isRequired = formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] === 'Provisional' && formData['Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)'] === 'Reappear Candidate';
    } else if (field.fieldName === 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 11th)') {
      isRequired = formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] === 'Provisional' && formData['Reason for Provisional (Class 12th)'] === 'Reappear Candidate';
    } else if (field.fieldName.includes('Year of Passing')) {
      // If converting Provisional -> Full, Year of Passing becomes mandatory.
      isRequired = (formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] === 'Full' || formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] === 'Full');
    } else if (field.fieldName.includes('Year of Appearing')) {
      isRequired = (formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] === 'Provisional' || formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] === 'Provisional');
    } else if (field.fieldName.includes('Total Marks Obtained') || field.fieldName.includes('Total Max. Marks')) {
      const name = field.fieldName;
      const is11or12Marks = (name.includes('Class 10th') || name.includes('Class 11th'));
      if (is11or12Marks) {
        // Marks mandatory if Full admission
        isRequired = (formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] === 'Full' || formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] === 'Full');
      } else {
        // For Class 9th marks (required for Class 10th admissions), keep original required flag
        isRequired = field.required;
      }
    }
    
    if (isRequired) {
      const fieldValue = formData[field.fieldName] ? formData[field.fieldName].toString().trim() : '';
      if (!fieldValue) {
        errors.push(field.fieldName);
      } else {
        if ((field.fieldType === 'number' || field.fieldType === 'number_range') && field.options && field.options.indexOf('-') > -1) {
          const parts = field.options.split('-');
          if (parts.length === 2) {
            const min = parseFloat(parts[0]);
            let max = parseFloat(parts[1]);
            // [NEW] Dynamic Max Year: If it's a year field, set max to current year dynamically
            const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
            if (field.fieldName.toLowerCase().includes('year') && max < currentYear) {
              max = currentYear;
            }
            const num = parseFloat(fieldValue);
            if (!isNaN(num) && (num < min || num > max)) {
              errors.push(field.fieldName);
            }
          }
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'PIN code') {
          if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(fieldValue)) errors.push('PIN code');
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'Aadhar No.') {
          if (!/^\d{12}$/.test(fieldValue)) errors.push('Aadhar No.');
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'E-mail ID') {
          const EMAIL_REGEX = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
          if (!EMAIL_REGEX.test(fieldValue)) errors.push('E-mail ID');
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)' || field.fieldName === "Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)") {
          const isMobile = /^[6-9]\d{9}$/.test(fieldValue);
          if (!isMobile) errors.push(field.fieldName);
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'Board Registration No. (Class 10th)' || field.fieldName === 'Board Registration No. (Class 11th)' || field.fieldName === 'Board Registration No. (Class 9th)') {
          const REG_REGEX = /^(?:[A-Za-z]\d{11}|\d{16})$/;
          const reg = fieldValue.replace(/\s+/g, '');
          if (!REG_REGEX.test(reg)) errors.push(field.fieldName);
        }
        if (field.fieldName === 'Exam Roll Number of Class 10th' || field.fieldName === 'Exam Roll Number of Class 11th') {
          const roll = fieldValue.replace(/\s+/g, '');
          if (!/^\d{9}$/.test(roll)) errors.push(field.fieldName);
        }
      }
    }
    // 3. Subject-specific validation
    // Special handling for Reappear fields: fixed whitelist, independent of stream
    if (field.fieldType === 'checkbox_dynamic' && (field.fieldName === 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 10th)' || field.fieldName === 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 11th)')) {
        const selected = formData[field.fieldName] ? formData[field.fieldName].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
        // Only validate if this reappear field is required by mode (Provisional + Reappear)
        if (isRequired) {
          const allowed10 = ['English','Mathematics','Science','Social Studies','Urdu','Arabic','Hindi','Kashmiri','Healthcare','IT and ITES'];
          const allowed11 = ['General English','Physics','Chemistry','Biology','Mathematics','Urdu','Economics','Education','History','Political Science','Environmental Science','Physical Education','IT and ITES','Healthcare'];
          const allowed = field.fieldName === 'Subjects to Reappear (Class 10th)' ? allowed10 : allowed11;
          // Whitelist check
          const invalid = selected.filter(s => !allowed.includes(s));
          if (invalid.length > 0) {
            errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Invalid selections: ${invalid.join('/')})`);
          }
          // Count check
          if (selected.length === 0) {
            errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Reappear subjects must be selected)`);
          } else if (selected.length > 5) {
            errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Max 5 subjects allowed for reappear)`);
          }
        }
        return;
    }
    // Standard subject validation (stream-based numeric rules)
    if (field.fieldType === 'checkbox_dynamic' && field.required) {
        const stream = formData['Stream for Class 11th'] || formData['Stream opted in Class 11th'] || 'General';
        const targetClass = classSought;
        const config = subjectsConfig[targetClass]?.[stream];
        if (config) {
            const selectedSubjects = formData[field.fieldName] ? formData[field.fieldName].split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s) : [];
            const compulsoryCount = config.compulsory.length;
            const g1count = selectedSubjects.filter(s => config.group1.includes(s)).length;
            const g2count = selectedSubjects.filter(s => config.group2.includes(s)).length;
            const total = selectedSubjects.length;
            // Check compulsory subjects inclusion
            const allCompulsoryIncluded = config.compulsory.every(comp => selectedSubjects.includes(comp));
            if (!allCompulsoryIncluded) {
              errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Group A: compulsory subject(s) not selected)`);
            }
            // Group counts
            if (g1count < config.g1Min || g1count > config.g1Max) {
              errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Group B: select ${config.g1Min}-${config.g1Max} subjects)`);
            }
            const g2MinEff = (g1count === config.g1Max && config.g1Min < config.g1Max) ? 0 : config.g2Min;
            if (g2count < g2MinEff || g2count > config.g2Max) {
              errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Group C: select ${g2MinEff}-${config.g2Max} subjects)`);
            }
            // Total
            if (total > config.maxTotal) {
              // Provide a more helpful message with group counts
              errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Max ${config.maxTotal} subjects total. Group A: ${compulsoryCount}, Group B: ${g1count}, Group C: ${g2count}; Selected: ${total})`);
            }
            if (config.minTotal && total < config.minTotal) {
              errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (At least ${config.minTotal} subjects total. Group A: ${compulsoryCount}, Group B: ${g1count}, Group C: ${g2count}; Selected: ${total})`);
            }
            // Reappear subject check
            if (field.fieldName.includes('Subjects to Reappear')) {
                if (isRequired) { // Only if reappear is the selected mode
                    if (total > 5) {
                        errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Max 5 subjects allowed for reappear)`);
                    } else if (total === 0) {
                         errors.push(`${field.fieldName} (Reappear subjects must be selected)`);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
  });
  // [NEW] Check for Declaration
  if (formData['Declaration'] !== 'TRUE' && formData['Declaration'] !== 'true' && formData['Declaration'] !== true) {
     errors.push('Declaration');
  }
    // [NEW] Ensure photo is present for final submission
    if (!formData['Student Photo'] || String(formData['Student Photo']).trim() === '') {
      errors.push('Student Photo');
    }
  // Cross-field checks for mobile numbers
  try {
    const m = String(formData['Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)'] || '').trim();
    const pm = String(formData["Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)"] || '').trim();
    if (m && pm && m === pm) {
      errors.push('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
      errors.push("Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)");
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  // Validate PEN and APAAR formats if provided
  if (formData['PEN number (given by UDISE portal)']) {
    const pen = String(formData['PEN number (given by UDISE portal)']).replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (!/^\d{11}$/.test(pen)) errors.push('PEN number (must be 11 digits)');
  }
  if (formData['APAAR ID']) {
    const a = String(formData['APAAR ID']).replace(/\s+/g, '');
    if (!/^\d{12}$/.test(a)) errors.push('APAAR ID (must be 12 digits)');
  }
  // Cross-field marks sanity checks: obtained <= max for classes 8?12
  try {
    const pairs = [
      ['Total Marks Obtained in Class 8th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 8th'],
      ['Total Marks Obtained in Class 9th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 9th'],
      ['Total Marks Obtained in Class 10th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 10th'],
      ['Total Marks Obtained in Class 11th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 11th'],
      ['Total Marks Obtained in Class 12th', 'Total Max. Marks in Class 12th']
    ];
    pairs.forEach(([obtName, maxName]) => {
      const obtVal = parseFloat(String(formData[obtName] || '').trim());
      const maxVal = parseFloat(String(formData[maxName] || '').trim());
      if (!isNaN(obtVal) && !Number.isInteger(obtVal)) {
        errors.push(`${obtName} (must be a whole number)`);
      }
      if (!isNaN(maxVal) && !Number.isInteger(maxVal)) {
        errors.push(`${maxName} (must be a whole number)`);
      }
      if (!isNaN(obtVal) && !isNaN(maxVal) && obtVal > maxVal) {
        errors.push(`${obtName} (cannot exceed ${maxName})`);
      }
      // Enforce allowed max marks values configured by admin per class
      try {
        const clsKeyMap = {
          'Total Max. Marks in Class 8th': 'max_marks_allowed_8th',
          'Total Max. Marks in Class 9th': 'max_marks_allowed_9th',
          'Total Max. Marks in Class 10th': 'max_marks_allowed_10th',
          'Total Max. Marks in Class 11th': 'max_marks_allowed_11th',
          'Total Max. Marks in Class 12th': 'max_marks_allowed_12th'
        };
        const settingsKey = clsKeyMap[maxName];
        const raw = settingsKey ? appSettings[settingsKey] : '';
        if (raw) {
          const allowed = String(raw).split(',').map(s => parseFloat(String(s).trim())).filter(n => !isNaN(n));
          if (allowed.length > 0 && !isNaN(maxVal) && allowed.indexOf(maxVal) === -1) {
            errors.push(`${maxName} (allowed values: ${allowed.join('/')})`);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    });
  } catch (e) {}
  return [...new Set(errors)]; // Unique
}

function rejectApplication(formNumber, reason, adminUser) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(adminUser, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(adminUser)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    if (!sheet) throw new Error('Admissions sheet not found');
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let headers = data[0];
    headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
    const formIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const nameIdx = headers.indexOf("Student's Name (as per school records)");
    let row = -1;
    let email = '';
    let studentName = '';
    let studentMobile = '';
    let fatherMobile = '';
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][formIdx]) === String(formNumber)) {
        row = i + 1;
        email = String(data[i][emailIdx] || '').trim();
        studentName = String(data[i][nameIdx] || '').trim();
        const mobileIdx = headers.indexOf('Mobile Number (Student/WhatsApp)');
        const fatherMobileIdx = headers.indexOf("Father's Mobile No.");
        studentMobile = String(data[i][mobileIdx] || '').trim();
        fatherMobile = String(data[i][fatherMobileIdx] || '').trim();
        break;
      }
    }
    if (row === -1) throw new Error('Application not found');
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const rowData = [formNumber, now, expiry, adminUser.email, `Rejected: ${reason || ''}`.trim(), 0];
    if (!unlocksSheet) throw new Error('Unlocks sheet not found');
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let targetRow = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (String(unlocksData[i][0]) === String(formNumber)) { targetRow = i + 1; break; }
    }
    if (targetRow > 0) {
      unlocksSheet.getRange(targetRow, 1, 1, 6).setValues([rowData]);
    } else {
      unlocksSheet.appendRow(rowData);
    }
    setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(sheet, headers, formNumber, {
      'Rejected At': now,
      'Rejected By': adminUser.email,
      'Lock Status': 'Unlocked'
    });
    const subject = `Application #${formNumber} Rejected ? Action Required`;
    const plainBody = `Dear ${studentName || email},\n\nYour admission application (Form No. ${formNumber}) has been rejected by the admin.\nReason: ${reason || 'Not specified'}.\n\nYour form has been unlocked for editing for 24 hours. Please log in at https://sites.google.com/view/hssshangus/login, correct the issues, and resubmit.\n\nRegards,\nHSS Shangus Admissions`;
    const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
      title: 'Application Rejected ? Action Required',
      preheader: `Form #${formNumber} rejected; please edit and resubmit`,
      greeting: `Dear ${studentName || email},`,
      intro: `Your admission application (Form No. ${formNumber}) has been rejected by the admin.`,
      steps: [
        `Reason: <strong style="color:#b91c1c;">${reason || 'Not specified'}</strong>`,
        'Your form has been unlocked for editing for <strong>24 hours</strong>.',
        'Log in to the portal at https://sites.google.com/view/hssshangus/login, correct the errors, and resubmit.'
      ],
      footer: 'If you face issues editing, reply to this email with details.',
      themeColor: '#b91c1c'
    });
    const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(email, subject, plainBody, { htmlBody, emailAction: 'Reject Application', formNumber: String(formNumber), adminEmail: adminUser.email });
    if (!emailResult.success) {
      console.warn('Reject notification email failed:', emailResult.message);
    }
    
    // Send WhatsApp message to student and/or father
    const whatsappMessage = `HSS Shangus: Your admission application (Form No. ${formNumber}) has been rejected. Reason: ${reason || 'Not specified'}. Your form has been unlocked for 24 hours for editing. Please log in at https://sites.google.com/view/hssshangus/login, correct the issues, and resubmit. -HSS Shangus Admissions`;
    
    let whatsappResult1 = null;
    let whatsappResult2 = null;
    
    if (studentMobile) {
      whatsappResult1 = sendWhatsAppMessage(studentMobile, whatsappMessage, { 
        messageAction: 'Reject Application - Student', 
        formNumber: String(formNumber) 
      });
    }
    
    if (fatherMobile && fatherMobile !== studentMobile) {
      whatsappResult2 = sendWhatsAppMessage(fatherMobile, whatsappMessage, { 
        messageAction: 'Reject Application - Father', 
        formNumber: String(formNumber) 
      });
    }
    
    const waSuccess = (whatsappResult1 && whatsappResult1.success) || (whatsappResult2 && whatsappResult2.success) ? 'WhatsApp: Sent' : 'WhatsApp: Not Sent';
    try { logActivity('Reject Application', adminUser, `Form ${formNumber} - ${reason || ''} (Email: ${emailResult.success ? 'Sent' : 'Failed'}, ${waSuccess})`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: `Application #${formNumber} rejected and unlocked for 24 hours.` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'rejectApplication');
    throw new Error(`Reject failed: ${e.message}`);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}
/**
 * Generates a PDF from a Google Doc template.
 */

function generatePdf(appData, isEdit = false) {
  const classSought = appData['Admission sought for class'] || '';
  // [MODIFIED] Check both 11th and 12th adm types
  const admTypeRaw = (classSought === '11th') ? appData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] : (classSought === '12th') ? appData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] : '';
  const admType = String(admTypeRaw || '').trim() || 'Full';
  let templateId;
  
  // [NEW] Try to get template IDs from Config sheet first, then fallback to SCRIPT_PROPERTIES
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  try {
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
        // Robust key matching: remove underscores and spaces, lowercase
        const key = String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase().replace(/[_\s]/g, '');
        const val = String(configData[i][1]).trim();
        
        if (!val) continue;

        if (admType === 'Provisional' && key === 'templateprovisional') {
          templateId = val;
        } else if (admType !== 'Provisional' && key === 'templateregular') {
          templateId = val;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { logError(e, 'generatePdf.getConfigTemplates'); }

  // Choose the correct template if not found in Config
  if (!templateId) {
    if (admType === 'Provisional') {
      templateId = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.templateDocIdProvisional;
    } else {
      templateId = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.templateDocIdRegular;
    }
  }
  
  // [NEW] Diagnostic: Log template selection
  try {
    console.log(`Class: ${classSought}, AdmType: ${admType}, TemplateID: ${templateId}`);
  } catch (e) {}

  if (!templateId) {
    throw new Error('Could not determine PDF template.');
  }
  // Get folders (robust creation if missing)
  let rootFolder;
  try {
    rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error('Storage folder misconfigured. Please set a valid rootFolderId in script properties.');
  }
  const pdfFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('Admission Form PDFs');
  const pdfFolder = pdfFolders.hasNext() ? pdfFolders.next() : rootFolder.createFolder('Admission Form PDFs');
  // [FIXED] Use '_Temp_PDF_Work' ? a name that is distinct from 'Template Document Copies'
  // to avoid accidentally depositing temp copies inside the protected template folder.
  const tempFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('_Temp_PDF_Work');
  const tempFolder = tempFolders.hasNext() ? tempFolders.next() : rootFolder.createFolder('_Temp_PDF_Work');
  // Create a temporary copy of the template (fallback to a blank doc if access fails)
  let tempFile;
  let tempDoc;
  let usedFallbackTemplate = false;
  let templateError = '';

  // [MODIFIED] Name the temp doc with Form No and Candidate Name for easier recovery
  const formNum = appData['Form Number'] || 'DRAFT';
  const sName = appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || 'Candidate';
  const cleanSName = String(sName).replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]/g, '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
  const tempDocName = `Temp_Form_${formNum}_${cleanSName}`;

  try {
    // [MODIFIED] Explicitly check for templateId existence
    if (!templateId) throw new Error('Template ID is missing');
    
    tempFile = DriveApp.getFileById(templateId).makeCopy(tempDocName, tempFolder);
    // Try to open with DocumentApp, but handle permission errors
    try {
      tempDoc = DocumentApp.openById(tempFile.getId());
    } catch (docAppError) {
      // If DocumentApp fails, delete the copied file and use HTML fallback
      tempFile.setTrashed(true);
      throw new Error(`DocumentApp permission error: ${docAppError.message}`);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    templateError = e.message;
    logError(e, `generatePdf.templateAccess (ID: ${templateId})`);
    
    // Log to Admin Activity for visibility
    try {
      logActivity('PDF Template Error', 'System', `Failed to access template ${templateId}: ${e.message}`);
    } catch (err) {}

    // Create a simple HTML file as fallback instead of DocumentApp.create
    const htmlContent = `
      <!DOCTYPE html>
      <html>
      <head>
        <title>Admission Form - Fallback</title>
        <style>
          body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 20px; }
          .error { color: red; text-align: center; }
          .info { text-align: center; margin: 10px 0; }
        </style>
      </head>
      <body>
        <h1 class="error">[!] PDF GENERATION ERROR</h1>
        <p class="info">The system could not access the required ${admType} template.</p>
        <p class="info">Template ID attempted: ${templateId}</p>
        <p class="info">Script Executing As: ${Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail()}</p>
        <p class="info">Error: ${templateError}</p>
        <hr>
        <h2>Admission Form</h2>
        <p><strong>Name:</strong> ${appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || ''}</p>
        <p><strong>Class:</strong> ${classSought}</p>
        <p><strong>Admission Type:</strong> ${admType}</p>
      </body>
      </html>
    `;
    
    tempFile = tempFolder.createFile('Admission_Form_Fallback.html', htmlContent, MimeType.HTML);
    usedFallbackTemplate = true;
  }
  
  let body;
  if (usedFallbackTemplate) {
    // For HTML fallback, we don't have a DocumentApp body to modify
    // The HTML content is already complete in the file
    body = null;
  } else {
    body = tempDoc.getBody();
    // Minimal header for fallback template to avoid empty PDF
    if (usedFallbackTemplate) {
      try {
        const studentName = appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || '';
        body.appendParagraph('[!] PDF GENERATION ERROR').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        body.appendParagraph(`The system could not access the required ${admType} template.`).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        body.appendParagraph(`Template ID attempted: ${templateId}`).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        body.appendParagraph(`Script Executing As: ${Session.getEffectiveUser().getEmail()}`).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        body.appendParagraph(`Error: ${templateError}`).setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        body.appendParagraph('--------------------------------------------------').setAlignment(DocumentApp.HorizontalAlignment.CENTER);
        
        body.appendParagraph('Admission Form').setHeading(DocumentApp.ParagraphHeading.HEADING1);
        body.appendParagraph(`Name: ${studentName}`);
        body.appendParagraph(`Class: ${classSought}`);
        body.appendParagraph(`Admission Type: ${admType}`);
        body.appendParagraph('');
      } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
  // Replace using placeholders from Form_Structure
  const structSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.FORM_STRUCTURE);
  if (structSheet && structSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    const structData = structSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    structData.shift(); // Remove headers
    const UPPER_FIELDS = [
      "IFSC code"
    ];
    const PROPER_FIELDS = [
      "Student's Name (as per school records)",
      "Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)",
      "Mother's Name (as per school records)",
      "Name of Previous School (Class 8th)", 
      "Name of Previous School (Class 9th)", 
      "Name of Previous School (Class 10th)", 
      "Name of Previous School (Class 11th)",
      "Father's/Guardian's Occupation",
      // Note: DO NOT apply proper-case to Socio-economic category as per user request
      'Whether Any Disability',
      'Type of Disability',
      'Your Mother Tongue',
      'Gender',
      // Use exact field names from form for proper casing
      'Name of your village', 'Block', 'Tehsil', 'District'
    ];
    const EXCLUDE_FIELDS = [
      'Email Address', 'Form Number', 'PDF URL', 'Student Photo',
      'Timestamp', 'Last Edited', 'Aadhaar Number', 'Bank Account Number',
      'Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)'
    ];
    for (const row of structData) {
      const fieldName = row[0];
      const placeholder = row[5] || '';
      if (placeholder && (appData[fieldName] !== undefined && appData[fieldName] !== null)) {
        // Skip photo placeholder replacement here so we can insert image later
        if (placeholder.trim() === '{{PHOTO}}' || fieldName === 'Student Photo') {
          continue;
        }
        let value = appData[fieldName];
        if (fieldName === "DoB (as per school records)" && value) {
            const v = String(value).trim();
            const ddmmyyyy = v.match(/^([0-9]{2})-([0-9]{2})-([0-9]{4})$/);
            const yyyymmdd = v.match(/^([0-9]{4})-([0-9]{2})-([0-9]{2})/);
            if (ddmmyyyy) {
              value = `${ddmmyyyy[1]}-${ddmmyyyy[2]}-${ddmmyyyy[3]}`;
            } else if (yyyymmdd) {
              const d = new Date(`${yyyymmdd[1]}-${yyyymmdd[2]}-${yyyymmdd[3]}T00:00:00Z`);
              value = Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd-MM-yyyy');
            } else if (value instanceof Date) {
              value = Utilities.formatDate(value, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd-MM-yyyy');
            }
        }
        else if ((fieldName === 'Timestamp' || fieldName === 'Last Edited') && value) {
          try {
            const d = (value instanceof Date) ? value : new Date(value);
            value = Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd-MM-yyyy, hh:mm:ss a');
          } catch (e) {}
        }
        else if (value instanceof Date) {
          value = Utilities.formatDate(value, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd-MM-yyyy');
        }
        // Apply casing rules
        if (typeof value === 'string') {
          if (fieldName === 'Email Address') {
            value = value.toLowerCase();
          } else if (UPPER_FIELDS.includes(fieldName)) {
            value = value.toUpperCase();
          } else if (!EXCLUDE_FIELDS.includes(fieldName)) {
            if (PROPER_FIELDS.includes(fieldName)) {
              value = toProperCase(value);
            }
          }
        }
        // [NEW] Handle Declaration Checkbox
        if (fieldName === 'Declaration' && (value === 'TRUE' || value === true)) {
          value = 'Yes, I Agree';
        }
        // Only replace text if we have a DocumentApp body (not HTML fallback)
        if (body) {
          body.replaceText(placeholder, String(value));
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // --- Handle Photo Replacement ---
  const photoUrl = appData['Student Photo'];
  if (photoUrl) {
    try {
      const m1 = String(photoUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
      const m2 = String(photoUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
      const photoId = (m1 && m1[1]) ? m1[1] : (m2 && m2[1]) ? m2[1] : '';
      if (photoId) {
        const photoBlob = DriveApp.getFileById(photoId).getBlob();
        if (body && !usedFallbackTemplate) {
          let start = null;
          while (true) {
            const found = body.findText('{{PHOTO}}', start);
            if (!found) break;
            const element = found.getElement().asText();
            const parent = element.getParent();
            const index = parent.getChildIndex(element);
            parent.insertInlineImage(index, photoBlob).setWidth(120).setHeight(150);
            element.removeFromParent();
            start = found;
          }
          // For HTML fallback, photo is already handled in the HTML content
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      logError(e, 'photoReplacement');
      if (body) {
        body.replaceText('{{PHOTO}}', '[PHOTO UPLOAD FAILED]');
      }
    }
  } else {
    if (body) {
      body.replaceText('{{PHOTO}}', '[NO PHOTO UPLOADED]');
    }
  }
  // --- Compute percentages and divisions for classes 8-12 and replace placeholders like {{per8}} / {{div8}} ---
  try {
    const classMap = {
      8: { marks: 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 8th', max: 'Total Max. Marks in Class 8th' },
      9: { marks: 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 9th', max: 'Total Max. Marks in Class 9th' },
      10: { marks: 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 10th', max: 'Total Max. Marks in Class 10th' },
      11: { marks: 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 11th', max: 'Total Max. Marks in Class 11th' },
      12: { marks: 'Total Marks Obtained in Class 12th', max: 'Total Max. Marks in Class 12th' }
    };
    Object.keys(classMap).forEach(k => {
      const entry = classMap[k];
      const obt = parseFloat(appData[entry.marks]) || 0;
      const maxm = parseFloat(appData[entry.max]) || 0;
      let perText = '';
      let divText = '';
      if (maxm > 0) {
        const per = (obt / maxm) * 100;
        perText = (Math.round(per * 100) / 100).toFixed(2) + '%';
        if (per >= 60) divText = '1st Div.';
        else if (per >= 50) divText = '2nd Div.';
        else if (per >= 40) divText = '3rd Div.';
        else divText = 'Fail / No Division';
      } else {
        perText = '';
        divText = '';
      }
      if (body) {
        body.replaceText('{{per' + k + '}}', perText);
        body.replaceText('{{div' + k + '}}', divText);
      }
    });
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'percentageDivisionCalculation');
  }
  // --- Session placeholder replacement: read from Config sheet so admin can control session ---
  try {
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    let sessionValue = '';
    if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
      const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
        if (String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase() === 'session') {
          sessionValue = configData[i][1] || '';
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    if (!sessionValue) sessionValue = '2025-26';
    if (body) {
      body.replaceText('{{session}}', sessionValue);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'sessionReplacement');
  }
  // Clean up any remaining placeholders
  if (body) {
    body.replaceText(/\{\{.*\}\}/g, ''); // Replaces any unfulfilled placeholders with empty string
  }
  
  // Save and close the temp doc
  if (tempDoc && body) {
    tempDoc.saveAndClose();
  }
  // Create the PDF
  const formNumber = appData['Form Number'];
  const studentName = appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || 'unknown';
  const cleanName = studentName.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9\s]/g, '').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_');
  const cleanClass = classSought.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '');
  const timestamp = Utilities.formatDate(new Date(), Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss');
  let pdfSuffix = isEdit ? '_adm_forme' : '_adm form';
  
  // [MODIFIED] If upgrading from Provisional to Full, make it distinct
  // We can't strictly know if it was Provisional BEFORE this edit without extra lookups,
  // but if we are in Full mode now and it is an edit, we assume it's an upgrade or correction.
  // The user explicitly asked for "both stored", so ensuring a new filename with timestamp is key.
  // We already have timestamp in the name, so just ensure we don't delete the old one in cleanup.
  if (isEdit && admType !== 'Provisional') {
      pdfSuffix = '_FULL_UPGRADE';
  }

  const pdfName = `${formNumber}_${cleanName}_${cleanClass}_${timestamp}${pdfSuffix}.pdf`;
  
  // Cleanup logic - ONLY delete old drafts if not upgrading
  if (!isEdit) {
    try {
      const existingFiles = pdfFolder.getFilesByName(pdfName);
      while (existingFiles.hasNext()) {
        const file = existingFiles.next();
        file.setTrashed(true);
      }
    } catch (e) { logError(e, 'generatePdf-cleanup'); }
  }

  const pdfBlob = tempFile.getAs(MimeType.PDF);
  const pdfFile = pdfFolder.createFile(pdfBlob.setName(pdfName));
  pdfFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
  const pdfUrl = pdfFile.getUrl();
  const pdfId = pdfFile.getId();
  // Provide a direct download URL for email links
  const pdfDownloadUrl = `https://drive.google.com/uc?export=download&id=${pdfFile.getId()}`;
  // Delete the temporary doc
  tempFile.setTrashed(true);
  return { pdfBlob, pdfUrl, pdfDownloadUrl, pdfId };
}
function generatePdfForForm(formNumber, user) {
  try {
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const formNumberIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    let appData = null;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (data[i][formNumberIdx] == formNumber) {
        appData = headers.reduce((obj, h, j) => {
          obj[h] = data[i][j] instanceof Date ? data[i][j].toISOString() : data[i][j];
          return obj;
        }, {});
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!appData) throw new Error('Application not found');
    // Prefer existing PDF URL if available
    if (appData['PDF URL']) {
      try { logActivity('Generate PDF (reuse)', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
      return { success: true, pdfUrl: appData['PDF URL'], pdfName: `Form_${formNumber}.pdf` };
    }
    // Fall back to generation
    const { pdfUrl } = generatePdf(appData);
    try { logActivity('Generate PDF', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, pdfUrl: pdfUrl, pdfName: `Form_${formNumber}.pdf` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'generatePdfForForm');
    throw new Error('PDF generation failed: ' + e.message);
  }
}
function copyBatchFiles(formNumbersStr, user) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000); // Prevent concurrent operations
  try {
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const formNumbers = formNumbersStr.split(',').map(f => String(f).trim()).filter(f => f && !isNaN(f));
    if (formNumbers.length === 0) throw new Error('Invalid form numbers: Provide comma-separated numbers (e.g., 250001,250002)');
    // Helper: try getFolderById, otherwise try to find/create by name under root
    function getFolderSafe(id, fallbackName) {
      try {
        if (id) return DriveApp.getFolderById(id);
      } catch (e) {
        // continue to fallback
      }
      try {
        const root = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
        const it = root.getFoldersByName(fallbackName);
        return it.hasNext() ? it.next() : root.createFolder(fallbackName);
      } catch (e2) {
        throw new Error(`Unable to access folder id '${id}' and failed to find/create fallback folder '${fallbackName}': ${e2.message}`);
      }
    }

    const sourcePdfFolder = getFolderSafe(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePdfFolderId, 'Admission Form PDFs');
    const sourcePhotoFolder = getFolderSafe(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId, 'Student Photos');
    const batchFolder = getFolderSafe(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId, 'Batch Downloads');
    const batchFiles = batchFolder.getFiles();
    while (batchFiles.hasNext()) {
      const f = batchFiles.next();
      try { f.setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {}
    }
    let copiedCount = 0;
    const missing = [];
    function pickLatestByNameTimestamp(file, currentBest, currentScore) {
      const name = file.getName();
      const m = name.match(/_(\d{8}_\d{6})/);
      let score = file.getLastUpdated().getTime();
      if (m) {
        const s = m[1];
        const y = parseInt(s.slice(0,4));
        const mo = parseInt(s.slice(4,6)) - 1;
        const d = parseInt(s.slice(6,8));
        const hh = parseInt(s.slice(9,11));
        const mm = parseInt(s.slice(11,13));
        const ts = new Date(y, mo, d, hh, mm, 0).getTime();
        if (ts > score) score = ts;
      }
      if (score > currentScore) return { best: file, score };
      return { best: currentBest, score: currentScore };
    }
    formNumbers.forEach(fn => {
      let bestPdf = null;
      let bestPdfScore = 0;
      try {
        const q = `title contains "${fn}" and trashed = false`;
        const pdfSearch = sourcePdfFolder.searchFiles(q);
        while (pdfSearch.hasNext()) {
          const file = pdfSearch.next();
          const name = file.getName();
          const isPdf = file.getMimeType() === MimeType.PDF || /\.pdf$/i.test(name);
          if (!isPdf) continue;
          const picked = pickLatestByNameTimestamp(file, bestPdf, bestPdfScore);
          bestPdf = picked.best; bestPdfScore = picked.score;
        }
        if (bestPdf) { bestPdf.makeCopy(`form_${fn}.pdf`, batchFolder); copiedCount++; }
      } catch (e) {}
      let bestPhoto = null;
      let bestPhotoScore = 0;
      try {
        const q2 = `title contains "${fn}" and trashed = false`;
        const photoSearch = sourcePhotoFolder.searchFiles(q2);
        while (photoSearch.hasNext()) {
          const file = photoSearch.next();
          const picked = pickLatestByNameTimestamp(file, bestPhoto, bestPhotoScore);
          bestPhoto = picked.best; bestPhotoScore = picked.score;
        }
        if (bestPhoto) { bestPhoto.makeCopy(`photo_${fn}${getExtension(bestPhoto.getName())}`, batchFolder); copiedCount++; }
      } catch (e) {}
      if (!bestPdf && !bestPhoto) missing.push(fn);
    });
    if (copiedCount === 0) {
      throw new Error(`No files found for form numbers: ${missing.join(',')}.`);
    }
    const folderUrl = `https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/${SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId}`;
    try { logActivity('Batch Copy Files', user, `Forms: ${formNumbers.join(', ')}`); } catch (e) {}
    return {
      success: true,
      folderUrl: folderUrl,
      message: `${copiedCount} files copied to batch folder.`
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'copyBatchFiles');
    throw new Error('Batch copy failed: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

// Small helper to extract file extension (default .jpg)
function getExtension(name) {
  try {
    const idx = name.lastIndexOf('.');
    return idx > -1 ? name.slice(idx) : '.jpg';
  } catch (e) { return '.jpg'; }
}

/**
 * Return the batch folder URL (safe access). Used by client 'View Batch' button.
 */
function getBatchFolderUrl() {
  try {
    // Try direct id
    if (SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId) {
      try { DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId); } catch (e) { /* continue */ }
    }
    // Attempt to construct URL; if id is invalid this still returns a URL but client may fail to open
    const folderId = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId || '';
    if (!folderId) throw new Error('Batch folder id not configured');
    return { success: true, folderUrl: `https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/${folderId}` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getBatchFolderUrl');
    throw new Error('Unable to get batch folder URL: ' + e.message);
  }
}
function generateFormNumber() {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const formNumberIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return 260000;
    const formNumbers = sheet.getRange(2, formNumberIdx + 1, lastRow - 1, 1).getValues().flat().map(Number);
    const lastFormNumber = Math.max(...formNumbers);
    return isNaN(lastFormNumber) ? 260000 : lastFormNumber + 1;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function unlockWithExpiry(formNumber, expiryStr, user) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const expiry = new Date(expiryStr);
    if (isNaN(expiry.getTime())) throw new Error('Invalid expiry date/time');
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowToUpdate = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (unlocksData[i][0] == formNumber) {
        rowToUpdate = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    const start = new Date();
    const rowData = [formNumber, start, expiry, user.email, '', 0]; // Draft Count starts at 0
    if (rowToUpdate > 0) {
      unlocksSheet.getRange(rowToUpdate, 1, 1, 6).setValues([rowData]);
    } else {
      unlocksSheet.appendRow(rowData);
    }
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (sheet) {
      let headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
      setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(sheet, headers, formNumber, { 'Lock Status': 'Unlocked' });
    }
    try { logActivity('Unlock Application', user, `Form ${formNumber} until ${expiryStr}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: `Application #${formNumber} unlocked until ${expiry.toLocaleString('en-IN')}. You can make up to 15 draft saves.` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'unlockWithExpiry');
    throw new Error(`Unlock failed: ${e.message}`);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/**
 * Temporarily unlock an application for the current admin session.
 * Adds a marker in Notes so the client can auto?lock on logout.
 */
function unlockForSession(formNumber, user) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + 2 * 60 * 60 * 1000); // 2 hours fail?safe
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let rowToUpdate = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (String(unlocksData[i][0]) == String(formNumber)) { rowToUpdate = i + 1; break; }
    }
    const rowData = [formNumber, now, expiry, user.email, 'TempEdit', 0];
    if (rowToUpdate > 0) unlocksSheet.getRange(rowToUpdate, 1, 1, 6).setValues([rowData]);
    else unlocksSheet.appendRow(rowData);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (sheet) {
      let headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
      headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
      setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(sheet, headers, formNumber, { 'Lock Status': 'Unlocked' });
    }
    try { logActivity('Unlock Application (session)', user, `Form ${formNumber} until ${expiry.toISOString()}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: `Application #${formNumber} unlocked for this session.` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'unlockForSession');
    throw new Error(`Unlock (session) failed: ${e.message}`);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/**
 * Auto?lock any temporary unlocks created during admin session.
 */
function lockTempUnlocksForAdmin(user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) return { success: true, locked: 0 };
    const data = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const formsToLock = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const r = data[i];
      const form = r[0];
      const adminEmail = String(r[3] || '').toLowerCase();
      const notes = String(r[4] || '');
      if (adminEmail === String(user.email).toLowerCase() && /tempedit/i.test(notes)) {
        formsToLock.push(form);
      }
    }
    let locked = 0;
    formsToLock.forEach(f => {
      try { const res = lockImmediately(f, user); if (res && res.success) locked++; } catch (e) {}
    });
    try { logActivity('Auto Lock (logout)', user, `Forms: ${formsToLock.join(', ')}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, locked };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'lockTempUnlocksForAdmin');
    return { success: false, locked: 0 };
  }
}

/**
 * Check unlock status and draft count limit (15 drafts max per unlock window)
 */
function checkUnlockStatus(formNumber, email) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { isUnlocked: false, draftCount: 0, canSave: false };
    }
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (unlocksData[i][0] == formNumber) {
        const unlockStart = unlocksData[i][1];
        const unlockExpiry = unlocksData[i][2];
        const draftCount = unlocksData[i][5] || 0;
        const now = new Date();
        const isExpired = now > new Date(unlockExpiry);
        const canSave = !isExpired && draftCount < 15;
        return {
          isUnlocked: !isExpired,
          draftCount: draftCount,
          canSave: canSave,
          expiryTime: unlockExpiry,
          remainingDrafts: Math.max(0, 15 - draftCount)
        };
      }
    }
    return { isUnlocked: false, draftCount: 0, canSave: false };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'checkUnlockStatus');
    return { isUnlocked: false, draftCount: 0, canSave: false };
  }
}

/**
 * Increment draft count when student saves while unlocked
 */
function incrementDraftCount(formNumber) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      throw new Error('No active unlock found');
    }
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (unlocksData[i][0] == formNumber) {
        const currentCount = unlocksData[i][5] || 0;
        if (currentCount >= 15) {
          throw new Error('Draft limit (15) reached for this unlock window');
        }
        unlocksSheet.getRange(i + 1, 6).setValue(currentCount + 1);
        return { success: true, newCount: currentCount + 1, remainingDrafts: 15 - (currentCount + 1) };
      }
    }
    throw new Error('No active unlock found for this form');
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'incrementDraftCount');
    throw new Error(`Cannot save: ${e.message}`);
  }
}

function deleteApplication(formNumber, user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const formNumberIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    const photoIdx = headers.indexOf('Student Photo');
    const formNumbers = sheet.getRange(2, formNumberIdx + 1, sheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
    const rowIdx = formNumbers.indexOf(Number(formNumber));
    if (rowIdx === -1) throw new Error('Application not found');
    if (pdfIdx !== -1) {
      const pdfUrl = sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 2, pdfIdx + 1).getValue();
      if (pdfUrl) {
        let pdfId = '';
        const m1 = String(pdfUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const m2 = String(pdfUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)\/view/);
        const m3 = String(pdfUrl).match(/id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        if (m1 && m1[1]) pdfId = m1[1];
        else if (m2 && m2[1]) pdfId = m2[1];
        else if (m3 && m3[1]) pdfId = m3[1];
        if (pdfId) {
          try { DriveApp.getFileById(pdfId).setTrashed(true); } catch (err) {}
        }
      }
    }
    if (photoIdx !== -1) {
      const photoUrl = sheet.getRange(rowIdx + 2, photoIdx + 1).getValue();
      if (photoUrl) {
        let photoId = '';
        const p1 = String(photoUrl).match(/[?&]id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        const p2 = String(photoUrl).match(/\/d\/([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)\/view/);
        const p3 = String(photoUrl).match(/id=([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)/);
        if (p1 && p1[1]) photoId = p1[1];
        else if (p2 && p2[1]) photoId = p2[1];
        else if (p3 && p3[1]) photoId = p3[1];
        if (photoId) {
          try { DriveApp.getFileById(photoId).setTrashed(true); } catch (err) {}
        }
      }
    }
    try { cleanupOldPdfs(formNumber, null); } catch (err) {}
    sheet.deleteRow(rowIdx + 2);
    try { logActivity('Delete Application', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: 'Application, PDF and Photo deleted.' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'deleteApplication');
    throw new Error(`Delete failed: ${e.message}`);
  }
}
function exportToCSV(user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length < 2) return "No data to export.";
    const csv = data.map(row =>
      row.map(field => `"${String(field).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`).join(',')
    ).join('\n');
    try { logActivity('Export CSV', user, `Rows: ${data.length - 1}`); } catch (e) {}
    return csv;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'exportToCSV');
    throw new Error(`Export failed: ${e.message}`);
  }
}
// =================================================================
// UTILITY FUNCTIONS
// =================================================================
function logError(e, context) {
  Logger.log(`Error in ${context}: ${e.message}\nStack: ${e.stack}`);
  // In a real app, you might log this to a spreadsheet
}
function logActivity(action, user, details) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTIVITY);
    if (!sheet) {
      sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTIVITY);
      sheet.appendRow(['Timestamp', 'UserEmail', 'Role', 'Action', 'Details']);
      sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    } else {
      const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.min(sheet.getLastColumn(), 10)).getValues()[0];
      if (headers.indexOf('Role') === -1) {
        sheet.insertColumnAfter(2);
        sheet.getRange(1, 3).setValue('Role').setFontWeight('bold');
      }
    }
    const email = (typeof user === 'object' ? user.email : user) || '';
    let role = (typeof user === 'object' ? user.role : '') || '';
    if (!role && email) {
      role = (String(email).toLowerCase() === String(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail).toLowerCase()) ? 'Admin' : 'Student';
    }
    
    // Re-read headers for correct append logic (in case we added column)
    const headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    const findIdx = (patterns) => headers.findIndex(h => patterns.some(p => p.test(String(h))));
    
    const tsIdx = findIdx([/^Timestamp$/i, /^Date$/i]);
    const emIdx = findIdx([/^UserEmail$/i, /^AdminEmail$/i, /^User\s*Email$/i, /^Admin\s*Email$/i, /^Email$/i]);
    const rlIdx = findIdx([/^Role$/i]);
    const acIdx = findIdx([/^Action$/i]);
    const dtIdx = findIdx([/^Details$/i]);
    
    const rowData = new Array(headers.length).fill('');
    if (tsIdx > -1) rowData[tsIdx] = new Date();
    if (emIdx > -1) rowData[emIdx] = String(email).toLowerCase();
    if (rlIdx > -1) rowData[rlIdx] = role;
    if (acIdx > -1) rowData[acIdx] = action;
    if (dtIdx > -1) rowData[dtIdx] = details || '';
    
    sheet.appendRow(rowData);
  } catch (err) {}
}
function getAdminActivityLog(user, options = {}) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!user || (!isUserAnyAdmin(user) && String(user.email).toLowerCase() !== String(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail).toLowerCase())) {
      throw new Error('Permission denied');
    }
    const limit = options.limit || 0;
    const offset = options.offset || 0;
    const actSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMIN_ACTIVITY);
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const usersSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
    const emailSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.EMAIL_LOGS);
    const now = new Date();
    // Build maps for joins
    let admData = [], admHeaders = [], usersData = [], usersHeaders = [];
    if (admSheet && admSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) { admData = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); admHeaders = admData.shift(); }
    if (usersSheet && usersSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) { usersData = usersSheet.getDataRange().getValues(); usersHeaders = usersData.shift(); }
    const formIdx = admHeaders.indexOf('Form Number');
    const emailIdx = admHeaders.indexOf('Email Address');
    const nameIdx = admHeaders.indexOf("Student's Name (as per school records)");
    const classIdx = admHeaders.indexOf('Admission sought for class');
    const studentMobIdx = admHeaders.indexOf('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
    const parentMobIdx = admHeaders.indexOf("Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)");
    const usersEmailIdx = usersHeaders.indexOf('Email');
    const usersMobileIdx = usersHeaders.indexOf('Mobile');
    const usersResidenceIdx = usersHeaders.indexOf('Residence');
    const usersNameIdx = usersHeaders.indexOf('Name');
    const admByForm = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < admData.length; i++) {
      const row = admData[i];
      const f = String(row[formIdx] || '');
      if (f) admByForm[f] = row;
    }
    const userByEmail = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < usersData.length; i++) {
      const row = usersData[i];
      const e = String(row[usersEmailIdx] || '').toLowerCase();
      if (e) userByEmail[e] = row;
    }
    let emails = [];
    if (emailSheet && emailSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      const eData = emailSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      const eHeaders = eData[0];
      emails = eData.slice(1).map(r => eHeaders.reduce((o, h, i) => { o[h] = r[i] instanceof Date ? r[i].toISOString() : r[i]; return o; }, {}));
    }

    // Activity rows
    let logs = [];
    const lastRow = actSheet ? actSheet.getLastRow() : 0;
    const totalLogs = Math.max(0, lastRow - 1);

    if (actSheet && lastRow >= 2) {
      let rowsToProcess = [];
      let headers = [];

      if (limit > 0) {
        // Fetch specific slice (most recent first)
        const endRow = lastRow - offset;
        const startRow = Math.max(2, endRow - limit + 1);
        const numRows = endRow - startRow + 1;

        if (numRows > 0) {
          headers = actSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, actSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
          const data = actSheet.getRange(startRow, 1, numRows, actSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues();
          rowsToProcess = data.map(r => headers.reduce((o, h, i) => { o[h] = r[i] instanceof Date ? r[i].toISOString() : r[i]; return o; }, {}));
        }
      } else {
        // Legacy/All
        const data = actSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        headers = data[0];
        rowsToProcess = data.slice(1).map(r => headers.reduce((o, h, i) => { o[h] = r[i] instanceof Date ? r[i].toISOString() : r[i]; return o; }, {}));
      }
      
      // Map for standardizing actions between Activity Log and Email Log
      const activityToEmailAction = {
        'Application Submission': 'Submit PDF',
        'Application Update': 'Edit Submit PDF',
        'Application Resubmitted': 'Submit PDF',
        'Lock Application': 'Lock Now Updated PDF',
        'Reject Application': 'Reject Application',
        'Send Password': 'Send Password'
      };

      // Enrich
      logs = rowsToProcess.map(r => {
        const details = String(r['Details'] || r['details'] || '');
        const userEmail = String(
          r['UserEmail'] || r['AdminEmail'] || r['User Email'] || r['Admin Email'] || r['Email'] || 
          r['useremail'] || r['adminemail'] || r['user email'] || r['admin email'] || r['email'] || ''
        ).toLowerCase().trim();
        const formMatch = details.match(/Form\s*[:#]?\s*(\d{5,})/i);
        const formNumber = formMatch ? formMatch[1] : '';
        let studentEmail = '', studentName = '', klass = '', accountMobile = '', studentMobile = '', parentMobile = '';
        if (formNumber && admByForm[formNumber]) {
          const row = admByForm[formNumber];
          studentEmail = String(row[emailIdx] || '');
          studentName = String(row[nameIdx] || '');
          klass = String(row[classIdx] || '');
          studentMobile = String(row[studentMobIdx] || '');
          parentMobile = String(row[parentMobIdx] || '');
          const prof = userByEmail[String(studentEmail).toLowerCase()] || null;
          accountMobile = prof ? String(prof[usersMobileIdx] || '') : '';
        } else if (details.startsWith('To ')) {
          const emailTo = details.replace(/^To\s+/,'').trim().toLowerCase();
          studentEmail = emailTo;
          const prof = userByEmail[emailTo] || null;
          if (prof) { accountMobile = String(prof[usersMobileIdx] || ''); studentName = String(prof[usersNameIdx] || ''); }
        }
        
        const action = String(r['Action'] || '');
        // Initial detection based on log text
        let emailSent = /Email:?\s*Sent/i.test(details) || /Send Password/i.test(action);
        
        // Final fallback to Email Logs check
        if (!emailSent && formNumber && emails.length > 0) {
          const targetEmailAction = activityToEmailAction[action] || action;
          emailSent = emails.some(e => 
            String(e.FormNumber) === String(formNumber) && 
            (String(e.Action) === targetEmailAction || String(e.Action) === action) &&
            String(e.Status || '').toLowerCase().includes('sent')
          );
        }

        const finalUser = userEmail || studentEmail || SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail;
        return Object.assign({}, r, {
          UserEmail: finalUser,
          Role: r['Role'] || (studentEmail && !userEmail ? 'Student' : ''),
          AdminEmail: finalUser, 
          FormNumber: formNumber,
          StudentEmail: studentEmail,
          StudentName: studentName,
          Class: klass,
          AccountMobile: accountMobile,
          StudentMobile: studentMobile,
          ParentMobile: parentMobile,
          EmailSent: emailSent
        });
      });
    }
    // OTPs
    let otps = [];
    if (otpSheet && otpSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
      const otpData = otpSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      const otpHeaders = otpData[0];
      const emailI = otpHeaders.indexOf('Email');
      const otpI = otpHeaders.indexOf('OTP');
      const expiryI = otpHeaders.indexOf('ExpiryTimestamp');
      const purposeI = otpHeaders.indexOf('Purpose');
      for (let i = 1; i < otpData.length; i++) {
        const r = otpData[i];
        const expiry = r[expiryI];
        const status = (expiry instanceof Date ? expiry : new Date(expiry)) > now ? 'Valid' : 'Expired';
        otps.push({
          Email: r[emailI],
          OTP: r[otpI],
          ExpiryTimestamp: (expiry instanceof Date) ? expiry.toISOString() : expiry,
          Purpose: purposeI > -1 ? (r[purposeI] || '') : '',
          Status: status
        });
      }
    }
    return { logs, otps, emails, totalLogs, lastModified: new Date().toISOString() };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getAdminActivityLog');
    throw new Error('Failed to fetch admin activity: ' + e.message);
  }
}
/**
 * [NEW] Immediately locks a submitted application by clearing any active unlock window.
 * If the application was edited during the unlock period, generates an updated PDF and emails the student.
 */
function lockImmediately(formNumber, user) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  lock.waitLock(30000);
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const unlocksSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.UNLOCKS);
    if (!unlocksSheet || unlocksSheet.getLastRow() < 2) {
      return { success: true, message: `No active unlock found for #${formNumber}.` };
    }
    const unlocksData = unlocksSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headersSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    if (!headersSheet) throw new Error(`Sheet ${SHEETS.ADMISSIONS} not found`);
    const data = headersSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let headers = data[0];
    headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(headersSheet, headers);
    const formIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const statusIdx = headers.indexOf('Status');
    const pdfIdx = headers.indexOf('PDF URL');
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const lastEditedIdx = headers.indexOf('Last Edited');
    const nameIdx = headers.indexOf("Student's Name (as per school records)");
    let appRowIndex = -1;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][formIdx]) == String(formNumber)) {
        appRowIndex = i + 1;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (appRowIndex === -1) throw new Error('Application not found');
    // Locate unlock row
    let unlockRow = -1;
    let unlockStart = null;
    for (let i = 1; i < unlocksData.length; i++) {
      if (String(unlocksData[i][0]) == String(formNumber)) {
        unlockRow = i + 1;
        unlockStart = unlocksData[i][1];
        break;
      }
    }
    if (unlockRow === -1) {
      try { logActivity('Lock Application (no unlock)', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
      return { success: true, message: `No active unlock found for #${formNumber}.` };
    }
    // Delete unlock row to lock immediately
    removeUnlockRows(unlocksSheet, formNumber);
    setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(headersSheet, headers, formNumber, { 'Lock Status': 'Locked' });
    // If edited after unlock start, regenerate PDF and email student
    const rowValues = headersSheet.getRange(appRowIndex, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
    const status = rowValues[statusIdx];
    // Allow locking drafts: simply end the unlock window without PDF regeneration or email
    if (status !== 'Submitted') {
      try { logActivity('Lock Draft', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
      return { success: true, message: `Draft #${formNumber} locked; editing disabled.` };
    }
    const lastEdited = rowValues[lastEditedIdx];
    const email = rowValues[emailIdx];
    const studentName = rowValues[nameIdx];
    const needsPdfUpdate = lastEdited && unlockStart && new Date(lastEdited) > new Date(unlockStart);
    
    // Release the main script lock early to prevent timeouts during PDF generation and emailing
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch(e) {}
    
    if (needsPdfUpdate) {
      const appData = headers.reduce((obj, h, i) => {
        obj[h] = rowValues[i];
        return obj;
      }, {});
      const { pdfBlob, pdfUrl, pdfDownloadUrl, pdfId } = generatePdf(appData, true);
      if (pdfIdx !== -1) {
        const _miniLock = LockService.getScriptLock();
        _miniLock.waitLock(30000);
        try {
          headersSheet.getRange(appRowIndex, pdfIdx + 1).setValue(pdfUrl);
        } finally {
          try { _miniLock.releaseLock(); } catch(e) {}
        }
      }
      try { cleanupOldPdfs(formNumber, pdfId); } catch (e) {}
      const subject = `Updated Admission Form Locked - Form #${formNumber}`;
      const plainBody = `Dear ${studentName},\n\nYour admission form was updated during the unlock period and is now locked.\n\nDownload your updated form: ${pdfUrl}\n(If download does not start, open in browser and save as PDF.)\n\nRegards,\nHSS Shangus Admission Portal`;
      const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
        title: 'Form Updated and Locked',
        preheader: `Form #${formNumber} locked`,
        greeting: `Dear ${studentName},`,
        intro: 'Your admission form was updated during the unlock window and is now locked. Use the button below to download your updated PDF.',
        steps: ['Download the updated form', 'Review and follow earlier submission instructions'],
        footer: 'For any queries, contact the admissions office.',
        ctaLabel: 'Download Updated PDF',
        ctaHref: pdfDownloadUrl || pdfUrl
      });
      const safeEmail = String(email || '').trim();
      if (safeEmail) {
        const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(safeEmail, subject, plainBody, { htmlBody: htmlBody, attachments: pdfBlob ? [pdfBlob] : undefined, emailAction: 'Lock Now Updated PDF', formNumber: String(formNumber), adminEmail: user.email });
        if (!emailResult.success) {
          console.warn('Lock notification email failed:', emailResult.message);
        }
      }
    }
    try { logActivity('Lock Application', user, `Form ${formNumber}`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true, message: `Application #${formNumber} locked immediately.` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'lockImmediately');
    throw new Error(`Lock failed: ${e.message}`);
  } finally {
    try { lock.releaseLock(); } catch (e) {}
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Builds responsive HTML email body. Note: Email clients block JavaScript;
 * keep styling to inline CSS and simple media queries.
 */
function buildEmailHtml({ title, preheader, greeting, intro, steps = [], footer = '', ctaLabel = '', ctaHref = '', themeColor = '#0f766e' }) {
  const stepsHtml = steps.length ? `<ol style="margin:0; padding-left:20px;">${steps.map(s => `<li style="margin-bottom:8px;">${s}</li>`).join('')}</ol>` : '';
  const style = `
    <style>
      .email-container { max-width: 640px; margin: 0 auto; background: #ffffff; border-radius: 12px; box-shadow: 0 3px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.08); overflow: hidden; }
      .email-header { background: ${themeColor}; color: #ffffff; padding: 16px 20px; font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif; }
      .email-body { padding: 20px; font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif; color: #334155; line-height: 1.6; }
      .email-title { margin: 0; font-size: 20px; }
      .email-footer { padding: 16px 20px; font-family: Arial, Helvetica, sans-serif; color: #475569; font-size: 12px; background: #e2e8f0; text-align: center; }
      .email-cta { display:inline-block; margin-top: 12px; padding: 10px 16px; background: #0ea5e9; color:#fff !important; text-decoration:none; border-radius:8px; font-weight:600; }
      @media (max-width: 480px) { .email-body { padding: 16px; } .email-title { font-size: 18px; } }
    </style>`;
  const preheaderHtml = `<div style="display:none; max-height:0; overflow:hidden; opacity:0;">${preheader || ''}</div>`;
  const defaultFooter = 'You are receiving this email because you registered or interacted with the HSS Shangus Admission Portal. If you did not request this, please ignore this email or reply to let us know.';
  const footerCombined = `${footer ? footer + '<br><br>' : ''}<strong>HSS Shangus Admissions Office</strong><br>${SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail}<br>${defaultFooter}`;
