  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="UTF-8">${style}</head><body style="background:#f1f5f9; margin:0; padding:24px;">
    ${preheaderHtml}
    <div class="email-container">
      <div class="email-header"><h1 class="email-title">${title}</h1></div>
      <div class="email-body">
        <p style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">${greeting}</p>
        <p style="margin:0 0 12px 0;">${intro}</p>
        ${stepsHtml}
        ${ctaHref ? `<p style="margin:16px 0 0 0; text-align:right;"><a class="email-cta" href="${ctaHref}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${ctaLabel || 'Download PDF'}</a></p>` : ''}
      </div>
      <div class="email-footer">${footerCombined}</div>
    </div>
  </body></html>`;
}
/**
 * Sends email with consistent identity to reduce spam flagging.
 */
function sendEmailSafe(to, subject, plainBody, options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const config = getAppSettings();
  
  // Mapping emailAction to config keys
  const actionToKey = {
    'Registration OTP': 'email_reg_otp',
    'Password Reset OTP': 'email_reset_otp',
    'Reject Application': 'email_rejection',
    'Upgrade Provisional to Full': 'email_upgrade',
    'Submit PDF': 'email_submission',
    'Edit Submit PDF': 'email_submission',
    'PDF Ready Reminder': 'email_submission',
    'Lock Now Updated PDF': 'email_submission'
  };
  
  if (opts.emailAction && actionToKey[opts.emailAction]) {
    const key = actionToKey[opts.emailAction];
    if (config[key] === false) {
      console.warn(`Email skipped: ${opts.emailAction} is disabled in settings.`);
      return { success: true, message: 'Email disabled in settings' };
    }
  }

  // --- Duplicate Guard using CacheService ---
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const formNum = opts.formNumber || 'OTP';
  const action = opts.emailAction || 'Generic';
  // Create a unique key for this specific email event
  const lockKey = `email_lock_${to.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_')}_${formNum}_${action.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_')}`;
  
  if (cache.get(lockKey)) {
    console.warn(`Duplicate email suppressed: ${subject} to ${to}`);
    return { success: true, message: 'Duplicate email suppressed.' };
  }
  // Set lock for 10 seconds (enough to prevent glitches/double-clicks, but short enough for retries)
  cache.put(lockKey, 'active', 10);

  let status = 'Sent';
  let success = true;
  let errorMessage = '';
  
  try {
    const adminEmail = opts.adminEmail || SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.adminEmail;
    
    // Modern signature: one object argument for reliability and better attachment handling
    const mailRequest = {
      to: to,
      subject: subject,
      body: plainBody,
      htmlBody: opts.htmlBody || plainBody,
      name: 'HSS Shangus Admissions',
      replyTo: adminEmail
    };

    if (opts.attachments && Array.isArray(opts.attachments) && opts.attachments.length > 0) {
      mailRequest.attachments = opts.attachments;
    }
    
    MailApp.sendEmail(mailRequest);

  } catch (e) {
    status = 'Failed: ' + e.message;
    success = false;
    errorMessage = e.message;
    // Remove lock on failure so a manual retry can proceed
    cache.remove(lockKey);
    console.warn('Email sending failed (non-critical):', e.message);
  } finally {
    try {
      const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
      let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.EMAIL_LOGS);
      if (!sheet) {
        sheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.EMAIL_LOGS);
        sheet.appendRow(['Timestamp','To','Subject','Action','FormNumber','AdminEmail','Status']);
        sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
      }
      const act = opts.emailAction || '';
      const fNum = opts.formNumber || '';
      const admEmail = opts.adminEmail || '';
      sheet.appendRow([new Date(), String(to).toLowerCase(), subject, act, fNum, String(admEmail).toLowerCase(), status]);
    } catch (logErr) {}
  }
  return { success: success, message: errorMessage };
}

/**
 * Sends WhatsApp message using configured API provider
 * Supports multiple providers: Twilio, Fast2SMS, or custom API
 */
function sendWhatsAppMessage(phoneNumber, message, options) {
  const opts = options || {};
  const config = getAppSettings();
  
  // Check if WhatsApp messaging is enabled
  if (config.whatsapp_rejection !== true && !opts.force) {
    console.warn('WhatsApp message skipped: whatsapp_rejection is disabled in settings');
    return { success: true, message: 'WhatsApp disabled in settings' };
  }
  
  // Clean phone number
  let cleanNumber = String(phoneNumber || '').replace(/\D/g, '');
  if (cleanNumber.startsWith('0')) {
    cleanNumber = '91' + cleanNumber.substring(1); // Convert to India format
  } else if (!cleanNumber.startsWith('91') && cleanNumber.length === 10) {
    cleanNumber = '91' + cleanNumber;
  }
  
  if (cleanNumber.length < 10) {
    return { success: false, message: 'Invalid phone number: ' + phoneNumber };
  }
  
  // --- Duplicate Guard using CacheService ---
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const formNum = opts.formNumber || 'MSG';
  const action = opts.messageAction || 'Generic';
  const lockKey = `whatsapp_lock_${cleanNumber}_${formNum}_${action.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '_')}`;
  
  if (cache.get(lockKey)) {
    console.warn(`Duplicate WhatsApp suppressed: ${action} to ${cleanNumber}`);
    return { success: true, message: 'Duplicate message suppressed.' };
  }
  cache.put(lockKey, 'active', 60); // 60 second lock
  
  let status = 'Sent';
  let success = true;
  let errorMessage = '';
  let apiResponse = null;
  
  try {
    const apiUrl = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.whatsappApiUrl;
    const apiKey = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.whatsappApiKey;
    const sender = SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.whatsappSender;
    
    if (!apiUrl) {
      // No API configured - try to use wa.me link as fallback
      const waMeLink = `https://wa.me/${cleanNumber}?text=${encodeURIComponent(message)}`;
      console.log('No WhatsApp API configured. Fallback wa.me link:', waMeLink);
      return { 
        success: true, 
        message: 'API not configured - wa.me link generated',
        waMeLink: waMeLink,
        phoneNumber: cleanNumber
      };
    }
    
    // Prepare API request based on common WhatsApp API formats
    let payload = {};
    let headers = {};
    
    // Detect API provider type from URL
    if (apiUrl.includes('twilio.com')) {
      // Twilio API format
      payload = {
        To: 'whatsapp:' + cleanNumber,
        From: 'whatsapp:' + sender,
        Body: message
      };
      headers = {
        'Authorization': 'Basic ' + Utilities.base64Encode(apiKey),
        'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'
      };
    } else if (apiUrl.includes('fast2sms') || apiUrl.includes('waapi')) {
      // Fast2SMS or similar Indian providers
      payload = {
        numbers: cleanNumber,
        message: message,
        sender_id: sender,
        route: 'whatsapp',
        language: 'english'
      };
      headers = {
        'Authorization': apiKey,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      };
    } else {
      // Generic/custom API format
      payload = {
        phone: cleanNumber,
        message: message,
        sender: sender,
        template: opts.template || 'generic'
      };
      headers = {
        'Authorization': 'Bearer ' + apiKey,
        'Content-Type': 'application/json'
      };
    }
    
    const fetchOptions = {
      method: 'post',
      headers: headers,
      payload: JSON.stringify(payload),
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    };
    
    const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(apiUrl, fetchOptions);
    const responseCode = response.getResponseCode();
    apiResponse = response.getContentText();
    
    if (responseCode >= 200 && responseCode < 300) {
      console.log('WhatsApp message sent successfully to', cleanNumber);
      status = 'Sent';
    } else {
      throw new Error('API returned: ' + apiResponse);
    }
    
  } catch (e) {
    status = 'Failed: ' + e.message;
    success = false;
    errorMessage = e.message;
    cache.remove(lockKey);
    console.warn('WhatsApp sending failed:', e.message);
  } finally {
    // Log to sheet
    try {
      const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
      let sheet = ss.getSheetByName('WhatsApp_Logs');
      if (!sheet) {
        sheet = ss.insertSheet('WhatsApp_Logs');
        sheet.appendRow(['Timestamp','Phone Number','Message Action','Form Number','Status','Response']);
        sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
      }
      const act = opts.messageAction || '';
      const fNum = opts.formNumber || '';
      sheet.appendRow([new Date(), cleanNumber, act, fNum, status, apiResponse || '']);
    } catch (logErr) {}
  }
  
  return { success: success, message: errorMessage, phoneNumber: cleanNumber };
}
/**
 * Sends the user's plain password by email. Admin-only operation.
 * NOTE: This function sends plaintext passwords stored in the sheet. This is insecure but requested by admin.
 */
function sendPasswordToUser(email, adminUser) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(adminUser, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(adminUser)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const emailIdx = headers.indexOf('Email');
    const nameIdx = headers.indexOf('Name');
    const plainIdx = headers.indexOf('PasswordPlain');
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase()) {
        const userName = data[i][nameIdx] || email;
        const plain = plainIdx > -1 ? data[i][plainIdx] : '';
        if (!plain) throw new Error('No plain password available for this user');
        const subject = 'HSS Shangus Portal ? Your Account Password';
        const body = `Dear ${userName},\n\nAs requested by the admin, here is your login information:\n\nEmail: ${email}\nPassword: ${plain}\n\nPlease change your password after logging in.`;
        const htmlBody = buildEmailHtml({
          title: 'Your Account Password',
          preheader: 'Your HSS Shangus account password',
          greeting: `Dear ${userName},`,
          intro: 'As requested by the admin, here is your login information:',
          steps: [`Email: <strong>${email}</strong>`, `Password: <strong>${plain}</strong>`],
          footer: 'Please change your password after logging in.'
        });
        const emailResult = sendEmailSafe(email, subject, body, { htmlBody: htmlBody, emailAction: 'Send Password', adminEmail: adminUser.email });
        if (!emailResult.success) {
          return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send password email. Error: ' + emailResult.message };
        }
        try { logActivity('Send Password', adminUser, `To ${email}`); } catch (e) {}
        return { success: true, message: `Password sent to ${email}.` };
      }
    }
    return { success: false, message: 'Email not registered.' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'sendPasswordToUser');
    return { success: false, message: 'Failed to send password: ' + e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Fetches recent OTP generation records for the admin dashboard.
 */
function getAdminOtpLogs(adminUser) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(adminUser, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(adminUser)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    
    const otpSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.OTPS);
    if (!otpSheet) return { logs: [] };
    
    const raw = otpSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = raw[0];
    const data = raw.slice(1);
    
    const logs = data.map(row => {
      // Current structure: Email, OTP, Expiry, Purpose, Name, Mobile, Created
      // Old structure might be shorter
      const expiryCell = row[2];
      const createdCell = row[6] || expiryCell;
      return {
        email: String(row[0] || ''),
        otp: String(row[1] || ''),
        expiry: expiryCell instanceof Date ? expiryCell.toISOString() : String(expiryCell || ''),
        purpose: String(row[3] || ''),
        name: String(row[4] || ''),
        mobile: String(row[5] || ''),
        timestamp: createdCell instanceof Date ? createdCell.toISOString() : String(createdCell || '')
      };
    });
    
    return { logs: logs };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getAdminOtpLogs');
    return { logs: [], error: e.message };
  }
}
// Provide server version for client auto-refresh on deploy
function getServerVersion() {
  return { version: PORTAL_VERSION, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
}

function cleanupOldPdfs(formNumber, keepId, mode) {
  try {
    const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
    const pdfFolders = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('Admission Form PDFs');
    if (!pdfFolders.hasNext()) return;
    const pdfFolder = pdfFolders.next();
    const q = `title contains "${formNumber}" and trashed = false`;
    const search = pdfFolder.searchFiles(q);
    while (search.hasNext()) {
      const file = search.next();
      if (file.getId() !== keepId) {
        if (mode === 'upgrade') {
          const oldName = file.getName();
          // Avoid double renaming if it already has 'p' after form number
          // We look for formNumber followed by an underscore or being at the start
          if (!oldName.includes(formNumber + 'p')) {
             const newName = oldName.replace(formNumber, formNumber + 'p');
             file.setName(newName);
          }
        } else {
          try { file.setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {}
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'cleanupOldPdfs');
  }
}

function cleanupOldPhotos(formNumber, keepId) {
  try {
    const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
    const photoFolder = rootFolder.getFoldersByName('Student Photos').next();
    const q = `title contains "${formNumber}" and trashed = false`;
    const search = photoFolder.searchFiles(q);
    while (search.hasNext()) {
      const file = search.next();
      if (file.getId() !== keepId) {
        try { file.setTrashed(true); } catch (e) {}
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'cleanupOldPhotos');
  }
}

// ADMIN TEST SUBMISSION FUNCTIONS - Demo Data Generation & Testing

/**
 * Generate demo application data for a specific class and admission scenario
 * Used by admin to test form submissions with various conditions

  // Base personal info - same for all classes
  const baseData = {
    "Student's Name (as per school records)": 'Demo Student ' + classSelected,

    'Gender': 'Male',

    'Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)': '9876543210',

    'House No.': '123',

    'Type of scholarship received': '',
    'Amount received (INR)': '',
    'Bank Account No.': '1234567890123456',
    'Name of Bank': 'SBI',
    'IFSC code': 'SBIN0001234',
    
    // Vocational (if applicable)
    'Vocational subject in previous class': 'No',
    'Percentage Obtained in Vocational Subject': '',
    
    // Additional Information
    'Passport No. (if available)': '',
    'Identification mark (if any)': 'Scar on right hand',
    'Previous participation in sports (if any)': 'Yes',
    'Games to participate': 'Cricket',
    'PEN number (given by UDISE portal)': '12345678901',
    'APAAR ID': '123456789012',

    'Declaration': 'checked'
  };
  
  // Class-specific academic data

      'Board (Class 8th)': 'JKBOSE',
      'Total Marks Obtained in Class 8th': '450',

      'Subjects to be taken in Class 9th': 'English,Mathematics,Science,Social Studies,Urdu',
      'Name of Previous Complex Head': 'Demo Principal'

  } else if (classSelected === '10th') {
    classSpecificData = {
      'Board Registration No. (Class 9th)': 'BRN123456',
      'Year of Passing Class 9th': '2024',
      'Name of Previous School (Class 9th)': 'Demo High School',
      'Board (Class 9th)': 'JKBOSE',
      'Total Max. Marks in Class 9th': '500',
      'Total Marks Obtained in Class 9th': '420',
      'Subjects Studied in Class 9th': 'English,Mathematics,Science,Social Studies,Urdu',
      'Subjects to be taken in Class 10th': 'English,Mathematics,Science,Social Studies,Urdu'

    const streamData = stream || 'Science';

      'Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)': isProvisional ? (provisionalReason || 'Reappear Candidate') : '',
      'Board Registration No. (Class 10th)': 'BRN654321',

      'Year of Passing Class 11th': isProvisional ? '' : '2024',

    };
  }
  
  return { ...baseData, ...classSpecificData };
}

/**
 * Create demo test applications - callable from admin UI
 */
function createDemoTestApplications(scenario = 'all', user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const results = {
      success: [],
      failed: [],
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };
    
    const scenarios = {
      'all': [
        { class: '9th', admType: 'Full' },
        { class: '10th', admType: 'Full' },
        { class: '11th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Science' },
        { class: '11th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Humanities' },
        { class: '11th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Science', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' },
        { class: '11th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Humanities', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' },
        { class: '12th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Science' },
        { class: '12th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Humanities' },
        { class: '12th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Science', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' },
        { class: '12th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Humanities', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' }
      ],
      '9th': [{ class: '9th', admType: 'Full' }],
      '10th': [{ class: '10th', admType: 'Full' }],
      '11th-full': [{ class: '11th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Science' }],
      '11th-provisional': [{ class: '11th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Science', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' }],
      '12th-full': [{ class: '12th', admType: 'Full', stream: 'Science' }],
      '12th-provisional': [{ class: '12th', admType: 'Provisional', stream: 'Science', reason: 'Reappear Candidate' }]
    };
    
    const testScenarios = scenarios[scenario] || scenarios['all'];
    
    for (const s of testScenarios) {
      try {
        const demoData = generateDemoApplicationData(s.class, s.admType, s.stream, s.reason);
        
        // Create temporary user for this demo
        const demoEmail = demoData['E-mail ID'];
        const tempResult = submitDemoApplication(demoEmail, demoData, s.class);
        
        if (tempResult.success) {
          results.success.push({
            email: demoEmail,
            class: s.class,
            admissionType: s.admType,
            stream: s.stream || 'N/A',
            formNumber: tempResult.formNumber,
            status: 'Submitted'
          });
        } else {
          results.failed.push({
            email: demoEmail,
            class: s.class,
            error: tempResult.message
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        results.failed.push({
          class: s.class,
          error: e.message
        });
      }
    }
    
    // Log results to sheet for reference
    logDemoTestResults(results);
    try { logActivity('Create Demo Test Applications', user, `Scenario: ${scenario}; Success: ${results.success.length}; Failed: ${results.failed.length}`); } catch (e) {}
    return results;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'createDemoTestApplications');
    return { success: [], failed: [e.message], timestamp: new Date().toISOString() };
  }
}

/**
 * Submit a demo application directly to the database
 */
function submitDemoApplication(email, applicationData, classSelected) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const formStructure = getFormStructure();
    
    // Determine current session from Config (fallback to 2025-26)
    let currentSession = '';
    try {
      const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
      if (configSheet && configSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
        const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
        for (let i = 1; i < configData.length; i++) {
          if (String(configData[i][0]).toLowerCase() === 'session') {
            currentSession = configData[i][1] || '';
            break;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (sessErr) { logError(sessErr, 'submitDemoApplication.getSession'); }
    if (!currentSession) currentSession = '2025-26';

    // Build session prefix (use last two digits of start year)
    let sessionPrefix = '00';
    try {
      const m = String(currentSession).match(/(\d{4})/);
      if (m) {
        const year = String(m[1]);
        sessionPrefix = year.slice(2); // e.g., 2025 -> '25'
      } else {
        // fallback: take first two digits found
        const mm = String(currentSession).match(/(\d{2})/);
        if (mm) sessionPrefix = mm[1];
      }
    } catch (e) { sessionPrefix = '00'; }

    const sessionBase = parseInt(sessionPrefix + '0000', 10);

    const headers = admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, admSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];

    const lastRow = admSheet.getLastRow();
    let formNumber = sessionBase;
    const formNumIndexHeader = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    if (formNumIndexHeader >= 0 && lastRow >= 2) {
      const existingVals = admSheet.getRange(2, formNumIndexHeader + 1, Math.max(0, lastRow - 1), 1).getValues().flat();
      let maxFound = sessionBase - 1;
      existingVals.forEach(v => {
        const num = parseInt(String(v).match(/\d+/)?.[0] || '0', 10);
        if (!isNaN(num) && String(num).startsWith(String(sessionPrefix))) {
          if (num > maxFound) maxFound = num;
        }
      });
      if (maxFound >= sessionBase) formNumber = maxFound + 1;
      else formNumber = sessionBase;
    }
    
    const rowData = new Array(headers.length).fill('');
    
    // Map form structure to row
    for (const config of formStructure) {
      const colIndex = headers.indexOf(config.fieldName);
      if (colIndex >= 0 && applicationData[config.fieldName]) {
        rowData[colIndex] = String(applicationData[config.fieldName]);
      }
    }
    
    // Set Form Number and Status
    const formNumIndex = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const statusIndex = headers.indexOf('Status');
    const emailIndex = headers.indexOf('Email') >= 0 ? headers.indexOf('Email') : headers.indexOf('Email Address');
    const submittedDateIndex = headers.indexOf('Submitted Date');
    const sessionIndex = headers.indexOf('Session');
    
    if (formNumIndex >= 0) rowData[formNumIndex] = formNumber;
    if (statusIndex >= 0) rowData[statusIndex] = 'Submitted';
    if (emailIndex >= 0) rowData[emailIndex] = email;
    if (submittedDateIndex >= 0) rowData[submittedDateIndex] = new Date();
    if (sessionIndex >= 0) rowData[sessionIndex] = currentSession;
    
    // Append to sheet
    admSheet.appendRow(rowData);
    // Ensure applicationData has Form Number for PDF generation
    try {
      applicationData['Form Number'] = formNumber;
      // Generate PDF for this demo application
      const pdfResult = generatePdf(applicationData);
      // Send email to admin/test address with PDF attached
      try {
        const adminEmail = 'adm.exam.hss.shangus@gmail.com';
        const subject = `Demo Application Submitted - Form ${formNumber} (${classSelected})`;
        const plainBody = `A demo application was submitted.\nForm Number: ${formNumber}\nClass: ${classSelected}\nEmail: ${email}`;
        const htmlBody = `<p>A demo application was submitted.</p><ul><li><strong>Form Number:</strong> ${formNumber}</li><li><strong>Class:</strong> ${classSelected}</li><li><strong>Email:</strong> ${email}</li></ul><p>PDF attached.</p><p>Download: <a href="${pdfResult.pdfDownloadUrl}">${pdfResult.pdfDownloadUrl}</a></p>`;
        sendEmailSafe(adminEmail, subject, plainBody, { htmlBody: htmlBody, attachments: [pdfResult.pdfBlob], emailAction: 'Demo Submission Notice', formNumber: String(formNumber), adminEmail: adminEmail });
      } catch (mailErr) {
        // Log but don't fail the demo submission entirely
        logError(mailErr, 'submitDemoApplication.sendEmail');
      }
    } catch (pdfErr) {
      logError(pdfErr, 'submitDemoApplication.generatePdf');
    }
    
    return {
      success: true,
      formNumber: formNumber,
      email: email,
      message: `Demo application created successfully`
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'submitDemoApplication');
    return {
      success: false,
      message: e.message
    };
  }
}

function assertEqual(actual, expected, label) {
  if (actual !== expected) throw new Error(label + ' expected ' + expected + ' got ' + actual);
}
function assertTrue(value, label) {
  if (!value) throw new Error(label + ' expected true');
}
function runFormLockingUnitTests() {
  const results = { passed: [], failed: [] };
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  let sheet = null;
  try {
    const name = 'Tests_Admissions_' + Date.now();
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(name);
    sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 2).setValues([['Form Number', 'Status']]);
    let headers = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    headers = ensureAdmissionColumns(sheet, headers);
    FORM_STATE_HEADERS.forEach(h => assertTrue(headers.indexOf(h) !== -1, 'Missing header ' + h));
    results.passed.push('ensureAdmissionColumns');
  } catch (e) {
    results.failed.push('ensureAdmissionColumns: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (sheet) ss.deleteSheet(sheet);
  }
  let unlockSheet = null;
  try {
    const name = 'Tests_Unlocks_' + Date.now();
    unlockSheet = ss.insertSheet(name);
    unlockSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 6).setValues([['Form Number', 'Unlock Start', 'Unlock Expiry', 'Admin Email', 'Notes', 'Draft Count']]);
    const now = new Date();
    const future = new Date(now.getTime() + 60 * 60 * 1000);
    const past = new Date(now.getTime() - 60 * 60 * 1000);
    unlockSheet.getRange(2, 1, 2, 6).setValues([
      ['F1', now, future, 'a@b.com', '', 0],
      ['F2', now, past, 'a@b.com', '', 0]
    ]);
    const infoActive = getUnlockInfo(unlockSheet, 'F1');
    const infoExpired = getUnlockInfo(unlockSheet, 'F2');
    assertTrue(infoActive.active, 'Unlock active');
    assertEqual(infoExpired.active, false, 'Unlock expired');
    results.passed.push('getUnlockInfo');
  } catch (e) {
    results.failed.push('getUnlockInfo: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (unlockSheet) ss.deleteSheet(unlockSheet);
  }
  let sheet2 = null;
  try {
    const name = 'Tests_State_' + Date.now();
    sheet2 = ss.insertSheet(name);
    sheet2.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([['Form Number', 'Status', 'Lock Status', 'Rejected At', 'Submission Attempts']]);
    sheet2.getRange(2, 1, 1, 5).setValues([['F3', 'Submitted', 'Unlocked', '', 1]]);
    let headers2 = sheet2.getRange(1, 1, 1, sheet2.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(sheet2, headers2, 'F3', { 'Lock Status': 'Locked', 'Rejected At': new Date(), 'Submission Attempts': 2 });
    const row = sheet2.getRange(2, 1, 1, headers2.length).getValues()[0];
    assertEqual(row[headers2.indexOf('Lock Status')], 'Locked', 'Lock status update');
    assertEqual(row[headers2.indexOf('Submission Attempts')], 2, 'Submission attempts update');
    results.passed.push('setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber');
  } catch (e) {
    results.failed.push('setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (sheet2) ss.deleteSheet(sheet2);
  }
  return results;
}
function runFormLockingIntegrationTests() {
  const results = { passed: [], failed: [] };
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  let admSheet = null;
  let unlockSheet = null;
  try {
    const admName = 'Tests_Adm_Int_' + Date.now();
    const unlockName = 'Tests_Unlocks_Int_' + Date.now();
    admSheet = ss.insertSheet(admName);
    unlockSheet = ss.insertSheet(unlockName);
    admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 8).setValues([['Form Number', 'Status', 'Lock Status', 'Rejected At', 'Submission Attempts', 'Last Submission At', 'Email Address', 'Admission sought for class']]);
    admSheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 8).setValues([['T100', 'Submitted', 'Locked', '', 1, '', 't@t.com', '11th']]);
    unlockSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 6).setValues([['Form Number', 'Unlock Start', 'Unlock Expiry', 'Admin Email', 'Notes', 'Draft Count']]);
    const now = new Date();
    const expiry = new Date(now.getTime() + 30 * 60 * 1000);
    unlockSheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, 6).setValues([['T100', now, expiry, 'admin@t.com', 'Rejected', 0]]);
    const info = getUnlockInfo(unlockSheet, 'T100');
    assertTrue(info.active, 'Unlock active integration');
    removeUnlockRows(unlockSheet, 'T100');
    const infoAfter = getUnlockInfo(unlockSheet, 'T100');
    assertEqual(infoAfter.active, false, 'Unlock removed integration');
    let headers = admSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, admSheet.getLastColumn()).getValues()[0];
    setAdmissionFieldsByFormNumber(admSheet, headers, 'T100', { 'Lock Status': 'Locked', 'Submission Attempts': 2 });
    const row = admSheet.getRange(2, 1, 1, headers.length).getValues()[0];
    assertEqual(row[headers.indexOf('Submission Attempts')], 2, 'Submission attempts integration');
    results.passed.push('integrationFlow');
  } catch (e) {
    results.failed.push('integrationFlow: ' + e.message);
  } finally {
    if (admSheet) ss.deleteSheet(admSheet);
    if (unlockSheet) ss.deleteSheet(unlockSheet);
  }
  return results;
}

/**
 * Log demo test results to a reference sheet
 */
function logDemoTestResults(results) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    let demoSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Demo_Test_Log');
    
    if (!demoSheet) {
      demoSheet = ss.insertSheet('Demo_Test_Log', 0);
      demoSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 5).setValues([['Timestamp', 'Class', 'Admission Type', 'Email', 'Form Number']])
        .setFontWeight('bold').setBackground('#4f46e5').setFontColor('#ffffff');
    }
    
    // Log successful submissions
    for (const app of results.success) {
      demoSheet.appendRow([
        results.timestamp,
        app.class,
        app.admissionType,
        app.email,
        app.formNumber
      ]);
    }
    
    // Log failures
    for (const fail of results.failed) {
      demoSheet.appendRow([
        results.timestamp,
        fail.class || 'Unknown',
        'Failed',
        fail.email || fail.error,
        'Error: ' + fail.error
      ]);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'logDemoTestResults');
  }
}

/**
 * Get demo data reference sheet for admin to understand what data is being tested
 */
function getDemoDataReference() {
  try {
    const scenarios = {
      '9th': generateDemoApplicationData('9th', 'Full'),
      '10th': generateDemoApplicationData('10th', 'Full'),
      '11th-Full': generateDemoApplicationData('11th', 'Full', 'Science'),
      '11th-Provisional-Reappear': generateDemoApplicationData('11th', 'Provisional', 'Science', 'Reappear Candidate'),
      '12th-Full': generateDemoApplicationData('12th', 'Full', 'Science'),
      '12th-Provisional-Reappear': generateDemoApplicationData('12th', 'Provisional', 'Science', 'Reappear Candidate')
    };
    
    return scenarios;
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getDemoDataReference');
    return {};
  }
}

/**
 * Clear demo test applications (admin utility)
 */
function clearDemoTestApplications() {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const demoSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Demo_Test_Log');
    
    if (demoSheet) {
      const lastRow = demoSheet.getLastRow();
      if (lastRow > 1) {
        demoSheet.deleteRows(2, lastRow - 1);
      }
    }
    
    return { success: true, message: 'Demo test log cleared' };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'clearDemoTestApplications');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}
    // Reuse existing Draft in current session for this account
    try {
      if (isNew && !isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
        const sessionIdx2 = headers.indexOf('Session');
        const existingSameSession = data.slice(1).filter(row =>
          String(row[emailIdx]).toLowerCase() === String(user.email).toLowerCase() &&
          row[sessionIdx2] === formData['Session']
        );
        const existingSameClass = existingSameSession.filter(row => row[classIdx] === classSought);
        // If a Submitted exists for same session and same class, block; otherwise reuse Draft for same class
        const submittedSameClass = existingSameClass.find(row => row[statusIdx] === 'Submitted');
        if (submittedSameClass) {
          throw new Error(`Only one application is allowed in session ${formData['Session']} for class ${classSought}.`);
        }
        const draftsSameClass = existingSameClass.filter(row => row[statusIdx] === 'Draft');
        if (draftsSameClass.length > 0) {
          const tsIdx = headers.indexOf('Timestamp');
          const sorted = draftsSameClass.sort((a,b) => new Date(b[tsIdx] || 0) - new Date(a[tsIdx] || 0));
          const latestDraft = sorted[0];
          const latestFormNumber = latestDraft[formNumberIdx];
          if (latestFormNumber) {
            formData['Form Number'] = latestFormNumber;
            isNew = false;
            formNumber = latestFormNumber;
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) { /* ignore fallback to new save */ }
    // Sanitize reappear subjects: clear when provisional but NOT reappear
    try {
      if (classSought === '11th') {
        const adm = String(formData['Admission Type (Class 11th)'] || '').trim();
        const reason = String(formData['Reason for Provisional (Class 11th)'] || '').trim();
        if (adm === 'Provisional' && reason !== 'Reappear Candidate') {
          formData['Subjects to Reappear (Class 10th)'] = '';
        }
        if (adm === 'Provisional') {
          formData['Total Marks Obtained in Class 10th'] = '';
          formData['Total Max. Marks in Class 10th'] = '';
          formData['Year of Passing Class 10th'] = '';
        } else if (adm === 'Full') {
          formData['Year of Appearing (Class 10th)'] = '';
        }
      } else if (classSought === '12th') {
        const adm = String(formData['Admission Type (Class 12th)'] || '').trim();
        const reason = String(formData['Reason for Provisional (Class 12th)'] || '').trim();
        if (adm === 'Provisional' && reason !== 'Reappear Candidate') {
          formData['Subjects to Reappear (Class 11th)'] = '';
        }
        if (adm === 'Provisional') {
          formData['Total Marks Obtained in Class 11th'] = '';
          formData['Total Max. Marks in Class 11th'] = '';
          formData['Year of Passing Class 11th'] = '';
        } else if (adm === 'Full') {
          formData['Year of Appearing (Class 11th)'] = '';
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {}

// BACKUP FILES TO BATCH FOLDER

/**
 * Backs up PDFs and Photos for specified form numbers to the Batch folder
 * Copies the most recent PDF and Photo for each form number
 * @param {Array} formNumbers - Array of form numbers to backup
 * @param {Object} user - Current user object
 * @returns {Object} - Success status and counts
 */
function backupFilesToBatchFolder(formNumbers, user) {
  try {
    // Verify admin access
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      throw new Error('Admin access required');
    }
    
    if (!formNumbers || formNumbers.length === 0) {
      throw new Error('No form numbers provided');
    }
    
    // Verify folder IDs exist
    if (!SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePdfFolderId) {
      throw new Error('PDF folder ID not configured');
    }
    if (!SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId) {
      throw new Error('Photo folder ID not configured');
    }
    if (!SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId) {
      throw new Error('Batch folder ID not configured');
    }
    
    // Access folders with error handling
    let sourcePdfFolder, sourcePhotoFolder, batchFolder;
    
    try {
      sourcePdfFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePdfFolderId);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error('Cannot access PDF folder: ' + e.message);
    }
    
    try {
      sourcePhotoFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error('Cannot access Photo folder: ' + e.message);
    }
    
    try {
      batchFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error('Cannot access Batch folder: ' + e.message);
    }
    
    let pdfCount = 0;
    let photoCount = 0;
    const errors = [];
    
    // Helper function to extract timestamp from filename
    // Looks for patterns like: 20231212_143045 or 20231212-143045 or 2023-12-12_14-30-45
    function extractTimestampFromFilename(filename) {
      try {
        // Pattern 1: 20231212_143045 or 20231212-143045
        let match = filename.match(/(\d{8})[_-](\d{6})/);
        if (match) {
          const dateStr = match[1]; // YYYYMMDD
          const timeStr = match[2]; // HHMMSS
          const year = dateStr.substr(0, 4);
          const month = dateStr.substr(4, 2);
          const day = dateStr.substr(6, 2);
          const hour = timeStr.substr(0, 2);
          const min = timeStr.substr(2, 2);
          const sec = timeStr.substr(4, 2);
          return new Date(year, month - 1, day, hour, min, sec);
        }
        
        // Pattern 2: 2023-12-12_14-30-45 or similar
        match = filename.match(/(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})[_\s](\d{2})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})/);
        if (match) {
          return new Date(match[1], match[2] - 1, match[3], match[4], match[5], match[6]);
        }
        
        // If no timestamp found, return epoch (very old date)
        return new Date(0);
      } catch (e) {
        return new Date(0);
      }
    }
    
    // Process each form number
    formNumbers.forEach(formNumber => {
      const formNum = String(formNumber).trim();
      if (!formNum) return;
      
      try {
        // ===== BACKUP PDF =====
        // Search for PDF files matching this form number
        const pdfSearchQuery = `title contains '${formNum}' and mimeType='application/pdf'`;
        const pdfFiles = sourcePdfFolder.searchFiles(pdfSearchQuery);
        
        let mostRecentPdf = null;
        let mostRecentPdfTimestamp = null;
        
        // Find the most recent PDF based on filename timestamp
        while (pdfFiles.hasNext()) {
          const pdfFile = pdfFiles.next();
          const pdfFilename = pdfFile.getName();
          const pdfTimestamp = extractTimestampFromFilename(pdfFilename);
          
          if (!mostRecentPdf || pdfTimestamp > mostRecentPdfTimestamp) {
            mostRecentPdf = pdfFile;
            mostRecentPdfTimestamp = pdfTimestamp;
          }
        }
        
        // Copy the most recent PDF to batch folder
        if (mostRecentPdf) {
          // Check if file already exists in batch folder
          const existingPdfQuery = `title='${mostRecentPdf.getName()}' and '${SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId}' in parents and trashed=false`;
          const existingPdfs = DriveApp.searchFiles(existingPdfQuery);
          
          if (!existingPdfs.hasNext()) {
            mostRecentPdf.makeCopy(mostRecentPdf.getName(), batchFolder);
            pdfCount++;
          } else {
            // File already exists, skip
            Logger.log(`PDF already exists for form ${formNum}`);
          }
        }
        
        // ===== BACKUP PHOTO =====
        // Search for photo files matching this form number
        const photoSearchQuery = `title contains '${formNum}'`;
        const photoFiles = sourcePhotoFolder.searchFiles(photoSearchQuery);
        
        let mostRecentPhoto = null;
        let mostRecentPhotoTimestamp = null;
        
        // Find the most recent photo based on filename timestamp
        while (photoFiles.hasNext()) {
          const photoFile = photoFiles.next();
          const mimeType = photoFile.getMimeType();
          
          // Only consider image files
          if (mimeType.startsWith('image/')) {
            const photoFilename = photoFile.getName();
            const photoTimestamp = extractTimestampFromFilename(photoFilename);
            
            if (!mostRecentPhoto || photoTimestamp > mostRecentPhotoTimestamp) {
              mostRecentPhoto = photoFile;
              mostRecentPhotoTimestamp = photoTimestamp;
            }
          }
        }
        
        // Copy the most recent photo to batch folder
        if (mostRecentPhoto) {
          // Check if file already exists in batch folder
          const existingPhotoQuery = `title='${mostRecentPhoto.getName()}' and '${SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId}' in parents and trashed=false`;
          const existingPhotos = DriveApp.searchFiles(existingPhotoQuery);
          
          if (!existingPhotos.hasNext()) {
            mostRecentPhoto.makeCopy(mostRecentPhoto.getName(), batchFolder);
            photoCount++;
          } else {
            // File already exists, skip
            Logger.log(`Photo already exists for form ${formNum}`);
          }
        }
        
      } catch (e) {
        errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: ${e.message}`);
        Logger.log(`Error backing up form ${formNum}: ${e.message}`);
      }
    });
    
    // Prepare response message
    let message = `Backup completed! ${pdfCount} PDFs and ${photoCount} photos copied to Batch folder.`;
    if (errors.length > 0) {
      message += ` (${errors.length} errors occurred)`;
    }
    
    return {
      success: true,
      message: message,
      pdfCount: pdfCount,
      photoCount: photoCount,
      errors: errors
    };
    } catch (e) {
      logError(e, 'backupFilesToBatchFolder');
      return {
        success: false,
        message: `Backup failed: ${e.message}`,
        pdfCount: 0,
        photoCount: 0
      };
    } finally {
      try { logActivity('Backup Files', user, `Copied ${pdfCount} PDFs and ${photoCount} photos to Batch folder`); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }
// =================================================================

 * @return {Object} Result with counts and details.
 */

    const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.rootFolderId);
    console.log("Drive API access successful.");

    console.log("Sheets API access successful.");

}

function saveContactsToGoogleInputs(formNumbersInput, user, taskId) {
  try {
    // Use CSV method - most reliable and doesn't require API setup
    return saveContactsToCSV(formNumbersInput, user, taskId);

  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveContactsToGoogleInputs');
    return { 
      success: false, 
      message: `Contact saving failed: ${e.message}`,
      savedCount: 0,
      errors: [e.message],
      skipped: []
    };
  }
}

/**
 * Saves contacts using People API (preferred method)
 */
function saveContactsToGoogleInputsWithPeopleAPI(formNumbersInput, user) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  ensureUserRole(user, ss);
  if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
    return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized: This feature is restricted to admin only." };
  }
  const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
  if (!admSheet) throw new Error("Admission sheet not found");

  // Get Session from Config
  let session = '2025-26'; // Default
  const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
  if (configSheet) {
    const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 1; r < configData.length; r++) {
      if (configData[r][0] === 'session') {
        session = configData[r][1];
        break;
      }
    }
  }

  const data = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  const headers = data[0];
  
  // Helper to get index safely
  const getIdx = (name) => headers.indexOf(name);

  // Find column indices for comprehensive contact information
  const idxForm = getIdx('Form Number');
  const idxName = getIdx("Student's Name (as per school records)");
  const idxMobile = getIdx('Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)');
  let idxEmail = getIdx('Email Address (Gmail Preferred - for updates)');
  if (idxEmail === -1) idxEmail = getIdx('Email Address');

  let idxClass = getIdx('Class to which admission is sought');
  if (idxClass === -1) idxClass = getIdx('Admission sought for class');
  
  let idxFather = getIdx("Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)");
  if (idxFather === -1) idxFather = getIdx("Father's Name"); // Fallback
  let idxMother = getIdx("Mother's Name (as per school records)");
  if (idxMother === -1) idxMother = getIdx("Mother's Name"); // Fallback

  const idxGender = getIdx('Gender');

  // Stream & Subjects Indices
  let idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream for Class 11th');
  if (idxStream11 === -1) idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream');
  if (idxStream11 === -1) idxStream11 = getIdx('Stream/Faculty');

  let idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 11th');
  if (idxSubjects11 === -1) idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects');
  if (idxSubjects11 === -1) idxSubjects11 = getIdx('Subjects Offered');

  let idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream & Subjects for Class 12th');
  if (idxStreamSubjects12 === -1) idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream & Subjects');
  if (idxStreamSubjects12 === -1) idxStreamSubjects12 = getIdx('Stream and Subjects');

  let idxSubjects9 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 9th');
  if (idxSubjects9 === -1) idxSubjects9 = getIdx('Subjects');

  let idxSubjects10 = getIdx('Subjects to be taken in Class 10th');
  if (idxSubjects10 === -1) idxSubjects10 = getIdx('Subjects');
  
  if (idxForm === -1 || idxName === -1 || idxMobile === -1) {
    throw new Error("Required columns (Form Number, Name, Mobile) not found.");
  }

  // Subject Abbreviation Helper
  const abbreviateSubjects = (subjStr) => {
    if (!subjStr) return '';
    const map = {
      // Academic Subjects
      'General English': 'GE', 'English Literature': 'EL', 'Functional English': 'FE',
      'Hindi': 'HI', 'Dogri': 'DG', 'Sanskrit': 'SA', 'Punjabi': 'PU', 'Bhoti': 'BO',
      'Arabic': 'AR', 'Persian': 'PE', 'Kashmiri': 'KA', 'Urdu': 'UR',
      'History': 'HT', 'Economics': 'EC', 'Geography': 'GG', 'Philosophy': 'PL',
      'Education': 'ED', 'Psychology': 'PY', 'Sociology': 'SO', 'Political Science': 'PS',
      'Home Science': 'HS', 'Home Science (Elective)': 'HS', 'Statistics': 'SS',
      'Mathematics': 'MA', 'Maths': 'MA', 'Physics': 'PH', 'Chemistry': 'CH',
      'Biology': 'BI', 'Botany': 'BO', 'Zoology': 'ZO', 'Computer Science': 'CS',
      'Information Technology': 'IT', 'Physical Education': 'PE', 'Fine Arts': 'FA',
      'Music': 'MU', 'Art': 'AR', 'Craft': 'CR', 'Environmental Studies': 'ES',
      'General Knowledge': 'GK', 'Moral Science': 'MS', 'Value Education': 'VE',
      'Life Skills': 'LS', 'Work Experience': 'WE', 'Health Education': 'HE',
      'Social Studies': 'SS', 'Civics': 'CV', 'Commerce': 'CM', 'Accountancy': 'AC',
      'Business Studies': 'BS', 'Entrepreneurship': 'EN', 'Agriculture': 'AG',
      'Horticulture': 'HO', 'Animal Husbandry': 'AH', 'Vocational Subjects': 'VS'
    };
    
    return subjStr.split(',')
      .map(s => {
        const trimmed = s.trim();
        return map[trimmed] || trimmed; 
      })
      .join(',');
  };

  // Parse input
  const requestedForms = formNumbersInput.split(',')
    .map(s => s.trim())
    .filter(s => s !== '');

  if (requestedForms.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, message: "No valid form numbers provided." };
  }

  let savedCount = 0;
  let errors = [];
  let skipped = [];

  // Map form numbers to row data for faster lookup
  const formMap = new Map();
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    const row = data[i];
    const fNo = String(row[idxForm]).trim();
    if (fNo) {
        const studentClass = idxClass !== -1 ? String(row[idxClass]).trim() : '';
        let stream = '';
        let subjects = '';

        // Logic to get Stream, Subjects based on Class
        if (studentClass.includes('11')) {
            stream = idxStream11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxStream11]) : '';
            subjects = idxSubjects11 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects11]) : '';
        } else if (studentClass.includes('12')) {
             const ss12 = idxStreamSubjects12 !== -1 ? String(row[idxStreamSubjects12]) : '';
             if (ss12.includes(' - ')) {
               const parts = ss12.split(' - ');
               stream = parts[0].trim();
               subjects = parts.slice(1).join(' - ').trim();
             } else {
               stream = ss12; 
             }
        } else if (studentClass.includes('9')) {
             subjects = idxSubjects9 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects9]) : '';
        } else if (studentClass.includes('10')) {
             subjects = idxSubjects10 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects10]) : '';
        }

        formMap.set(fNo, {
          name: row[idxName],
          mobile: String(row[idxMobile]),
          email: idxEmail !== -1 ? String(row[idxEmail]) : '',
          class: studentClass,
          father: idxFather !== -1 ? String(row[idxFather]) : '',
          mother: idxMother !== -1 ? String(row[idxMother]) : '',
          gender: idxGender !== -1 ? String(row[idxGender]) : '',
          stream: stream,
          subjects: subjects,
          subjects9: idxSubjects9 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects9]) : '',
          subjects10: idxSubjects10 !== -1 ? String(row[idxSubjects10]) : ''
        });
    }
  }

  // Get existing contacts to check for duplicates
  let existingContacts = [];
  try {
    // Use People API to get existing contacts
    const people = People.People.Connections.list('people/me', {
      personFields: 'names,phoneNumbers,emailAddresses',
      pageSize: 1000
    });
    existingContacts = people.connections || [];
  } catch (e) {
    console.log('Note: Could not fetch existing contacts for duplicate checking:', e.message);
  }

  requestedForms.forEach(formNum => {
    const student = formMap.get(formNum);
    if (student) {
      try {
        const contactName = student.name.toString().trim();
        const mobile = student.mobile.toString().trim();
        const email = student.email.toString().trim();
        const studentClass = student.class.toString().trim();
        const fatherName = student.father.toString().trim();
        const motherName = student.mother.toString().trim();
        const gender = student.gender.toString().trim();
        const stream = student.stream.toString().trim();
        // Extract subjects based on class
        let subjects = '';
        if (studentClass.includes('9th')) {
          subjects = student.subjects9 ? student.subjects9.toString().trim() : '';
        } else if (studentClass.includes('10th')) {
          subjects = student.subjects10 ? student.subjects10.toString().trim() : '';
        } else if (studentClass.includes('11th') || studentClass.includes('12th')) {
          subjects = student.subjects ? student.subjects.toString().trim() : '';
        }

        if (!contactName || !mobile) {
          errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Missing name or mobile number`);
          return;
        }

        // Validate mobile number (basic validation)
        const cleanMobile = mobile.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
        if (cleanMobile.length < 10) {
          errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Invalid mobile number format '${mobile}'`);
          return;
        }

        // Check for duplicate by phone number
        let contactExists = false;
        if (existingContacts.length > 0) {
          contactExists = existingContacts.some(contact => {
            return contact.phoneNumbers && contact.phoneNumbers.some(phone => {
              const existingPhone = phone.value.replace(/[^\d]/g, '');
              return existingPhone === cleanMobile;
            });
          });
        }

        if (contactExists) {
          skipped.push(`Form ${formNum} (${contactName}) - Already exists`);
          return;
        }

        // Construct Display Name per User Request
        // Format: "1. MUSKAAN BASHIR-BASHIR AHMAD BHAT, 11th student (2025-26)F,Humanities_GE,UR,ED,HT,HTC"
        const fullName = fatherName ? `${contactName}-${fatherName}` : contactName;
        const genderCode = gender.startsWith('F') ? 'F' : (gender.startsWith('M') ? 'M' : '');
        const subjectStr = abbreviateSubjects(subjects);
        
        let subjectsSuffix = '';
        if (stream && subjectStr) {
             subjectsSuffix = `,${stream}_${subjectStr}`;
        } else if (subjectStr) {
             subjectsSuffix = `,${subjectStr}`;
        } else if (stream) {
             subjectsSuffix = `,${stream}`;
        }

        const displayName = `${formNum}. ${fullName}, student (${studentClass}_${session})${genderCode}${subjectsSuffix}`;

        // Create contact using People API
        const contact = {
          names: [{
            givenName: contactName,
            displayName: displayName
          }],
          phoneNumbers: [{
            value: cleanMobile,
            type: 'mobile',
            formattedType: 'Mobile'
          }],
          biographies: [{
            value: `HSS Shangus Student\nForm Number: ${formNum}${studentClass ? '\nClass: ' + studentClass : ''}${fatherName ? '\nFather: ' + fatherName : ''}${motherName ? '\nMother: ' + motherName : ''}${subjects ? '\nSubjects: ' + subjects : ''}\nAdded on: ${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}\nAdded via: Bulk Contact Saver`,
            contentType: 'TEXT_PLAIN'
          }]
        };

        // Add email if available
        if (email && email.includes('@')) {
          contact.emailAddresses = [{
            value: email,
            type: 'work',
            formattedType: 'Work'
          }];
        }

        // Create the contact
        People.People.createContact(contact);
        savedCount++;

      } catch (err) {
        errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: ${err.message}`);
      }
    } else {
      errors.push(`Form ${formNum}: Not found in admissions data`);
    }
  });

  return {
    success: true,
    savedCount: savedCount,
    message: `Successfully saved ${savedCount} contacts. ${skipped.length} skipped (already exist). ${errors.length} errors.`,
    errors: errors,
    skipped: skipped
  };
}

/**
 * Syncs assigned roll number records to an external Source DB.
 * Appends data to 'source_data' sheet of 1pZz0YkcTvWJnnDlBSguUIU4Ujc0aPf5119B7nX7TRZg
 */
function syncDataToSource(classFilter, sessionFilter, previewOnly, user, order, taskId, protectedColumns) {
  try {
    const sortOrder = order || 'roll_number';
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) {
      return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized: This feature is restricted to admin only." };
    }

    // Target Google Sheet ID as requested
    const targetSpreadsheetId = '1pZz0YkcTvWJnnDlBSguUIU4Ujc0aPf5119B7nX7TRZg';
    const targetSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(targetSpreadsheetId);
    const targetSheet = targetSs.getSheetByName('source_data');
    if (!targetSheet) {
      return { success: false, message: "Target sheet 'source_data' not found in external spreadsheet." };
    }

    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];

    // Get Session Config
    let session = '2025-26'; // Default
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.CONFIG);
    if (configSheet) {
      const configData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
      for (let r = 1; r < configData.length; r++) {
        if (configData[r][0] === 'session') {
          session = String(configData[r][1]);
          break;
        }
      }
    }

    const normalize = (str) => String(str || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().toLowerCase();
    const normalizedHeaders = headers.map(normalize);
    const getIdx = (name) => normalizedHeaders.indexOf(normalize(name));
    const getVal = (row, name) => {
      let idx = getIdx(name);
      return idx !== -1 ? String(row[idx] || '').trim() : '';
    };

    // --- PROTECTED COLUMNS LOGIC ---
    const protectedColsSet = new Set(Array.isArray(protectedColumns) ? protectedColumns : []);
    const isFieldProtected = (portalKey) => {
        if (!portalKey) return false;
        const pIdx = cIdxMap[portalKey];
        const pHeader = (pIdx !== undefined && pIdx !== -1) ? headers[pIdx] : null;
        return pHeader && protectedColsSet.has(pHeader);
    };
    // --- TARGET DATA OPTIMIZATION ---
    // User requested to absolutely NOT touch/read upto row 5664.
    const PROTECTED_ROWS = 5664; 
    const startTargetRow = PROTECTED_ROWS + 1;
    const targetLastRow = targetSheet.getLastRow();
    const targetLastCol = Math.max(targetSheet.getLastColumn(), 68);

    const targetHeadersRaw = targetSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, targetLastCol).getValues()[0];
    const targetHeadersNorm = targetHeadersRaw.map(normalize);
    const findTargetIdx = (names) => {
      for (let n of names) {
        let idx = targetHeadersNorm.indexOf(normalize(n));
        if (idx !== -1) return idx;
      }
      return -1;
    };

    const targetFormIdx = findTargetIdx(['Form Number', 'Form No.', 'Form No', 'form_no']);
    const targetRollIdx = findTargetIdx(['Class Roll No', 'Class Roll No.', 'Roll No', 'roll_no']);
    const targetSessIdx = findTargetIdx(['Session', 'Academic Session', 'session']);
    const targetClsIdx = findTargetIdx(['Class', 'Admission Year', 'Admission Category', 'class']);
    
    // Fallbacks to known positions if not found by name
    const fIdx = targetFormIdx !== -1 ? targetFormIdx : 1;
    const rIdx = targetRollIdx !== -1 ? targetRollIdx : 2;
    const sIdx = targetSessIdx !== -1 ? targetSessIdx : 7;
    const cIdx = targetClsIdx !== -1 ? targetClsIdx : 6;

    const managedColumns = 68;
    const totalTargetColumns = Math.max(targetHeadersRaw.length, managedColumns);
    const bpPhotoIdIdx = targetHeadersNorm.indexOf("photo_id");

    let targetDataChunk = [];
    if (targetLastRow >= startTargetRow) {
       targetDataChunk = targetSheet.getRange(startTargetRow, 1, targetLastRow - startTargetRow + 1, totalTargetColumns).getValues();
    }

    const targetFormMap = {}; // Maps existing indices RELATIVE to targetDataChunk
    for (let r = 0; r < targetDataChunk.length; r++) {
      const fNum = String(targetDataChunk[r][fIdx] || '').trim();
      const sVal = String(targetDataChunk[r][sIdx] || '').trim();
      if (fNum) targetFormMap[`${fNum}|${sVal}`] = r;
    }
    let updatedCount = 0;
    let appendedCount = 0;
    const recordsToAppend = [];
    const previewChanges = [];
    const alreadyPushedRows = []; // Now used for rows that would be UPDATED if not previewOnly

    if (taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 5, "Preparing source data headers and mapping...");

    const dataRows = data.slice(1);
    const getClsRank = (clsStr) => {
        const s = String(clsStr).toLowerCase();
        if (s.includes('9')) return 1;
        if (s.includes('10')) return 2;
        if (s.includes('11')) return 3;
        if (s.includes('12')) return 4;
        return 99;
    };

    // Helper for real-time progress
    let lastProgressTime = 0;
    let progressRowIdx = -1;
    function updateProgress(pct, msg) {
       const now = Date.now();
       if (now - lastProgressTime < 2500) return; // Prevent writing too often to save time
       lastProgressTime = now;
       
       if (configSheet) {
          if (progressRowIdx === -1) {
              const cData = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
              for (let r = 0; r < cData.length; r++) {
                 if (cData[r][0] === 'pushToSourceProgress') {
                    progressRowIdx = r + 1;
                    break;
                 }
              }
          }
          
          if (progressRowIdx === -1) {
              configSheet.appendRow(['pushToSourceProgress', `${pct}|${msg}`]);
              progressRowIdx = configSheet.getLastRow();
          } else {
              configSheet.getRange(progressRowIdx, 2).setValue(`${pct}|${msg}`);
          }
          SpreadsheetApp.flush();
       }
       if (taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, pct, msg);
    }
    updateProgress(5, "Analyzing source data...");

    // --- FIELD MAPPING --- Precompute indices for speed
    const colIndices = {};
    const getCachedIdx = (names) => {
        for (let n of names) {
            let idx = normalizedHeaders.indexOf(normalize(n));
            if (idx !== -1) return idx;
        }
        return -1;
    };
    const cIdxMap = {
        formNum: getCachedIdx(['Form Number', 'Form No.', 'Form No']),
        rollNo: getCachedIdx(['Class Roll No', 'Class Roll No.', 'Roll No', 'Roll Number']),
        studentClass: getCachedIdx(['Admission sought for class', 'Class to which admission is sought', 'Class']),
        session: getCachedIdx(['Session', 'Academic Session']),
        name: getCachedIdx(["Student's Name (as per school records)"]),
        streamRaw: getCachedIdx(['Stream opted in Class 11th', 'Stream', 'Stream/Faculty']),
        father: getCachedIdx(["Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)", "Father's Name"]),
        mother: getCachedIdx(["Mother's Name (as per school records)", "Mother's Name"]),
        dob: getCachedIdx(["DoB (as per school records)"]),
        pdfUrl: getCachedIdx(["PDF URL", "PDF_URL", "PDF Link"]),
        studentMob: getCachedIdx(['Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)']),
        parentMob: getCachedIdx(["Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)", "Parent's Mobile No."]),
        village: getCachedIdx(['Name of your village']),
        block: getCachedIdx(['Block']),
        tehsil: getCachedIdx(['Tehsil']),
        district: getCachedIdx(['District']),
        pincode: getCachedIdx(['PIN code']),
        stateUt: getCachedIdx(['State/UT']),
        gender: getCachedIdx(['Gender']),
        religion: getCachedIdx(['Religion']),
        disability: getCachedIdx(['Whether Any Disability']),
        disabilityType: getCachedIdx(['Type of Disability']),
        email1: getCachedIdx(['Email Address (Gmail Preferred - for updates)', 'Email Address']),
        email2: getCachedIdx(['E-mail ID']),
        aadhar: getCachedIdx(['Aadhar No.']),
        accNo: getCachedIdx(['Bank Account No.']),
        bankName: getCachedIdx(['Name of Bank']),
        ifsc: getCachedIdx(['IFSC code']),
        bloodGroup: getCachedIdx(['Blood Group']),
        height: getCachedIdx(['Height (cm)']),
        weight: getCachedIdx(['Weight (kg)']),
        socialCat: getCachedIdx(['Social category']),
        socioEcon: getCachedIdx(['Socio-economic category']),
        houseNo: getCachedIdx(['House No.']),
        vocPerc: getCachedIdx(['Percentage Obtained in Vocational Subject']),
        prevComplex: getCachedIdx(['Name of Previous Complex Head']),
        penNum: getCachedIdx(['PEN number (given by UDISE portal)']),
        remarks: getCachedIdx(['Remarks/Feedback (if any)']),
        photo: getCachedIdx(['Student Photo']),
        subs11_taken: getCachedIdx(['Subjects to be taken in Class 11th']),
        subs11_studied: getCachedIdx(['Subjects Studied in Class 11th']),
        prevReg10: getCachedIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 10th)']),
        prevBoard10: getCachedIdx(['Board (Class 10th)']),
        prevSchool10: getCachedIdx(['Name of Previous School (Class 10th)']),
        prevMarks10: getCachedIdx(['Total Marks Obtained in Class 10th']),
        prevMax10: getCachedIdx(['Total Max. Marks in Class 10th']),
        prevExamRoll10: getCachedIdx(['Exam Roll Number of Class 10th', 'Exam Roll No. (Class 10th)']),
        prevDiv10: getCachedIdx(['Division/Distinction (Class 10th)', 'Division Obtained in Class 10th', 'Overall Division/Grade Obtained in Class 10th']),
        ss12: getCachedIdx(['Stream & Subjects for Class 12th', 'Subjects to be taken in Class 12th', 'Class 12th Subjects']),
        prevReg11: getCachedIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 11th)']),
        prevBoard11: getCachedIdx(['Board (Class 11th)']),
        prevSchool11: getCachedIdx(['Name of Previous School (Class 11th)']),
        prevMarks11: getCachedIdx(['Total Marks Obtained in Class 11th']),
        prevMax11: getCachedIdx(['Total Max. Marks in Class 11th']),
        prevExamRoll11: getCachedIdx(['Exam Roll Number of Class 11th', 'Exam Roll No. (Class 11th)']),
        prevDiv11: getCachedIdx(['Division/Distinction (Class 11th)', 'Division Obtained in Class 11th', 'Overall Division/Grade Obtained in Class 11th']),
        subs9: getCachedIdx(['Subjects to be taken in Class 9th']),
        prevRegDiet: getCachedIdx(['DIET Registration No.']),
        prevBoard8: getCachedIdx(['Board (Class 8th)']),
        prevSchool8: getCachedIdx(['Name of Previous School (Class 8th)']),
        prevMarks8: getCachedIdx(['Total Marks Obtained in Class 8th']),
        prevMax8: getCachedIdx(['Total Max. Marks in Class 8th']),
        prevExamRoll8: getCachedIdx(['Exam Roll Number of Class 8th', 'Exam Roll No. (Class 8th)']),
        prevDiv8: getCachedIdx(['Division/Distinction (Class 8th)', 'Division Obtained in Class 8th', 'Overall Division/Grade Obtained in Class 8th']),
        subs10: getCachedIdx(['Subjects to be taken in Class 10th']),
        prevReg9: getCachedIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 9th)']),
        prevBoard9: getCachedIdx(['Board (Class 9th)']),
        prevSchool9: getCachedIdx(['Name of Previous School (Class 9th)']),
        prevMarks9: getCachedIdx(['Total Marks Obtained in Class 9th']),
        prevMax9: getCachedIdx(['Total Max. Marks in Class 9th']),
        prevExamRoll9: getCachedIdx(['Exam Roll Number of Class 9th', 'Exam Roll No. (Class 9th)']),
        prevDiv9: getCachedIdx(['Division/Distinction (Class 9th)', 'Division Obtained in Class 9th', 'Overall Division/Grade Obtained in Class 9th']),
        timestamp: getCachedIdx(['timestamp'])
    };

    const fastGet = (row, idx) => idx !== -1 ? String(row[idx] || '').trim() : '';

    // Process source rows - Run completely optimized
    const startDataIdx = 0; 
    
    for (let i = startDataIdx; i < dataRows.length; i++) {
        // Progress every 5 records or based on percentage
        if (i % 5 === 0) {
            const pct = 15 + Math.round((i / dataRows.length) * 75);
            updateProgress(pct, `Analyzing record ${i + 1} of ${dataRows.length}...`);
        }
        const row = dataRows[i];
        
        const formNum = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.formNum);
        const rollNo = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.rollNo);
        const studentClass = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.studentClass);
        const portalSession = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.session);
        const studentSession = portalSession || session;
        const name = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.name);
        const streamRaw = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.streamRaw);

        if (!formNum || !String(rollNo).trim()) continue;
        
        // [FIX] Handle classFilter/sessionFilter as Arrays (from multi-select) OR legacy strings
        const classFilterArr = Array.isArray(classFilter)
            ? classFilter
            : (classFilter && classFilter !== 'All' ? [classFilter] : []);
        const sessionFilterArr = Array.isArray(sessionFilter)
            ? sessionFilter
            : (sessionFilter && sessionFilter !== 'All' ? [sessionFilter] : []);

        if (classFilterArr.length > 0 && classFilterArr.length < 4) {
            // Check if studentClass matches any of the selected classes
            const classMatches = classFilterArr.some(cf => {
                const numMatch = String(cf).match(/\d+/);
                if (numMatch) return studentClass.includes(numMatch[0]);
                return studentClass.replace(/[\s\(\)]/g, '').toLowerCase().includes(String(cf).toLowerCase());
            });
            if (!classMatches) continue;
        }
        if (sessionFilterArr.length > 0 && !sessionFilterArr.includes(studentSession)) continue;

        // --- FIELD EXTRACTION FROM CACHE ---
        const father = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.father);
        const mother = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.mother);
        const dob = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.dob);
        const pdfUrl = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.pdfUrl);
        const studentMob = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.studentMob);
        const parentMob = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.parentMob);
        const village = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.village);
        const block = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.block);
        const tehsil = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.tehsil);
        const district = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.district);
        const pincode = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.pincode);
        const stateUt = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.stateUt);
        const gender = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.gender);
        const religion = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.religion);
        const disability = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.disability);
        const disabilityType = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.disabilityType);
        const email1 = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.email1);
        const email2 = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.email2);
        const aadhar = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.aadhar);
        const accNo = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.accNo);
        const bankName = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.bankName);
        const ifsc = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.ifsc);
        const bloodGroup = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.bloodGroup);
        const height = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.height);
        const weight = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.weight);
        const socialCat = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.socialCat);
        const socioEcon = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.socioEcon);
        const houseNo = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.houseNo);
        const vocPerc = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.vocPerc);
        const prevComplex = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevComplex);
        const penNum = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.penNum);
        const remarks = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.remarks);
        const photo = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.photo);

        let stream = streamRaw, subjectsStr = '', prevSchool = '', prevMarks = '', prevMax = '', prevReg = '', prevBoard = '', prevExamRoll = '', prevDiv = '';
        if (studentClass.includes('11')) {
            subjectsStr = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.subs11_taken) || fastGet(row, cIdxMap.subs11_studied);
            prevReg = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevReg10);
            prevBoard = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevBoard10);
            prevSchool = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevSchool10);
            prevMarks = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMarks10);
            prevMax = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMax10);
            prevExamRoll = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevExamRoll10);
            prevDiv = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevDiv10);
        } else if (studentClass.includes('12')) {
            // User confirmed for 12th admission, subjects are in "Subjects Studied in Class 11th"
            subjectsStr = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.subs11_studied);
            
            // Extract stream from prefixes if present (e.g., "Medical: Physics, Chemistry...")
            if (subjectsStr.includes(':')) {
                const parts = subjectsStr.split(':');
                if (!stream) stream = parts[0].trim();
                subjectsStr = parts.slice(1).join(':').trim();
            } 
            
            if (!stream) {
                const s_low_12 = subjectsStr.toLowerCase();
                const hasPhy = /physics|\bph\b/i.test(s_low_12);
                const hasChe = /chemistry|\bch\b/i.test(s_low_12);
                if (hasPhy && hasChe) stream = 'Science';
                else if (subjectsStr) stream = 'Humanities';
            }
            
            prevReg = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevReg11);
            prevBoard = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevBoard11);
            prevSchool = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevSchool11);
            prevMarks = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMarks11);
            prevMax = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMax11);
            prevExamRoll = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevExamRoll11);
            prevDiv = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevDiv11);
        } else if (studentClass.includes('9')) {
            subjectsStr = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.subs9);
            prevReg = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevRegDiet);
            prevBoard = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevBoard8);
            prevSchool = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevSchool8);
            prevMarks = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMarks8);
            prevMax = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMax8);
            prevExamRoll = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevExamRoll8);
            prevDiv = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevDiv8);
        } else if (studentClass.includes('10')) {
            subjectsStr = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.subs10);
            prevReg = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevReg9);
            prevBoard = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevBoard9);
            prevSchool = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevSchool9);
            prevMarks = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMarks9);
            prevMax = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevMax9);
            prevExamRoll = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevExamRoll9);
            prevDiv = fastGet(row, cIdxMap.prevDiv9);
        }

        // Global Stream Fallback if not set by class logic
        if (!stream && subjectsStr) {
            const s_low = subjectsStr.toLowerCase();
            if (/social studies|\bss\b/i.test(s_low)) stream = 'General';
            else if (/physics|chemistry|\bph\b|\bch\b/i.test(s_low)) stream = 'Science';
            else stream = 'Humanities';
        }
        if (!stream) stream = 'Humanities'; // Final fallback

        let p_marks = parseFloat(prevMarks), p_max = parseFloat(prevMax);
        let prevPerc = (!isNaN(p_marks) && !isNaN(p_max) && p_max > 0) ? ((p_marks / p_max) * 100).toFixed(2) : '';
        if (prevPerc !== '') {
            const p = parseFloat(prevPerc);
            if (p >= 75) prevDiv = 'Distinction';
            else if (p >= 60) prevDiv = '1st Division';
            else if (p >= 50) prevDiv = '2nd Division';
            else if (p >= 33) prevDiv = '3rd Division';
            else prevDiv = 'Fail';
        }

        const abbrMap = { 'ACCOUNTANCY': 'AY', 'ARABIC': 'AR', 'BIOLOGY': 'BI', 'BIOTECHNOLOGY': 'BT', 'BUSINESS STUDIES': 'BS', 'CHEMISTRY': 'CH', 'ECONOMICS': 'EC', 'EDUCATION': 'ED', 'ENTREPRENEURSHIP': 'EP', 'ENVIRONMENTAL SCIENCE': 'ES', 'GLOBAL ECONOMY': 'GE', 'GENERAL ENGLISH': 'GE', 'ENGLISH': 'EN', 'GEOGRAPHY': 'GG', 'GEOLOGY': 'GO', 'HEALTHCARE': 'HTC', 'HISTORY': 'HT', 'IT AND ITES': 'ITE', 'MATHEMATICS': 'MA', 'PERSIAN': 'PE', 'PHYSICAL EDUCATION': 'PD', 'PHYSICS': 'PH', 'POLITICAL SCIENCE': 'PS', 'PSYCHOLOGY': 'PY', 'SOCIOLOGY': 'SO', 'STATISTICS': 'SS', 'TOURISM': 'TOU', 'TOURISM AND HOSPITALITY': 'TH', 'TYPEWRITING AND SHORTHAND': 'TS', 'URDU': 'UR', 'SOCIAL STUDIES': 'SS', 'SCIENCE': 'SC' };
        const subArrRaw = subjectsStr.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(s => s);
        const finalSubsStr = subArrRaw.map(s => abbrMap[s.toUpperCase()] || s).join(', ');

        const tsRaw = cIdxMap.timestamp !== -1 ? row[cIdxMap.timestamp] : '';
        let ts = '';
        if (tsRaw instanceof Date && !isNaN(tsRaw)) {
            const pad = n => n.toString().padStart(2, '0');
            ts = `${pad(tsRaw.getDate())}-${pad(tsRaw.getMonth()+1)}-${tsRaw.getFullYear()} ${pad(tsRaw.getHours())}:${pad(tsRaw.getMinutes())}:${pad(tsRaw.getSeconds())}`;
        } else ts = String(tsRaw || '');

        // --- UPSERT LOGIC ---
        const lookupKey = `${String(formNum).trim()}|${String(studentSession).trim()}`;
        const existingIdx = targetFormMap[lookupKey];
        let mappedRow;
        
        if (existingIdx !== undefined) {
            // Update existing row: start with a copy of current data to preserve unmanaged columns
            mappedRow = [...targetDataChunk[existingIdx]];
        } else {
            // New record: initialize with empty values
            mappedRow = new Array(totalTargetColumns).fill('');
        }

        // --- COMMITTEE UPDATES WITH PROTECTION ---
        const setM = (tIdx, val, pKey) => {
            if (existingIdx !== undefined && isFieldProtected(pKey) && String(mappedRow[tIdx]).trim() !== "") return;
            mappedRow[tIdx] = val;
        };

        if (formNum) setM(fIdx, formNum, 'formNum');
        setM(rIdx, rollNo, 'rollNo');
        setM(3, ts, 'timestamp');
        setM(cIdx, studentClass, 'studentClass');
        
        // Session Logic: Keep existing if portal is empty
        if (portalSession) {
          setM(sIdx, portalSession, 'session');
        } else if (!mappedRow[sIdx]) {
          setM(sIdx, studentSession, 'session');
        }

        if (prevReg) {
            const regKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevReg10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevReg11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevRegDiet' : 'prevReg9'));
            setM(8, prevReg, regKey);
        }
        
        const boardKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevBoard10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevBoard11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevBoard8' : 'prevBoard9'));
        setM(9, boardKey, boardKey); 

        setM(10, name, 'name'); 
        setM(11, father, 'father'); 
        setM(12, mother, 'mother');
        setM(13, dob, 'dob'); 
        setM(15, village, 'village'); 
        setM(16, block, 'block'); 
        setM(17, tehsil, 'tehsil');
        setM(18, district, 'district'); 
        setM(19, pincode, 'pincode'); 
        setM(20, stateUt, 'stateUt');
        
        // Combined Residence/Address
        const cleanStr = (s) => String(s || '').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
        const v_c = cleanStr(village), d_c = cleanStr(district);
        const combinedAddress = (v_c && d_c) ? `${v_c}, ${d_c}` : (v_c || d_c);
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && (isFieldProtected('village') || isFieldProtected('district')) && String(mappedRow[21]).trim() !== "")) {
            mappedRow[21] = combinedAddress;
        }

        setM(22, gender, 'gender'); 
        setM(23, socialCat, 'socialCat'); 
        setM(24, religion, 'religion');
        setM(25, disability, 'disability'); 
        setM(26, disabilityType, 'disabilityType'); 
        setM(27, stream, 'streamRaw');

        const subsKeys = studentClass.includes('11') ? ['subs11_taken', 'subs11_studied'] : (studentClass.includes('12') ? ['ss12'] : (studentClass.includes('9') ? ['subs9'] : ['subs10']));
        const isSubsProtected = () => subsKeys.some(k => isFieldProtected(k));
        
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[28]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[28] = subArrRaw[0] || ''; 
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[29]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[29] = subArrRaw[1] || ''; 
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[30]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[30] = subArrRaw[2] || '';
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[31]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[31] = subArrRaw[3] || ''; 
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[32]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[32] = subArrRaw[4] || ''; 
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[33]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[33] = subArrRaw[5] || '';
        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isSubsProtected() && String(mappedRow[34]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[34] = finalSubsStr;

        setM(35, email1, 'email1'); 
        setM(36, email2, 'email2');
        setM(37, studentMob, 'studentMob'); 
        setM(38, parentMob, 'parentMob'); 
        setM(39, aadhar, 'aadhar');
        setM(40, accNo, 'accNo'); 
        setM(41, bankName, 'bankName'); 
        setM(42, ifsc, 'ifsc');
        setM(43, bloodGroup, 'bloodGroup'); 
        setM(44, height, 'height'); 
        setM(45, weight, 'weight');
