        setM(46, socialCat, 'socialCat'); 
        setM(47, socioEcon, 'socioEcon'); 
        setM(48, houseNo, 'houseNo');
        setM(49, vocPerc, 'vocPerc'); 
        setM(50, prevComplex, 'prevComplex'); 
        setM(51, penNum, 'penNum');
        
        const prevSchoolKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevSchool10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevSchool11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevSchool8' : 'prevSchool9'));
        setM(52, prevSchool, prevSchoolKey); 

        const prevRollKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevExamRoll10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevExamRoll11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevExamRoll8' : 'prevExamRoll9'));
        setM(55, prevExamRoll, prevRollKey); 

        const prevMarksKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevMarks10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevMarks11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevMarks8' : 'prevMarks9'));
        setM(56, prevMarks, prevMarksKey);

        const prevMaxKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevMax10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevMax11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevMax8' : 'prevMax9'));
        setM(57, prevMax, prevMaxKey); 

        if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && (isFieldProtected(prevMarksKey) || isFieldProtected(prevMaxKey)) && String(mappedRow[58]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[58] = prevPerc; 
        
        const prevDivKey = studentClass.includes('11') ? 'prevDiv10' : (studentClass.includes('12') ? 'prevDiv11' : (studentClass.includes('9') ? 'prevDiv8' : 'prevDiv9'));
        setM(59, prevDiv, prevDivKey); 

        setM(66, remarks, 'remarks');
        
        if (bpPhotoIdIdx !== -1) {
            if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isFieldProtected('Student Photo') && String(mappedRow[bpPhotoIdIdx]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[bpPhotoIdIdx] = photo;
        } else if (managedColumns > 67) {
            if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isFieldProtected('Student Photo') && String(mappedRow[67]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[67] = photo; 
        }
        
        const pdfIdxFind = targetHeadersRaw.findIndex(h => String(h).toUpperCase().includes("PDF"));
        if (pdfIdxFind !== -1) {
            if (!(existingIdx !== undefined && isFieldProtected('PDF URL') && String(mappedRow[pdfIdxFind]).trim() !== "")) mappedRow[pdfIdxFind] = pdfUrl;
        }

        // --- COMMIT CHANGES ---
        if (existingIdx !== undefined) {
            targetDataChunk[existingIdx] = mappedRow;
            updatedCount++;
            if (previewOnly) {
                previewChanges.push({ form: formNum, roll: rollNo, name: name, cls: studentClass, session: studentSession, stream: stream, status: 'Updated' });
            }
        } else {
            recordsToAppend.push(mappedRow);
            appendedCount++;
            if (previewOnly) {
                previewChanges.push({ form: formNum, roll: rollNo, name: name, cls: studentClass, session: studentSession, stream: stream, status: 'New' });
            }
        }
    }

    if (previewOnly) {
        const sorter = (a, b) => {
            if (sortOrder === 'form_number') return (parseInt(a.form) || 0) - (parseInt(b.form) || 0);
            if (sortOrder === 'name') return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
            const rA = parseFloat(a.roll), rB = parseFloat(b.roll);
            if (!isNaN(rA) && !isNaN(rB)) return rA - rB;
            return String(a.roll).localeCompare(String(b.roll), undefined, {numeric: true});
        };
        previewChanges.sort(sorter);
        return {
          success: true, previewMode: true, count: appendedCount + updatedCount,
          data: previewChanges, appended: appendedCount, updated: updatedCount
       };
    }

    // --- APPLY CHANGES TO TARGET DATA CHUNK ---
    let finalData = targetDataChunk.concat(recordsToAppend);

    // Sort ONLY the newly appended/updated data portion
    finalData.sort((a, b) => {
        if (sortOrder === 'form_number') {
            return (parseInt(a[fIdx]) || 0) - (parseInt(b[fIdx]) || 0);
        } else if (sortOrder === 'name') {
            return String(a[10]).localeCompare(String(b[10]), undefined, {numeric: true});
        } else {
            const sessA = String(a[sIdx] || ''), sessB = String(b[sIdx] || '');
            if (sessA !== sessB) return sessA.localeCompare(sessB, undefined, {numeric: true});
            
            const rankA = getClsRank(a[cIdx]), rankB = getClsRank(b[cIdx]);
            if (rankA !== rankB) return rankA - rankB;
            
            const rA = parseFloat(a[rIdx]), rB = parseFloat(b[rIdx]);
            if (!isNaN(rA) && !isNaN(rB)) return rA - rB;
            return String(a[rIdx]).localeCompare(String(b[rIdx]), undefined, {numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base'});
        }
    });

    let nextSNo = PROTECTED_ROWS + 1; // E.g., starts at 5665
    // Final serial numbers and formula assignment
    for (let i = 0; i < finalData.length; i++) {
        finalData[i][0] = nextSNo++;
        finalData[i][14] = '=textDateToWords(N' + (startTargetRow + i) + ')'; // Assign custom formula purely for valid subset!
    }
    
    updateProgress(95, "Committing changes to external sheet...");
    // Clear purely the active region and rewrite only active subset
    const numRowsToRemove = Math.max(0, targetSheet.getLastRow() - startTargetRow + 1);
    if (numRowsToRemove > 0) {
        targetSheet.getRange(startTargetRow, 1, numRowsToRemove, totalTargetColumns).clearContent();
    }
    if (finalData.length > 0) {
        targetSheet.getRange(startTargetRow, 1, finalData.length, totalTargetColumns).setValues(finalData);
        targetSheet.getRange(startTargetRow, 1, finalData.length, totalTargetColumns).clearDataValidations();
    }
    
    // Clear progress
    updateProgress(100, "Sync complete");

    // [NEW] Trigger real-time sync to practicals portal
    syncToPracticalsList();

    return {
      success: true,
      message: `Successfully synchronized data to protected database. Found ${updatedCount} existing and appended ${appendedCount} new records.`
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'syncDataToSource');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Real-time Sync to Practicals list Sheet
 * Automatically updates the list sheet in the Practicals portal whenever data is pushed to source_data.
 */
function syncToPracticalsList() {
  try {
    const sourceSsId = '1pZz0YkcTvWJnnDlBSguUIU4Ujc0aPf5119B7nX7TRZg';
    const sourceSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(sourceSsId);
    const sourceSheet = sourceSs.getSheetByName('source_data');
    if (!sourceSheet) return;

    const sourceData = sourceSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (sourceData.length < 2) return;

    const practicalsSsId = '1EafSDSambzX5E_Am7pDzjL6lnPtqKW3Id3KhatWfcDI';
    const practicalsSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(practicalsSsId);
    let listSheet = practicalsSs.getSheetByName('list');
    if (!listSheet) {
        listSheet = practicalsSs.insertSheet('list');
    }

    const headers = sourceData[0].map(h => String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase());
    const findIdx = (terms) => {
      for (const term of terms) {
        const i = headers.indexOf(term.toLowerCase());
        if (i !== -1) return i;
      }
      for (const term of terms) {
        const i = headers.findIndex(h => h.includes(term.toLowerCase()));
        if (i !== -1) return i;
      }
      return -1;
    };

    const idx = {
      roll: findIdx(['class roll no', 'class r.no.', 'roll no', 'r.no']),
      examRoll: findIdx(['exam r.no. (current)', 'exam r.no.', 'exam roll no', 'exam roll', 'board roll']),
      name: findIdx(['student\'s name', 'name']),
      father: findIdx(['father\'s name', 'father name']),
      class: findIdx(['class', 'grade']),
      session: findIdx(['session', 'academic session']),
      mobile: findIdx(['mobile', 'phone', 'contact']),
      sub1: findIdx(['subjects1', 'subject 1', 'sub1']),
      sub2: findIdx(['subjects2', 'subject 2', 'sub2']),
      sub3: findIdx(['subjects3', 'subject 3', 'sub3']),
      sub4: findIdx(['subjects4', 'subject 4', 'sub4']),
      sub5: findIdx(['subjects5', 'subject 5', 'sub5'])
    };

    const listData = [];
    listData.push(['class roll no', 'exam roll no', 'name', 'fathers name', 'subjects (comma separated)', 'session', 'class', 'mobile']);

    for (let i = 1; i < sourceData.length; i++) {
      const row = sourceData[i];
      const rollNo = idx.roll !== -1 ? String(row[idx.roll] || '').trim() : '';
      if (!rollNo) continue;

      let sessionVal = idx.session !== -1 ? String(row[idx.session] || '').trim() : '';
      const sessionMatch = sessionVal.match(/-(\d{2})$/);
      if (sessionMatch) {
          sessionVal = "20" + sessionMatch[1];
      }

      // Aggregate subjects
      const subArr = [];
      if (idx.sub1 !== -1 && row[idx.sub1]) subArr.push(String(row[idx.sub1]).trim());
      if (idx.sub2 !== -1 && row[idx.sub2]) subArr.push(String(row[idx.sub2]).trim());
      if (idx.sub3 !== -1 && row[idx.sub3]) subArr.push(String(row[idx.sub3]).trim());
      if (idx.sub4 !== -1 && row[idx.sub4]) subArr.push(String(row[idx.sub4]).trim());
      if (idx.sub5 !== -1 && row[idx.sub5]) subArr.push(String(row[idx.sub5]).trim());
      const subjectsJoined = subArr.filter(s => s !== '').join(', ');

      listData.push([
        rollNo,
        idx.examRoll !== -1 ? row[idx.examRoll] : '',
        idx.name !== -1 ? row[idx.name] : '',
        idx.father !== -1 ? row[idx.father] : '',
        subjectsJoined,
        sessionVal,    
        idx.class !== -1 ? row[idx.class] : '',
        idx.mobile !== -1 ? row[idx.mobile] : ''
      ]);
    }

    // Clear and write to practicals list sheet
    listSheet.clearContents();
    if (listData.length > 0) {
        listSheet.getRange(1, 1, listData.length, listData[0].length).setValues(listData);
    }
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log("Sync to Practicals failed: " + e.message);
  }
}



function getPushProgress() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName('adm_config') || ss.getSheetByName('Config');
  if (configSheet) {
    const data = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    for (let r = 0; r < data.length; r++) {
      if (data[r][0] === 'pushToSourceProgress') {
        const parts = String(data[r][1]).split('|');
        return { pct: parseInt(parts[0], 10) || 0, msg: parts[1] || '' };
      }
    }
  }
  return { pct: 0, msg: '' };
}

/**
 * [NEW] Syncs filtered student data to the Automation Portal spreadsheet.
 */
function syncDataToAutomation(classFilter, sessionFilter, user, taskId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) return { success: false, message: "Unauthorized." };

    const automationSheetId = SCHOOL_PORTALS.automation.sheetId;
    const targetSs = SpreadsheetApp.openById(automationSheetId);
    const targetSheet = targetSs.getSheetByName('Sheet1');
    if (!targetSheet) return { success: false, message: "Target sheet not found." };

    const admSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = admSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const normalizedHeaders = headers.map(h => String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase());

    const getIdx = (names) => {
      for (let n of names) {
        let idx = normalizedHeaders.indexOf(n.toLowerCase());
        if (idx !== -1) return idx;
      }
      return -1;
    };

    const idxMap = {
      formNo: getIdx(['Form Number', 'Form No.', 'Form No']),
      classRollNo: getIdx(['Class Roll No', 'Class Roll No.', 'Roll No', 'Roll Number']),
      studentClass: getIdx(['Admission sought for class', 'Class to which admission is sought', 'Class']),
      session: getIdx(['Session', 'Academic Session']),
      name: getIdx(["Student's Name (as per school records)"]),
      regDiet: getIdx(['DIET Registration No.']),
      reg9: getIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 9th)']),
      reg10: getIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 10th)']),
      reg11: getIdx(['Board Registration No. (Class 11th)']),
      roll10: getIdx(["Exam Roll Number of Class 10th", "Exam Roll No. (Class 10th)"]),
      roll11: getIdx(["Exam Roll Number of Class 11th", "Exam Roll No. (Class 11th)"])
    };

    const fastGet = (row, idx) => idx !== -1 ? String(row[idx] || '').trim() : '';
    const recordsToPush = [];
    const classFilterArr = Array.isArray(classFilter) ? classFilter : (classFilter ? [classFilter] : []);
    const sessionFilterArr = Array.isArray(sessionFilter) ? sessionFilter : (sessionFilter ? [sessionFilter] : []);

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (i % 20 === 0 && taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 10 + (i / data.length) * 80, `Filtering... ${i}`);
        const row = data[i];
        const studentClass = fastGet(row, idxMap.studentClass);
        const studentSession = fastGet(row, idxMap.session);
        
        // Revised Logic: Use previous class roll/reg for 11th and 12th
        let rollNo = "";
        let regNo = "";

        if (studentClass.includes("11")) {
            rollNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.roll10);
            regNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.reg11) || fastGet(row, idxMap.reg10); // JKBOSE reg is usually same
        } else if (studentClass.includes("12")) {
            rollNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.roll11);
            regNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.reg11);
        } else {
            rollNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.classRollNo);
            if (studentClass.includes("9")) regNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.regDiet);
            else if (studentClass.includes("10")) regNo = fastGet(row, idxMap.reg9);
        }

        if (classFilterArr.length > 0 && !classFilterArr.some(cf => studentClass.includes(cf.replace(/\D/g,'')))) continue;
        if (sessionFilterArr.length > 0 && !sessionFilterArr.includes(studentSession)) continue;
        if (!rollNo || rollNo === '-' || rollNo === '') continue;

        recordsToPush.push({ rollNo, regNo, name: fastGet(row, idxMap.name), session: studentSession, class: studentClass, formNo: fastGet(row, idxMap.formNo) });
    }

    if (recordsToPush.length === 0) return { success: false, message: "No data found." };
    if (taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 90, "Updating...");

    // Force text format in target sheet
    targetSheet.getRange("B:B").setNumberFormat("@");
    targetSheet.getRange("H:H").setNumberFormat("@");

    const targetData = targetSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const targetMap = {};
    for (let j = 1; j < targetData.length; j++) {
        const key = `${targetData[j][0]}|${targetData[j][4]}|${targetData[j][3]}`.toLowerCase();
        targetMap[key] = j + 1;
    }

    recordsToPush.forEach(rec => {
        const key = `${rec.rollNo}|${rec.class}|${rec.session}`.toLowerCase();
        const safeReg = "'" + rec.regNo;
        const safeForm = "'" + rec.formNo;

        if (targetMap[key]) {
            const rowIdx = targetMap[key];
            targetSheet.getRange(rowIdx, 1, 1, 5).setValues([[rec.rollNo, safeReg, rec.name, rec.session, rec.class]]);
            targetSheet.getRange(rowIdx, 8).setValue(safeForm);
        } else {
            targetSheet.appendRow([rec.rollNo, safeReg, rec.name, rec.session, rec.class, '', '', safeForm]);
        }
    });

    logActivity('Push to Automation', user, `Synced ${recordsToPush.length} records.`);
    return { success: true, message: `Pushed ${recordsToPush.length} records.` };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'syncDataToAutomation');
    return { success: false, message: "Failed: " + e.message };
  }
}


/**
 * Updates admin tab permissions in the Users sheet.
 * Stores permissions as a JSON string in a new 'Permissions' column.
 */
function updateAdminTabPermissions(permissions, user) {
  try {
    const SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL = 'adm.exam.hss.shangus@gmail.com';
    if (String(user.email).toLowerCase() !== SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: Only super admin can change permissions.');
    }

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    let userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    if (!userSheet) throw new Error('Users sheet not found.');
    
    const data = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    
    // Find or create 'Permissions' column
    let permColIdx = headers.indexOf('Permissions');
    if (permColIdx === -1) {
      permColIdx = headers.length;
      userSheet.getRange(1, permColIdx + 1).setValue('Permissions');
      // Refresh headers
      headers.push('Permissions');
    }

    let emailColIdx = headers.indexOf('EmailAddress');
    if (emailColIdx === -1) emailColIdx = headers.indexOf('Email');
    if (emailColIdx === -1) throw new Error('Email column not found in Users sheet.');

    // Process each user row
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        const email = String(data[i][emailColIdx]).toLowerCase().trim();
        if (permissions[email]) {
            const permJson = JSON.stringify(permissions[email]);
            userSheet.getRange(i + 1, permColIdx + 1).setValue(permJson);
        }
    }

    try { logActivity('Update Web Permissions', user.email, `Updated permissions for ${Object.keys(permissions).length} users`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'updateAdminTabPermissions');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Saves general admin settings to the Config sheet.
 */
function saveAdminSettings(settings, user) {
  try {
    const SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL = 'adm.exam.hss.shangus@gmail.com';
    if (String(user.email).toLowerCase() !== SUPER_ADMIN_EMAIL) {
      throw new Error('Unauthorized: Only super admin can change settings.');
    }

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const configSheet = ss.getSheetByName('adm_config') || ss.getSheetByName('Config');
    if (!configSheet) throw new Error('Configuration sheet not found.');

    const data = configSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    
    for (const key in settings) {
      let found = false;
      for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (String(data[i][0]).toLowerCase() === key.toLowerCase()) {
          configSheet.getRange(i + 1, 2).setValue(settings[key]);
          found = true;
          break;
        }
      }
      if (!found) {
        configSheet.appendRow([key, settings[key]]);
      }
    }

    try { logActivity('Update Admin Settings', user.email, `Updated ${Object.keys(settings).length} settings`); } catch (e) {}
    return { success: true };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'saveAdminSettings');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Zips a Google Drive folder and returns a base64 string for download.
 */
function getFolderAsZip(folderId, fileName) {
  try {
    const folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId);
    const files = folder.getFiles();
    const blobs = [];
    while (files.hasNext()) {
      try { blobs.push(files.next().getBlob()); } catch (err) {}
    }
    if (blobs.length === 0) throw new Error('No files in folder');
    const zipName = (fileName || folder.getName()) + '.zip';
    const zipBlob = Utilities.zip(blobs, zipName);
    return { success: true, base64: Utilities.base64Encode(zipBlob.getBytes()), fileName: zipName };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getFolderAsZip');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Resets roll numbers in admissions sheet.
 */
function resetRollNumbers(params, user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Permission denied');
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    const rollIdx = headers.indexOf('Class Roll No');
    if (rollIdx === -1) throw new Error('Roll No column not found');
    const formIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');
    const sessionIdx = headers.indexOf('Session');
    const classIdx = headers.indexOf('Admission sought for class');

    const p = params || {};
    const formNumbers = p.formNumbers || [];
    const filterClass = p.class || '';
    const filterSession = p.session || '';
    const resetAll = !formNumbers.length && !filterClass;

    let resetCount = 0;
    const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 2) return { success: true, count: 0 };

    const rollRange = sheet.getRange(1, rollIdx + 1, lastRow, 1);
    const rollValues = rollRange.getValues();

    const sessionHeaderIdx = headers.indexOf('Session');
    const classHeaderIdx = headers.indexOf('Admission sought for class');
    const formHeaderIdx = headers.indexOf('Form Number');

    if (rollIdx === -1 || formHeaderIdx === -1) throw new Error('Required columns not found');

    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        let shouldReset = false;
        const currentRollNo = String(data[i][rollIdx] || '').trim();
        const isCurrentlyUnassigned = !currentRollNo || currentRollNo === '-';

        if (p.onlyUnassigned) {
            // Special Case: Reset/Validate for those who don't have roll numbers? 
            // Actually, if we are in "Not Assigned" filter, we might want to clear them specifically or just ignore.
            // But if the user clicks "Reset All" while in "Not Assigned", they might expect a different result.
            // For now, let's make it work as: reset only if it's currently unassigned (which does nothing to the value, but we can log it).
            // Actually, if the user picks 'Not Assigned', resetAll is false.
            if (isCurrentlyUnassigned) {
               if (filterSession && sessionHeaderIdx !== -1) {
                   shouldReset = String(data[i][sessionHeaderIdx]) === filterSession;
               } else {
                   shouldReset = true;
               }
            }
        } else if (resetAll) {
            shouldReset = true;
        } else if (formNumbers.length > 0) {
            shouldReset = formNumbers.map(String).includes(String(data[i][formHeaderIdx]));
        } else if (filterClass && filterSession && classHeaderIdx !== -1 && sessionHeaderIdx !== -1) {
            shouldReset = String(data[i][classHeaderIdx]) === filterClass && String(data[i][sessionHeaderIdx]) === filterSession;
        }

        if (shouldReset) {
            rollValues[i][0] = ''; // Clear roll number
            resetCount++;
        }
    }

    if (resetCount > 0) {
        rollRange.setValues(rollValues);
        try { 
            const userEmail = (user && user.email) ? user.email : 'Unknown Admin';
            logActivity('Reset Roll Numbers', userEmail, `Cleared ${resetCount} roll numbers` + (p.onlyUnassigned ? ' (Filter: Not Assigned)' : (resetAll ? ' (All)' : ''))); 
        } catch (e) {}
    }
    return { success: true, count: resetCount };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'resetRollNumbers');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Generate ID PDFs for a range of roll numbers - BATCH PROCESSING VERSION
 * Processes in batches to avoid 6-minute timeout
 */
function generateIdPdfsInRange(requestData, user) {
  try {
    const { 
      classFilter, startRoll, endRoll, taskId,
      batchSize = 25, continuationToken = null, folderId = null, timestamp = null, totalProcessed = 0 
    } = requestData || {};

    // Normalize classFilter to an array
    let classFilterArr = [];
    if (Array.isArray(classFilter)) {
      classFilterArr = classFilter.filter(Boolean);
    } else if (classFilter && classFilter !== 'All' && classFilter !== '') {
      classFilterArr = [String(classFilter)];
    }
    
    const classLabel = classFilterArr.length ? classFilterArr.join('+') : 'All';
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error('Unauthorized');
    
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];
    
    const getIdx = (name) => headers.indexOf(name);
    const idxClass = getIdx('Admission sought for class');
    const idxRoll = getIdx('Class Roll No');
    const idxForm = getIdx('Form Number');

    if (idxClass === -1 || idxRoll === -1 || idxForm === -1) throw new Error('Required columns missing.');

    const templates = {
      '9th': '1BXIkjuOl1DmXz_1GAENLDyL1C-XSKnUycMFHWmRDlB4',
      '10th': '1Ei1RV4OKW3XudPQYlX-HHdUTqQgyeEALCkxF_jo2Xrs',
      '11th': '1_cCDb7PZtAnIvbh42AD0RRkhUSLlX0WQrzOKGlG4wuY',
      '12th': '1nllKjc0Al_SecjOb5hejgQDu1hFYlaT5tZm1TV83Bi0'
    };

    // Filter eligible students
    const eligibleStudents = [];
    for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
      const cls = String(data[r][idxClass] || '');
      const roll = parseFloat(data[r][idxRoll]);
      
      if (isNaN(roll)) continue;
      if (classFilterArr.length > 0 && !classFilterArr.some(cf => cls.includes(cf))) continue;
      if (startRoll !== null && roll < startRoll) continue;
      if (endRoll !== null && roll > endRoll) continue;

      let templateId = '';
      if (cls.includes('9')) templateId = templates['9th'];
      else if (cls.includes('10')) templateId = templates['10th'];
      else if (cls.includes('11')) templateId = templates['11th'];
      else if (cls.includes('12')) templateId = templates['12th'];
      
      if (templateId) {
        const appData = {};
        headers.forEach((h, i) => appData[h] = data[r][i]);
        eligibleStudents.push({ appData, templateId });
      }
    }

    const totalStudents = eligibleStudents.length;
    if (totalStudents === 0) throw new Error('No students found in the specified range. Please check if roll numbers are assigned and status is "Submitted".');

    const startIndex = continuationToken ? parseInt(continuationToken) : 0;
    
    // [MODIFIED] Sort by Class then Roll No to ensure strict batch order
    eligibleStudents.sort((a, b) => {
        const clsA = String(a.appData['Admission sought for class'] || '');
        const clsB = String(b.appData['Admission sought for class'] || '');
        if (clsA !== clsB) {
            return clsA.localeCompare(clsB, undefined, { numeric: true, sensitivity: 'base' });
        }
        
        const rollA = parseFloat(a.appData['Class Roll No']) || 0;
        const rollB = parseFloat(b.appData['Class Roll No']) || 0;
        if (rollA !== rollB) {
            return rollA - rollB;
        }
        
        // Tie-breaker: Form Number (Essential for stable paging across multiple server calls)
        const formA = parseFloat(a.appData['Form Number']) || 0;
        const formB = parseFloat(b.appData['Form Number']) || 0;
        return formA - formB;
    });

    const abortKey = taskId ? 'abort_' + taskId : null;

    // Initialize/Refresh folder
    const centralBatchFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.batchFolderId);
    let currentTimestamp = timestamp || new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-').slice(0, 19);
    let folder;
    
    if (folderId) {
      folder = DriveApp.getFolderById(folderId);
    } else {
      // [NEW] Attempt to locate recently generated folder for these classes (within 30 days)
      const cutoffDate = new Date(Date.now() - 30 * 24 * 3600 * 1000);
      const searchFolders = centralBatchFolder.getFolders();
      let existingFolder = null;
      const expectedPrefix = `ID_Cards_${classLabel}_`;
      
      while (searchFolders.hasNext()) {
        const f = searchFolders.next();
        if (f.getName().startsWith(expectedPrefix) && f.getDateCreated() > cutoffDate) {
          existingFolder = f;
          break;
        }
      }

      if (existingFolder) {
        folder = existingFolder;
      } else {
        folder = centralBatchFolder.createFolder(`ID_Cards_${classLabel}_${currentTimestamp}`);
        makePublic(folder);
      }
    }

    // [OPTIMIZED] Process as many as possible within 4.5 minutes
    const serverStartTime = Date.now();
    const TIME_LIMIT = 4.5 * 60 * 1000;
    // [NEW] Multi-run prevention (Task Lock)
    const lockKey = `lock_id_pdfs_${classLabel}_${user.id || 'system'}`;
    const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    if (!continuationToken && cache.get(lockKey)) {
        return { success: false, message: "A PDF generation task for this class is already running. Please wait for it to finish or stop the current process." };
    }
    // Set lock for 1 minute (renewed on each batch call)
    cache.put(lockKey, 'true', 60);

    let currentIndex = startIndex;
    let count = totalProcessed;
    let failed = 0;


    while (currentIndex < totalStudents && (Date.now() - serverStartTime < TIME_LIMIT)) {
      const batchEnd = Math.min(currentIndex + batchSize, totalStudents);
      const currentBatchNum = Math.floor(currentIndex / batchSize) + 1;
      const currentBatchFolderName = `Batch ${currentBatchNum}`;
      const currentBatchIterator = folder.getFoldersByName(currentBatchFolderName);
      const subFolder = currentBatchIterator.hasNext() ? currentBatchIterator.next() : folder.createFolder(currentBatchFolderName);
      makePublic(subFolder);

      for (let i = currentIndex; i < batchEnd; i++) {
          // [NEW] Abort check inside the loop for better responsiveness
          if (abortKey && CacheService.getScriptCache().get(abortKey) === 'true') {
              Logger.log('Task aborted by user: ' + taskId);
              cache.remove(lockKey); // Release lock on abort
              return { success: true, aborted: true, hasMore: false, count: count, totalCount: totalStudents };
          }
          
          const student = eligibleStudents[i];
          
          if (taskId) {
              const pct = Math.round((i / totalStudents) * 100);
              setTaskProgress(taskId, pct, `ID Card PDFs: Processing ${student.appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || '...'} (${i + 1}/${totalStudents})...`);
          }
          
          try {
              // Pass the root folder as well for global deduplication check
              generateSingleIdPdf(student.appData, student.templateId, subFolder, folder);
              count++;
          } catch (err) {
              failed++;
              console.error('Failed for student: ' + (student.appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || 'Unknown'));
          }
      }
      currentIndex = batchEnd;
    }
    const endIndex = currentIndex;

    const hasMore = endIndex < totalStudents;
    const nextToken = hasMore ? String(endIndex) : null;
    
    // Release or renew lock
    if (!hasMore) {
        cache.remove(lockKey);
    } else {
        cache.put(lockKey, 'true', 60); // Renew lock for next call
    }

    if (!hasMore && taskId) setTaskProgress(taskId, 100, `Successfully generated ${count} ID cards.`);
    
    return { 
      success: true, 
      count: count, 
      failed: failed, 
      totalCount: totalStudents,
      folderId: folder.getId(),
      folderUrl: folder.getUrl(),
      hasMore: hasMore,
      continuationToken: nextToken,
      timestamp: currentTimestamp
    };
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'generateIdPdfsInRange');
    return { success: false, message: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Helper for generateIdPdfsInRange
 */
function generateSingleIdPdf(appData, templateId, targetFolder, rootFolder) {
  const formNum = appData['Form Number'];
  const rollNo = appData['Class Roll No'];
  
  // Custom Formatting for ID Card placeholders (Limit to 3 words)
  const nameRaw = String(appData["Student's Name (as per school records)"] || 'Student').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ');
  const fatherRaw = String(appData["Father's/Guardian's Name (as per school records)"] || '').trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ');

  const formattedName = nameRaw.toUpperCase();
  const formattedFather = toProperCase(fatherRaw);

  const fileName = `${rollNo}_${formattedName}_ID`;
  
  // [NEW] Global deduplication check: Check both target subfolder AND parent root folder
  // This prevents duplicates if a student was processed in a previous batch call.
  let existing = targetFolder.getFilesByName(fileName + '.pdf');
  if (existing.hasNext()) {
    return { skipped: true, url: existing.next().getUrl() };
  }
  
  // Also check root folder (recursively if needed, but here we just check the structure we know)
  if (rootFolder) {
      // Use searchFiles for a more reliable global check across subfolders
      const globalSearch = rootFolder.searchFiles(`title = '${fileName}.pdf' and trashed = false`);
      if (globalSearch.hasNext()) {
          return { skipped: true, url: globalSearch.next().getUrl() };
      }
  }
  
  const tempDocFile = DriveApp.getFileById(templateId).makeCopy(fileName, targetFolder);
  const tempDoc = DocumentApp.openById(tempDocFile.getId());
  const body = tempDoc.getBody();

  // Field mapping
  const subjectsStr = appData['Subjects to be taken in Class 11th'] || 
                     appData['Subjects to be taken in Class 12th'] || 
                     appData['Subjects to be taken in Class 9th'] || 
                     appData['Subjects to be taken in Class 10th'] || 
                     appData['Subjects Studied in Class 11th'] || '';

  // Standardized Residence
  const formattedResidence = formatResidence(appData['Name of your village'], appData['District']);

  let stream = appData['Stream opted in Class 11th'] || appData['Stream for Class 11th'] || '';
  const appCls = String(appData['Admission sought for class'] || '').toLowerCase();

  // Override / Set stream based on class and subjects
  if (appCls.indexOf('9th') !== -1 || appCls.indexOf('10th') !== -1 || appCls.indexOf('8th') !== -1) {
    stream = 'General';
  } else if (appCls.indexOf('11th') !== -1 || appCls.indexOf('12th') !== -1) {
    const s = subjectsStr.toLowerCase();
    const hasPhysics = /physics|\bph\b/i.test(s);
    const hasChemistry = /chemistry|\bch\b/i.test(s);
    if (hasPhysics && hasChemistry) {
      stream = 'Science';
    } else {
      stream = 'Humanities';
    }
  } else if (!stream && subjectsStr) {
    const s = subjectsStr.toLowerCase();
    const isScience = /physics|\bph\b/i.test(s);
    const isGeneral = /social studies|\bss\b/i.test(s);
    if (isGeneral) {
      stream = 'General';
    } else if (isScience) {
      stream = 'Science';
    } else {
      stream = 'Humanities';
    }
  }

  const replacements = {
    '{{StudentName}}': formattedName,
    '{{ClassSought}}': appData['Admission sought for class'] || '',
    '{{Stream11}}': stream,
    '{{StreamOpted11}}': stream,
    '{{FatherName}}': formattedFather,
    '{{Residence}}': formattedResidence,
    '{{Village}}': formattedResidence,
    '{{MiddleName}}': appData['Middle Name'] || '',
    '{{LastName}}': appData['Last Name'] || '',
    '{{MobileNo}}': appData['Mobile No. (with working WhatsApp)'] || '',
    '{{ParentMobile}}': appData["Parent's Mobile No. (must be working)"] || '',
    '{{DOB}}': appData['Date of Birth'] || '',
    '{{Gender}}': appData['Gender'] || '',
    '{{ClassRollNo}}': rollNo,
    '{{Subjects}}': subjectsStr
  };

  // [NEW] Format subjects comma separated using abbreviateSubject (available in code_backend_funcs.gs)
  if (typeof abbreviateSubject === 'function') {
      replacements['{{Subjects}}'] = abbreviateSubject(subjectsStr);
  } else {
      replacements['{{Subjects}}'] = subjectsStr.split(/[,\n;]/).map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(', ');
  }

  // --- 1. Apply Specialized Formatting Replacements First (ID Card specific) ---
  for (const placeholder in replacements) {
    var val = replacements[placeholder] !== undefined && replacements[placeholder] !== null ? String(replacements[placeholder]) : '';
    body.replaceText(placeholder, val);
  }

  // --- 2. Dynamic mapping from Form_Structure (Regular form fields) ---
  // Only replace tags NOT already in our specialized list
  const specTags = Object.keys(replacements);
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
  const structSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.FORM_STRUCTURE);
  if (structSheet && structSheet.getLastRow() > 1) {
    const structData = structSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    structData.shift(); // Remove headers
    for (const row of structData) {
      const fieldName = row[0];
      const placeholder = row[5] || '';
      
      if (placeholder && placeholder.trim()) {
        if (specTags.indexOf(placeholder) !== -1) continue; // Skip tags already handled
        if (placeholder === '{{PHOTO}}' || placeholder === '{{PHOTO_IC}}') continue;
        
        let value = appData[fieldName] !== undefined && appData[fieldName] !== null ? String(appData[fieldName]) : '';
        // Simple DOB check (Basic, standard form has more robust logic but this is fine for ID)
        if (fieldName.toLowerCase().includes('dob') || fieldName.toLowerCase().includes('date of birth')) {
           try {
             const d = new Date(value);
             if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) value = Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), 'dd-MM-yyyy');
           } catch(e){}
        }
        body.replaceText(placeholder, value);
      }
    }

    // --- 3. Insert QR image when template contains {{qr_id}} ---
    try {
      const qrMatch = body.findText('{{qr_id}}');
      if (qrMatch) {
        // Build compact QR payload from key fields
        const qrPayloadObj = {
          formNumber: formNum || '',
          rollNo: rollNo || '',
          name: formattedName || '',
          father: formattedFather || '',
          class: replacements['{{ClassSought}}'] || '',
          mobile: replacements['{{MobileNo}}'] || '',
          residence: formattedResidence || '',
          subjects: replacements['{{Subjects}}'] || ''
        };
        try {
          // Create or reuse QR file in Drive folder
          var qrFile = ensureQrFile(qrPayloadObj, SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.qrFolderId);
          if (qrFile) {
            var blob = qrFile.getBlob();
            var el = qrMatch.getElement();
            var start = qrMatch.getStartOffset();
            var end = qrMatch.getEndOffsetInclusive();

            // Find paragraph ancestor to insert image
            var ancestor = el;
            while (ancestor && ancestor.getType && ancestor.getType() !== DocumentApp.ElementType.PARAGRAPH) {
              ancestor = ancestor.getParent();
            }

            try {
              el.asText().deleteText(start, end);
              if (ancestor && ancestor.getType && ancestor.getType() === DocumentApp.ElementType.PARAGRAPH) {
                var paragraph = ancestor;
                var idx = paragraph.getChildIndex(el);
                var img = paragraph.insertInlineImage(idx + 1, blob);
                try { img.setWidth(180).setHeight(180); } catch (e) {}
              } else {
                var p = body.appendParagraph('');
                var img = p.insertInlineImage(0, blob);
                try { img.setWidth(180).setHeight(180); } catch (e) {}
              }
            } catch (insErr) {
              try { el.asText().deleteText(start, end); } catch (d) {}
              Logger.log('QR insert failed: ' + (insErr && insErr.message));
              try { logQrEvent(formNum, 'insert_error', insErr.message); } catch (ee) {}
              try { qrMatch.getElement().asText().replaceText('{{qr_id}}', '[QR unavailable — check QR_Log sheet]'); } catch (ee) {}
            }
          } else {
            try { qrMatch.getElement().asText().replaceText('{{qr_id}}', '[QR unavailable — check QR_Log sheet]'); } catch (ee) {}
            try { logQrEvent(formNum, 'missing', 'ensureQrFile returned no file or folder inaccessible'); } catch (ee) {}
          }
        } catch (e) {
          try { qrMatch.getElement().asText().replaceText('{{qr_id}}', '[QR unavailable — check QR_Log sheet]'); } catch (ee) {}
          Logger.log('QR placeholder handling failed: ' + (e && e.message));
          try { logQrEvent(formNum, 'error', 'QR placeholder handling failed: ' + (e && e.message)); } catch (ee) {}
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      Logger.log('QR placeholder handling failed: ' + (e && e.message));
    }
  }

  // Handle Photo - Locate by name: RollNo_FormNo (Supporting files like Roll_Form_Name.jpg)
  try {
    const photoFileNamePrefix = `${rollNo}_${formNum}`;
    const fallbackPrefix = String(formNum); // [FALLBACK]
    
    const findInResults = (files, prefix) => {
        while (files.hasNext()) {
            let file = files.next();
            let name = file.getName();
            if (name.indexOf(prefix) === 0 && (name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.jpg') || name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.jpeg') || name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.png'))) {
                return file;
            }
        }
        return null;
    };

    let photoFile = findInResults(DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId).searchFiles(`title contains "${photoFileNamePrefix}" and trashed = false`), photoFileNamePrefix);
    
    // [FALLBACK] Try searching only by Form Number if combined name fails
    if (!photoFile) {
        photoFile = findInResults(DriveApp.getFolderById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sourcePhotoFolderId).searchFiles(`title contains "${fallbackPrefix}" and trashed = false`), fallbackPrefix);
    }

    let found = body.findText('{{PHOTO_IC}}');
    if (found) {
        if (photoFile) {
            const photoBlob = photoFile.getBlob();
            const el = found.getElement();
            const parent = el.getParent();
            const img = parent.asParagraph().insertInlineImage(parent.getChildIndex(el), photoBlob);
            img.setWidth(162).setHeight(190); // 2.25 in x 2.8 in (162 pts x 201.6 pts)
            el.asText().setText('');
        } else {
            // If photo missing, clear the placeholder so it's not ugly
            found.getElement().asText().setText('');
            console.warn('Photo not found for student: ' + photoFileNamePrefix);
        }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Error adding photo: ' + e.message);
  }

  try {
    tempDoc.saveAndClose();
    Utilities.sleep(1200); // Give Drive time to sync replacements
    const pdfBlob = tempDocFile.getBlob().getAs(MimeType.PDF);
    const pdfFile = targetFolder.createFile(pdfBlob);
    makePublic(pdfFile);
    tempDocFile.setTrashed(true);
  } catch (pdfErr) {
    Logger.log('PDF Conversion failed for ' + fileName + ': ' + pdfErr.message);
    // We leave the Doc in the folder so the user can see what failed
    throw pdfErr;
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Helper to make a Drive Item (File/Folder) publicly viewable via link
 * Used to ensure reports/backups are accessible by admins without permission requests
 */
function makePublic(item) {
  if (!item) return;
  try {
    item.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('Could not set sharing for ' + item.getName() + ': ' + e.message);
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] SSO Token Engine - Secure tokens for cross-portal navigation
 */
function generateSSOToken(userEmail) {
  if (!userEmail) return standardResponse(false, null, 'Email required');
  const token = Utilities.getUuid();
  const timestamp = new Date().getTime();
  const expiry = timestamp + (SSO_TOKEN_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60 * 1000);
  
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    let ssoSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SSO_TOKENS);
    
    // Create sheet if it doesn't exist
    if (!ssoSheet) {
      ssoSheet = ss.insertSheet(SHEETS.SSO_TOKENS);
      ssoSheet.appendRow(['Token', 'Email', 'Role', 'Expiry', 'Timestamp']);
      ssoSheet.hideSheet();
    }
    
    // Clean up expired tokens first to keep sheet small
    const data = ssoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const rowsToDelete = [];
    for (let i = data.length - 1; i >= 1; i--) {
      if (timestamp > data[i][3]) {
        ssoSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
      }
    }
    
    // Find user role from Users sheet
    const userSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.USERS);
    const userData = userSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    let role = 'Student';
    for (let j = 1; j < userData.length; j++) {
      if (String(userData[j][0]).toLowerCase() === String(userEmail).toLowerCase()) {
        role = userData[j][4];
        break;
      }
    }
    
    // Append new token
    ssoSheet.appendRow([token, userEmail, role, expiry, new Date()]);
    return standardResponse(true, { token: token, portals: SCHOOL_PORTALS });
    
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('SSO Generation Error:', e);
    return standardResponse(false, null, 'Failed to generate token: ' + e.message);
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Validate SSO token from another portal (Cross-project compatible)
 */
function validateSSOToken(token) {
  if (!token) return standardResponse(false, null, 'Token required');
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    const ssoSheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.SSO_TOKENS);
    if (!ssoSheet) return standardResponse(false, null, 'Gateway database not initialized');
    
    const data = ssoSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const now = new Date().getTime();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
        if (data[i][0] === token) {
            if (now > data[i][3]) {
                ssoSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
                return standardResponse(false, null, 'Token has expired');
            }
            // Token is valid! Return user data
            const email = data[i][1];
            const role = data[i][2];
            
            let userObj = getUserByEmail(email, ss);
            if (!userObj) {
              userObj = { email: email, role: role };
            } else {
              userObj.role = role; // Ensure role from token is preserved
            }
            
            let initialData = null;
            try {
              initialData = getInitialDataForUser(userObj);
            } catch (e) {}
            
            // Optional: Delete token after one-time use for maximum security
            ssoSheet.deleteRow(i + 1);
            return standardResponse(true, { user: userObj, initialData: initialData });
        }
    }
    return standardResponse(false, null, 'Invalid session token');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('SSO Validation Error:', e);
    return standardResponse(false, null, 'Validation error');
  }
}



/**
 * [NEW] Fetches data for the Admission Register report.
 * Filters: All 11th grade, and 12th grade with "readmission" or "yes" in readmission column.
 */
function getAdmissionRegisterData(session, user) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SCRIPT_PROPERTIES.sheetId);
    ensureUserRole(user, ss);
    if (!isUserAnyAdmin(user)) throw new Error("Unauthorized access");

    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName(SHEETS.ADMISSIONS);
    const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    const headers = data[0];

    const getIdx = (name) => {
        let idx = headers.indexOf(name);
        if (idx === -1) {
            // Case-insensitive fallback
            idx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).toLowerCase().trim() === String(name).toLowerCase().trim());
        }
        return idx;
    };

    const idxSession = getIdx('Session');
    const idxClass = getIdx('Class');
    const idxStatus = getIdx('Status'); // Still checking this if present, fallback to readmission
    const idxReadmission = getIdx('readmission');
    
    // Core details
    const idxForm = getIdx('Form No.');
    const idxRoll = getIdx('Class R.No.');
    const idxTimestamp = getIdx('Online Subm. Date');
    const idxAdmDate = getIdx('Adm. Date');
    const idxAdmNo = getIdx('Adm. No.');
    const idxBoardReg = getIdx('Board Reg. No.');
    const idxName = getIdx("Student's Name");
    const idxFather = getIdx("Father's Name");
    const idxMother = getIdx("Mother's Name");
    const idxDob = getIdx("DoB (figures)");
    const idxGender = getIdx('Gender');
    const idxVillage = getIdx('Village/Town');
    const idxTehsil = getIdx('Tehsil');
    const idxDistrict = getIdx('District');
    const idxBlock = getIdx('Block');
    const idxMobile = getIdx("Student's Contact");
    const idxParentMobile = getIdx("Parent's Contact");
    const idxPhoto = getIdx('photo_id');

    // Page 2 details
    const idxStream = getIdx('Stream');
    const idxSubs = getIdx('Subs');
    const idxAadhar = getIdx('Aadhar No.');
    const idxCategory = getIdx('Social category');
    const idxSocioEcon = getIdx('Socio-economic category');
    const idxBlood = getIdx('Blood Type');
    const idxAccount = getIdx('Bank Account Number');
    const idxIfsc = getIdx('IFSC Code');
    const idxPrevSchool = getIdx('Previous School');
    const idxPrevRoll = getIdx('Exam R.No. (Prev.)');
    const idxMarksObt = getIdx('Marks Obt. (Prev.)');
    const idxMaxMarks = getIdx('Max. Marks (Prev.)');
    const idxPen = getIdx('PEN No.');
    const idxRemarks = getIdx('Remarks');

    const students = [];
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const row = data[i];
      if (row[idxStatus] !== 'Submitted' && row[idxStatus] !== 'Draft') continue; // [MODIFIED] Include drafts? User usually wants submitted
      if (session && row[idxSession] !== session) continue;

      const cls = String(row[idxClass]);
      const is11th = cls.includes('11th');
      const is12th = cls.includes('12th');

      let include = false;
      if (is11th) {
        include = true;
      } else if (is12th && idxReadmission !== -1) {
        const val = String(row[idxReadmission]).toLowerCase();
        if (val.includes('readmission') || val.includes('yes')) {
          include = true;
        }
      }

      if (include) {
        // Format dates
        const rawDob = row[idxDob];
        let dobFigures = '';
        let dobWords = '';
        if (rawDob) {
            const d = new Date(rawDob);
            if (!isNaN(d.getTime())) {
                dobFigures = Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "dd-MM-yyyy");
                dobWords = formatDateToWords(d);
            }
        }

        const rawTimestamp = row[idxTimestamp];
        let onlineStatus = '';
        if (rawTimestamp) {
            const dt = new Date(rawTimestamp);
            if (!isNaN(dt.getTime())) onlineStatus = Utilities.formatDate(dt, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "dd-MM-yyyy");
        }

        // PEN Logic: Use 'PEN No.' only, return 'NA' if 'Not Found' or empty
        let penVal = idxPen !== -1 ? String(row[idxPen] || '').trim() : '';
        if (!penVal || penVal.toLowerCase() === 'not found') penVal = 'NA';

        let admDateStr = '';
        if (idxAdmDate !== -1 && row[idxAdmDate]) {
            const dAdm = new Date(row[idxAdmDate]);
            if (!isNaN(dAdm.getTime())) admDateStr = Utilities.formatDate(dAdm, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "dd-MM-yyyy");
        }

        const student = {
            sno: students.length + 1,
            photo: idxPhoto !== -1 ? (row[idxPhoto] || '') : '',
            rollNo: idxRoll !== -1 ? (row[idxRoll] || '') : '',
            formNo: idxForm !== -1 ? (row[idxForm] || '') : '',
            onlineStatus: onlineStatus,
            admDate: admDateStr || onlineStatus, // Use Adm. Date if available, else Subm. Date
            admNo: idxAdmNo !== -1 ? row[idxAdmNo] : '',
            class: cls,
            boardReg: idxBoardReg !== -1 ? (row[idxBoardReg] || '') : '',
            name: idxName !== -1 ? (row[idxName] || '') : '',
            father: idxFather !== -1 ? (row[idxFather] || '') : '',
            mother: idxMother !== -1 ? (row[idxMother] || '') : '',
            dobFigures: dobFigures,
            dobWords: dobWords,
            gender: idxGender !== -1 ? (row[idxGender] || '') : '',
            village: idxVillage !== -1 ? (row[idxVillage] || '') : '',
            block: idxBlock !== -1 ? row[idxBlock] : '',
            tehsil: idxTehsil !== -1 ? (row[idxTehsil] || '') : '',
            district: idxDistrict !== -1 ? (row[idxDistrict] || '') : '',
            mobile: idxMobile !== -1 ? (row[idxMobile] || '') : '',
            parentMobile: idxParentMobile !== -1 ? (row[idxParentMobile] || '') : '',
            stream: idxStream !== -1 ? (row[idxStream] || '') : '',
            subs: idxSubs !== -1 ? (row[idxSubs] || '') : '',
            aadhar: idxAadhar !== -1 ? (row[idxAadhar] || '') : '',
            category: idxCategory !== -1 ? (row[idxCategory] || '') : '',
            socioEcon: idxSocioEcon !== -1 ? row[idxSocioEcon] : '',
            blood: idxBlood !== -1 ? (row[idxBlood] || '') : '',
            account: idxAccount !== -1 ? (row[idxAccount] || '') : '',
            ifsc: idxIfsc !== -1 ? (row[idxIfsc] || '') : '',
            prevSchool: idxPrevSchool !== -1 ? (row[idxPrevSchool] || '') : '',
            prevRoll: idxPrevRoll !== -1 ? (row[idxPrevRoll] || '') : '',
            prevResult: (idxMarksObt !== -1 && idxMaxMarks !== -1) ? `${row[idxMarksObt] || ''}/${row[idxMaxMarks] || ''}` : '',
            pen: penVal,
            remarks: idxRemarks !== -1 ? (row[idxRemarks] || '') : ''
        };
        students.push(student);
      }
    }

    return standardResponse(true, students);
  } catch (e) {
    logError(e, 'getAdmissionRegisterData');
    return standardResponse(false, null, e.message);
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Converts a Date object to words as per the requested format.
 * Example: 11-04-2008 -> eleventh of April two thousand eight
 */
function formatDateToWords(date) {
  try {
    const days = ['zero', 'first', 'second', 'third', 'fourth', 'fifth', 'sixth', 'seventh', 'eighth', 'ninth', 'tenth', 'eleventh', 'twelfth', 'thirteenth', 'fourteenth', 'fifteenth', 'sixteenth', 'seventeenth', 'eighteenth', 'nineteenth', 'twentieth', 'twenty-first', 'twenty-second', 'twenty-third', 'twenty-fourth', 'twenty-fifth', 'twenty-sixth', 'twenty-seventh', 'twenty-eighth', 'twenty-ninth', 'thirtieth', 'thirty-first'];
    const months = ['January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June', 'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'];
    
    const d = date.getDate();
    const m = date.getMonth();
    const y = date.getFullYear();

    const dayWord = days[d];
    const monthWord = months[m];
    
    // Year conversion (simplified for 2000-2030)
    let yearWord = '';
    if (y >= 2000 && y < 2010) {
        const ones = ['zero', 'one', 'two', 'three', 'four', 'five', 'six', 'seven', 'eight', 'nine'];
        yearWord = 'two thousand ' + ones[y - 2000];
    } else if (y >= 2010 && y < 2020) {
        const teens = ['ten', 'eleven', 'twelve', 'thirteen', 'fourteen', 'fifteen', 'sixteen', 'seventeen', 'eighteen', 'nineteen'];
        yearWord = 'two thousand ' + teens[y - 2010];
    } else if (y >= 2020 && y <= 2030) {
        const twenties = ['twenty', 'twenty-one', 'twenty-two', 'twenty-three', 'twenty-four', 'twenty-five', 'twenty-six', 'twenty-seven', 'twenty-eight', 'twenty-nine', 'thirty'];
        yearWord = 'two thousand ' + twenties[y - 2020];
    } else {
        yearWord = String(y); // Fallback
    }

    return `${dayWord} of ${monthWord} ${yearWord}`;
  } catch (e) {
    return '';
  }
}

/**
 * [NEW] Generates the Admission Register HTML for a new tab.
 */
function getAdmissionRegisterHtml(session, user) {
  try {
    const response = getAdmissionRegisterData(session, user);
    if (!response.success) throw new Error(response.message);

    const students = response.data;
    const template = HtmlService.createTemplateFromFile('AdmissionRegister');
    const html = template.evaluate().getContent();
    
    // Inject data via script tag
    const script = `<script>init(${JSON.stringify(students)}, ${JSON.stringify(session)})</script>`;
    return html + script;
  } catch (e) {
    return `<h1>Error</h1><p>${e.message}</p>`;
  }
}
